The eager voice and the old, familiar names, the home things: she was afraid of all those people, those words. Life was lonely here. Perhaps if she were to keep him, she would have to give up and move back among his own kind. She observed to herself that this man, who was to bind her to The People, seemed to be driving her yet farther apart from them.
===Chinese===
那人长发披散，光着头不戴帽子，一身地道的纳瓦霍装束，饰物沉甸甸的，一看便知是北方地界来的。笑娃（Laughing Boy）管他叫表哥，拉着他说长问短，问特奥·特莱凯（T’o Tlakai）的人和事，问整个吉恩德（Gyende）地区的情形。那热切的话音，那些古老而耳熟的名字，那些家乡的物事：这一切都叫她害怕——那些人，那些字眼。这里的日子冷清。她若要留住这个男人，到头来兴许只得认输，搬回他本族人中间去住。她暗自看出：这个本该把她同迪内（The People）拴在一处的男人，眼下倒像是把她同他们越推越远了。

===Original===
When they were alone for a minute, he said, “Why did you not give me my drink? Why did you not offer him one?”
===Chinese===
趁两人单独待上一小会儿的当口，他说：“我的那杯怎么不给我？怎么不也给他一杯？”

===Original===
“That drink is medicine that I know. You must leave it to me. There are things that must not be done about it, just like prayer-sticks and sacred cigarettes.” As she spoke, she prepared a stiff dose. “That man must not have it or know about it. You must not speak of it unless I say you may.”
===Chinese===
“那杯东西是我懂的药。你只许交给我来经管。它有它的忌讳，动不得，跟祈祷枝和敬神的香烟一个道理。”她一面说，一面兑起一份浓浓的。“那个人不许沾它，也不许知道它。除非我许你说，你一个字也不许提。”

===Original===
“Good, then, I hear you.” He drained it off. He had missed it.
===Chinese===
“好，我听你的。”他一饮而尽。他可惦记这一口了。

===Original===
“I was afraid you would speak of it before him.”
===Chinese===
“我还怕你当着他的面提起它呢。”

===Original===
“I thought about it. You had some reason, I thought. So I waited.”
===Chinese===
“我思量过了。我想你自有道理。所以我就等着。”

===Original===
She nodded.
===Chinese===
她点了点头。

===Original===
“He is my uncle’s son; not Wounded Face at Tsé Lani, another one from T’o Tlakai. His sister is sick. They are going to hold a full Night Chant, ten nights. They want us to come, he says. Mountain Singer wants me to dance in it; it is a song that I know well. I have been in it before when he led.”
===Chinese===
“他是我舅舅家的儿子；不是特塞拉尼（Tsé Lani）的疤脸（Wounded Face）舅舅，是特奥·特莱凯那边的另一家。他妹妹病了。他们要办一场全本的夜颂仪（Night Chant），一连十夜。他说，想请咱们去。山地颂唱师（Mountain Singer）要我在里头跳舞；那支歌我熟得很。从前他领唱的时候，我就在里头跳过。”

===Original===
His voice told her, “This time I want to go. Now you must do something for me.” She saw that it would be a mistake to oppose him.
===Chinese===
他的话音告诉了她：“这一回我想去。如今该你为我出点力了。”她看得出，这当口跟他拗着来是要失着的。

===Original===
“Let us go, then; I think it will be a good thing. I shall be glad to see your country and your people, and a big dance like that is always good to go to.” There was under-pleading in her voice, but he knew that this was a gift to him. “When is the dance to be?”
===Chinese===
“那就去吧；我想这是件好事。能去看看你的家乡、你的人们，我会高兴的，况且那样一场大舞，去见识见识总是好的。”她话音底下藏着一层央告，可他知道，这是她送给他的一份礼。“舞定在哪时候？”

===Original===
“At the full of Little Snow Moon.”
===Chinese===
“小雪月月圆的时候。”

===Original===
It was obvious that he looked forward eagerly to the visit. This was to be her test that was coming, one more test, and she felt there were enough already. She excelled herself in tenderness and charm, and strengthened his drinks. His response to her was evidence of a steadily burning fire that would momentarily lull her doubts. In every act and word and look he seemed to testify his steadfastness, but still she was uneasy.
===Chinese===
他一心巴望这趟做客，这是明摆着的事。而这临到她头上的又是一场考验——再一场考验，她已经觉得够多的了。她拿出加倍的温柔和妩媚，把他那杯东西也兑得愈发浓了。他待她的种种回应，便是一炉火稳稳烧着的凭据，一时能把她的疑心哄得睡去。他的一举一动、一言一瞥，都像在作证他的坚定不移，可她心里终究安不下来。

===Original===
On the night before the start for T’o Tlakai, they sat late by the fire. He spoke eagerly of his own country, while she answered little. The colourful cliffs and canyons, the warm rock, the blue masses of distant mountains﻿—
===Chinese===
动身去特奥·特莱凯的前夜，两人挨着火坐到很晚。他兴致勃勃地讲自己的家乡，她应和得少。那些色彩缤纷的峭壁和峡谷，那暖烘烘的岩石，那远处一团一团的蓝色山影——

===Original===
“When it gets all hot there in the valley, when it is sunlight in the little crevices, and everything you look at seems to jump out at you, you look over towards the east. Just above the rim of the cliffs you see Chiz-na Hozolchi Mountain. It is far away, it is blue and soft. Even when the sky is blue as turquoise and hard as a knife-blade, it is soft, and more blue. You will like that country.”
===Chinese===
“谷地里热透了的时候，小石缝里都盛满阳光的时候，你看什么都像要迎面朝你蹦出来，这时你就朝东边望。就在峭壁的崖口上头一点，你望得见 Chiz-na Hozolchi 山。它远远的，蓝蓝的，软软的。哪怕天蓝得像绿松石、硬得像刀刃，它还是软的，还更蓝些。你会喜欢那地方的。”

===Original===
Is he trying to persuade me to stay there? Perhaps we shall have to, in the end. I shall need all my strength.
===Chinese===
他这是要劝我留在那儿么？兴许到头来我们不得不留。我可得拿出全身的力气来。

===Original===
“It will be fine when we ride in together. We shall have two good ponies. They will envy our jewelry. They will envy my saddle-blanket that you made me.”
===Chinese===
“咱们俩并辔骑进去，那才叫风光。咱们会有两匹好小马。他们要眼红咱们的首饰，还要眼红你给我织的那条鞍毯。”

===Original===
And they will know about me, and his own people will talk to him.
===Chinese===
可他们会知道我的底细，他自家人会跟他嚼舌根的。

===Original===
“They are good people. You will like them.”
===Chinese===
“他们是好人。你会喜欢他们的。”

===Original===
They are my enemies, more than if they were Utes.
===Chinese===
他们是敌人，比一帮尤特人（Utes）还要更是敌人。

===Original===
When he fell silent, he would touch her arm with his fingertips. Then he would speak again, staring into the fire as a man will when he is seeing something, but always turning to look at her, almost shyly.
===Chinese===
他住了口的时候，会用指尖轻轻碰碰她的胳膊。过后又说起来，眼睛望着火，像人望见了什么景象时那样，却总要回过头来看她一眼，几乎带着羞怯。

===Original===
She relaxed, relieved of her fear. I am a fool. I am a crazy damned fool. I am the centre of all that he is thinking. He is all tied up in me. He cares for this and that, but I am the door through which it all comes. Listen to the way he is talking, see how he looks. We can go to a thousand dances and he will still be mine. Not all The People in the world can take him away. If he is ever lost to me, it will be I who have lost him.
===Chinese===
她松快下来，恐惧散了。我真是个傻瓜。我是个疯透了的天杀的傻瓜。他心里转的一切，中心都是我。他整个人都拴在我身上。他也牵挂这个、在意那个，可我是那扇门，这一切都打我这扇门里过。听听他说话的这个样子，看看他这副神气。咱们就是去上一千场舞，他也还是我的。普天之下所有的迪内加起来，也夺不走他。他若真有从我这里丢的一天，那也是我自己丢的。

===Original===
She moved over and leant against him, her head on his shoulder. “I think your country will be very beautiful. I shall be glad to see it. Your people will not like me, I think, but I do not care, if we are together.”
===Chinese===
她挪过去偎着他，头靠在他肩上。“我想你的家乡一定很美。我会高高兴兴去看它。你的人不会喜欢我，我想，可我不在乎，只要咱们俩在一起。”

### II / II

===Original===
Slim Girl’s idea of travel on horseback was that one should ride during the cooler part of the morning, rest out the noon downpour of light and heat in a shady place, and use the last of the day to find the nearest friendly hogan. There could be none of that now, she knew. Her man was a Navajo and a horseman; when he settled in the saddle, as the sides of his calves touched his pony’s barrel, and he felt the one current run through them, there was always that little look of uplift. Probably half of the waking hours of his life had been spent on a horse’s back, but not the longest day could destroy in him a certain pleasure in even the workaday jog or mechanical, mile-eating lope of a good pony.
===Chinese===
在瘦姑娘看来，骑马赶路的道理本该是：趁清早凉快的时辰骑马，晌午那阵泼下来的光和热，寻个荫凉处歇将过去，再拿一天剩下的时辰，去找最近一处相熟的霍根屋（hogan，纳瓦霍人泥木筑的小屋）。如今这一套是行不通了，她知道。她的男人是纳瓦霍人，天生骑手；他一坐进鞍子，小腿肚一贴上小马滚圆的肚腹，觉出人马之间那股一气贯通的劲道，脸上总要漾起那么一丝昂扬的神采。他这辈子醒着的时辰，怕有一半是在马背上过的；可日子再长，也磨不掉他心里那份兴头——哪怕是做工的小颠步，哪怕是一匹好小马那种机械的、一英里一英里吞着路的大步跑，他都觉得受用。

===Original===
She thought of this, as they skirted Los Palos in the dawn, and sighed, foreseeing heat and fatigue, stiffness and soreness in unromantic places, all to be concealed from this man of hers. He did not even know that it was necessary for one to be toughened to the saddle; he thought people were born that way, if he thought about it at all. She wondered, doubting, if any of the exaltation of their first ride to Los Palos would carry her through this.
===Chinese===
拂晓时分，他们打洛斯帕洛斯（Los Palos）边上绕过去，她想着这些，叹了口气，预见到暑热与劳乏，预见到身上那些不体面的地方又要发僵、又要酸痛，而这一切都得在这个男人跟前遮掩住。他甚至不知道人的筋骨须经鞍子磨炼才成；他多半压根儿没想过这一层，只当人生来就经得住。她疑疑惑惑地寻思，头一回同骑去洛斯帕洛斯时那股飞扬的陶醉，这一回还能不能撑着她走完。

===Original===
It was not so bad as she had feared. At this late time of year it was hardly hot even at midday. Her weaving and occasional hours in the cornfield had hardened her somewhat. The high-cantled Navajo saddle he had made for her, with its seat of slung leather over which a dyed goatskin was thrown, was more comfortable than one would have thought possible. The miles stretched out before them, shrank, and were overpassed. She was tired in the late afternoon, thirsty from dust, silent. She watched this man who rode before her, so easy in his saddle, so at home, going back to his own country.
===Chinese===
事情并没有她怕的那么糟。时节这么晚了，连晌午都谈不上热。织布的活计，加上玉米地里偶尔下的那几个时辰，多少把她的筋骨磨出来一些。他给她做的那具高鞍桥的纳瓦霍鞍，鞍座是绷绷吊起的皮面，上头又铺了一张染过色的山羊皮，比人想得到的还舒服。一英里一英里的路在眼前铺开，缩短，被一一越过。下半晌她累了，嘴里叫尘土呛得发渴，不大言语。她望着前头骑马的这个男人，鞍上那样安适，那样如鱼得水，正回他自己的家乡去。

===Original===
She no longer had her own, different background. She was afraid because of him. It was no longer she who was strong, leading, marking the places for him to set down his feet. Now it was she who must fumble, uncertain, and he who must hold her up. What hobbles would she have on him now? It was all right, that he felt all for her, that she was the centre of things, but how could she be sure when his own people and his own things spoke to him? There was nothing to do but wait and be watchful, and meantime a little mouse was gnawing at her heart.
===Chinese===
她那套自己的、另类的底子，如今是没有了。她因为他而害怕了。再不是她强、她领路、替他记认该把脚往哪里放的时候了。如今是她要摸索、要拿不稳，是他得扶住她了。这会儿，她还能拿什么绊索绊得住他呢？他把心思全放在她身上、她是一切事物的中心，这自然是好的；可等他自家人、自家那些东西开口同他说话时，她又怎么拿得稳？没别的法子，只有等，只有时刻提防，而这当口，一只小老鼠正在啃她的心。

===Original===
They spent the night at a friendly hogan. There, too, he was at home and she astray. She saw his natural sociability expand in the evening gossip, and she learned with surprise that he had an established place among these people, who looked at her faintly askance. He was already known, and his opinions on horses were listened to with respect.
===Chinese===
他们在一户相熟的霍根屋过了夜。在那里也一样，他如归故里，她如失路途。晚间闲谈里，她眼见他天生的合群劲儿舒展开来；她又吃惊地得知，他在这些人中间原占着一个站稳了的位置，而这些人看她的目光，微微带着斜睨。他早有了名声，说起马上的见解，人人都敬着听。

===Original===
She had been drawn to him first just because of these things. She wanted him as a link between herself and just such as these people. But more, terribly more now, she needed him, himself completely hers with no fragment left out, and so they had become her enemies.
===Chinese===
当初，恰恰是这些东西把他牵到她心里的。她要他做一根链环，把她自己同这样的人、这样的人家连在一起。可如今更要紧了——要紧得吓人——她需要的是他这个人，整个人完完全全归她，一星半点也不许流落在外；于是，他们便成了她的敌人。

===Original===
Yet there was plenty with which to comfort herself. Their opinion of her changed visibly when they learned that it was she who had woven Laughing Boy’s saddle-blanket. The red background, with the black and white interlocked fret of the heat lightning, was a gay and handsome thing. The women examined it, felt its weave, and spoke highly of it. There was an evident, kindhearted relief at this proof that she was regular.
===Chinese===
不过，可以宽慰自己的东西也尽有。待大家知道笑娃那条鞍毯原来出自她的手，众人对她的看法便眼见着变了。红底子上黑白相锁的热闪回纹，是件鲜亮而体面的东西。妇人们把毯子端详来端详去，捻它的织工，赞不绝口。这一下证明了她是个规矩人，人人脸上都现出一片好心肠的宽心来。

===Original===
More important was the subtle difference, the special quality of her husband’s attitude towards her when compared with their host and his wife. In that house was the usual peace and understanding of an Indian’s home, but there was none of that faint reverence and intimate desire that she felt when Laughing Boy spoke to her. She knew she should be proud and happy, but sleep was long in coming as they lay in their blankets about the dying fire.
===Chinese===
更要紧的，是那一层微妙的分别：把丈夫待她的态度，同这家的男主人和他婆娘放在一起一对，自有一种特别的成色。那座屋里有着印第安人家惯有的安和与体谅，可笑娃同她说话时她感受到的那一种淡淡的敬惜、那一种贴着心的欲念，这里一丝也没有。她知道自己原该骄傲、该欢喜，可两人裹着毯子躺在将熄的火堆旁，睡意迟迟不肯来。

===Original===
The second day was like the first, save that, instead of growing stiff and sore, she grew stiffer and sorer. Her fears rode with her behind the saddle; she wondered after her old, arrogant sureness.
===Chinese===
第二天同第一天一个样，只是非但没把僵和疼缓过来，反倒愈僵愈疼了。她的种种恐惧同她一道骑在鞍后；她怅怅地追想着自己旧日那股傲然的笃定。

===Original===
They made camp for themselves, having come to a section where no one lived. She was unhappy in mind and body, not overjoyed at their roofless stopping place and the prospect of a cold night, nor pleased with bread and coffee and a little dried meat. After supper they sat in silence, smoking and looking into the coals. She thought that silence was inimical.
===Chinese===
他们走进一段没有人烟的地界，只好自家安营。她身心两下都不痛快：这头顶无遮的宿处，寒夜将临的前景，她喜欢不起来；面包、咖啡和一小点干肉，也叫她提不起兴致。晚饭后两人默默地坐着，抽烟，望着炭火出神。她觉得这沉默跟她作对。

===Original===
At last he said, “I shall set a trap back there by those rocks; we should have a prairie-dog for breakfast. They are good. I know you do not care for this food we just had. You are used to better.”
===Chinese===
末了他说：“我到后头那些石头跟前下个套子；明早咱们就该有草原犬鼠可吃了。那东西好。我知道你不待见方才那些吃食。你吃惯了更好的。”

===Original===
“I do not mind. You must not think about me.”
===Chinese===
“我不在乎。你别总惦记着我。”

===Original===
“I wish you had brought some of that whiskey. Since you have taught me that, everything is flat without it. There is no salt in things. I missed it last night, and I do now.”
===Chinese===
“你要是带了些那种威士忌来就好了。打你教会了我这个，没有它，样样东西都平塌塌的没滋味。东西里没有盐味儿。昨儿夜里我就惦记，这会儿还惦记。”

===Original===
“I brought some. I did not know you wanted it. Here is about enough for two drinks. You will have to take it just plain.”
===Chinese===
“我带了些。我不知道你想要。这儿约莫够两杯的量。你可只能净着喝，什么也掺不得了。”

===Original===
“That is all right. Give me some, then.”
===Chinese===
“那也使得。那就给我一些吧。”

===Original===
He drank his dose eagerly.
===Chinese===
他把那一份急切切地喝了下去。

===Original===
“There will be none of that at T’o Tlakai,” she told him.
===Chinese===
“到了特奥·特莱凯，可就没有那个了。”她告诉他。

===Original===
“That is all right. It does not belong there; it is part of the new world you have made for me. I do not think I could go back to just living, like these other people.”
===Chinese===
“那也没关系。它本来就不属于那儿；它是你给我造出来的新世界的一部分。我想我回不去了——回不到光是活着的日子，像这些旁人那样。”

===Original===
She thought to herself, that is well enough, while we are alone. You will lose the need for the drink in the time we are there, perhaps you will forget about it.
===Chinese===
她心里想，这样也尽够了，趁着咱们俩单独在一处。等住到那边、日子一长，你自会丢掉对那杯东西的需要，兴许你把它整个儿忘了。

===Original===
Nonetheless she felt better, and noticed that the night was beautiful with stars. After all, camping thus was part of her people’s heritage. She was doing a Navajo thing. Her blanket sufficed to keep her warm; she fell asleep as soon as she closed her eyes.
===Chinese===
话虽这么说，她心里好受了些，还留意到这夜晚缀满了星，很美。毕竟，这样露营原是她族人的一份传承。她做的是一桩纳瓦霍人的事。毯子尽够她暖；她一阖眼便睡着了。

===Original===
As they went farther north, at first the desert rather appalled her. She was accustomed to the southeastern part of the Navajo country, grey bluffs, and grey rolling plateaus and harshly monotonous, distant mountains. Since she had known fertile California and the bustle and comfort of the places where civilized man gathers together to domesticate the scenery, she had never been able to feel any deep liking for the empty desert and the hostile fury of its silence. Now they were come among warm, golden cliffs, painted with red and purplish brown and luminous shadows, a broken country that changed with the changing sun, narrow canyons, great mesas, yellow sands, and distant, blue mountains.
===Chinese===
再往北去，起初那片荒漠叫她心里发怵。她看惯的是纳瓦霍地方的东南部——灰色的断崖，灰色起伏的台地，还有远处生硬而单调的山。打她见识过肥沃的加利福尼亚，见识过文明人聚族而居、把风景驯得服服帖帖的那些地方的喧嚣与安逸之后，她就再也没能对这空空的荒漠、对它沉默里那股满怀敌意的狂怒，生出半点深的喜爱。如今他们走进了暖烘烘、金灿灿的峭壁之间——石上涂着红、紫棕和明亮的阴影——一片随着日头变幻的破碎山地，窄窄的峡谷，巍巍的平顶高山，黄沙，还有远处蓝莹莹的山。

===Original===
They rode along a defile, scarcely a hundred yards wide, whose walls, twice as high, looked as though they had just drawn apart, and might decide to close again. Scrub oak, in the bottom, clustered along a running stream. The place was full of shadow. Looking up, one saw magnificent, dark firs growing along the ledges and hanging valleys. Up there, the ruddy rock, touched by the sunlight, became dull orange and buff, with flecks of gold, and a golden line where it met a flawless sky. Their horses’ feet made a tiny, soft noise in the sand. Nested on one ledge was a village of the long-vanished Old People, square little stone houses high up, with black spots of doorways that watched the canyon. Laughing Boy pointed to the ruin.
===Chinese===
他们沿着一道隘谷骑行，谷宽不足一百码，两壁比谷宽还高出一倍，模样像是方才才裂开，兴许一转念还要合上。谷底里，矮栎沿着一条活水溪丛丛簇簇。这地方满是荫翳。抬头望去，只见深色的冷杉长在岩阶上、悬谷里，一株株气象堂堂。高处，赭红的岩石叫太阳一照，变成暗橙和米黄，带着点点金的斑，同无瑕的天空相接处，勾出一线金边。马蹄在沙里踏出细小绵软的声响。一方岩阶上，窝着一座早已消散无踪的古人（Old People）的村落，方方的小石屋高踞在上，黑洞洞的一处处门口守望着峡谷。笑娃指了指那片残墟。

===Original===
“Yota Kien,” he said. “Some of the Divine Ones live there, they say. The two brothers came here when they were looking for Talking God, they say.”
===Chinese===
“尤塔基恩（Yota Kien）。”他说。“听说神圣之灵（Divine Ones）有一些就住在那儿。听说那两兄弟当年寻找说话神（Talking God）的时候，来过这里。”

===Original===
They stopped to rest and water the horses. She looked about her, feeling the quiet, absorbing the place. She had a sense of rest and of growth. She had not known that one could feel intimate about anything so grand.
===Chinese===
他们停下歇脚，饮了马。她四下里望着，领受这份寂静，把这地方一寸寸吸进心里。她有一种安息之感，一种生长之感。她还不知道，人对这么壮阔的东西，竟也能觉得亲近。

===Original===
He brought her to a high place late one afternoon, a spur of Dzhil Clizhini. It had been a fatiguing, scrambling climb, with one piece to be done on foot, alleviated by the increasing growth of jack pine and spruce. At length they trotted along a level, following a winding path under firs. There was a short stretch of broken ground, grey, knobbed rock, oaks whose branches one had to duck, a tumbling little gorge at the left, with the smell of water. They were shut in by trees.
===Chinese===
一天下半晌，他把她带上一处高地，是吉尔·克利日尼（Dzhil Clizhini）的一道支脉。那一程攀爬叫人疲乏，有一截还得下马步行，好在短叶松和云杉渐渐多起来，教人松快些。末了他们沿一条平地跑马，顺着冷杉底下一弯一弯的小径。有一小截烂地——灰色的、疙瘩累累的岩石，得低头躲过的栎树枝，左手边一道翻腾的小峡谷，带着水的气味。他们叫树围了个严严实实。

===Original===
He drew rein, motioning to her to come up beside him. She did so, crowding past the twigs that hemmed in the path. Right before their horses’ feet the cliff fell away, some fourteen hundred feet, and there, under their hands, lay all the North Country. It was red in the late sunlight, fierce, narrow canyons with ribbons of shadow, broad valleys and lesser hills streaked with purple opaque shadows like deep holes in the world, cast by the upthrust mesas. The great, black volcanic core of Agathla was a sombre monstrosity in the midst of colour. Away and away it stretched, jumbled, vast, the crazy shapes of the Monuments, the clay hills of Utah, and far beyond everything, floating blue mountain shapes softer than the skies. She drew back in the saddle.
===Chinese===
他勒住缰，招手叫她赶到自己身旁。她照办了，侧身挤过篱在小径两边的枝条。就在两匹马的蹄子前头，崖壁直直塌落下去，约莫一千四百英尺，而整个北国，就卧在他们手底下。斜阳里，它一片红：狰狞的窄谷缠着一条条阴影的带子；宽谷与小山上划着紫色不透光的阴影，像世界身上的深窟窿，是那些拔地而起的平顶高山投下的。阿加斯拉（Agathla）巨大的黑色火山岩芯，黑沉沉戳在满目色彩中央，是个阴郁的怪物。远处更远处，它一路铺展开去，乱纷纷，浩荡荡——纪念碑群峰（the Monuments）癫狂的形状，犹他的黏土丘陵，而在这一切之外更远的地方，浮着比天空还要柔和的蓝色山影。她在鞍上把身子往后一缩。

===Original===
“When anyone comes here, even if he has been here many times, it hits him in the face. Wait and look, by and by you grow until you can take all this inside of you. Then nothing can make you angry or disturb you.”
===Chinese===
“不管谁到了这里，哪怕来过多少回，它都会劈面给他一下。等着，看着，慢慢地你就长大了，大得能把这一切都收进自己身子里。到那时，什么也不能叫你动怒，什么也搅扰不着你。”

===Original===
They sat in silence, looking, absorbing. He dismounted, added one to a cairn of stones, and squatted, gazing out. There was something about it that made Slim Girl choke. It made her want to cry.
===Chinese===
两人默默坐着，看着，领受着。他下了马，往一座石堆上添了一块石头，蹲下来望着远处。那景象里有一种东西，堵得瘦姑娘喉咙发哽。它叫她想哭。

===Original===
The trail led down over the face of the cliff in an alarming manner, a test for surefooted ponies. Below, it was all thick shadow. Their animals, stepping delicately, were taking them down from sunlight into late evening.
===Chinese===
小径贴着崖壁的面皮下去，下得叫人心惊，是对小马脚步稳当与否的一场试炼。底下已是一片浓影。他们的牲口细细地踏着步子，正把他们从阳光里驮下去，驮进傍晚。

===Original===
You, too, have your magic, your strong medicine, Laughing Boy, and I think it is greater than mine. This is what I want you for. Some day we shall put our two magics together; some day you will bring me here, to have this always. You will bring me, if it does not take you from me first.
===Chinese===
笑娃，你也有你的法术，你的强药，我想它比我的还大。我要你，为的就是这个。总有一天，咱们要把两个人的法术合在一处；总有一天，你会带我上这儿来，让这个永远归着我。你会带我来的——只要它不先把你从我身边夺走。

### III / III

===Original===
At length they were reaching T’o Tlakai, riding down a slope of bald rock into a valley about three miles square, surrounded by moderately high cliffs. Here and there, at their feet, were clumps of scrub oak, peach trees, and the marks of summer cornfields, where water seeped out under the rocks. Along the north cliff was a long ledge, with the rock above it rising in a concave shell of light reflected under shadow. Along the ledge stretched an imposing ruin of the Old People, at one end of which, where there must be a spring, a strip of grass showed very green. Down the middle of the valley spindly cottonwoods marked the course of the wash. The rest was dull and colourless﻿—sand hills, sand, rocks, sagebrush, greasewood, some sheep. Nearly in the centre were five hogans, two square ones of leaves, deserted now that winter was at hand, and three dome-shaped mud ones. The framework of the medicine-lodge for the dance had already been set up. There were a good many horses tethered around the settlement.
===Chinese===
他们终于快到特奥·特莱凯了，顺着一面秃岩坡下去，进到一片约莫三英里见方的谷地，四周围着不算高的峭壁。脚边这里一丛那里一丛，是矮栎、桃树，还有夏天玉米地的印痕，水便打岩石底下渗出来。北面崖壁下横着一道长长的岩阶，上头的岩壁穹起，凹成一爿壳，阴影底下映着天光。岩阶上躺着一长片古人的残墟，气象森然；一头上——那处必有泉水——一线草色显得极绿。谷地正中，细瘦的三角叶杨标出干河床的去向。其余的一切都暗沉沉没有颜色——沙冈、沙、岩石、山艾、肥皂木，还有些羊。靠近谷心是五座霍根屋：两座方头的是树叶搭的，眼见冬天临头，已经弃置；三座是圆顶的泥屋。那座专为舞仪预备的医药霍根屋，骨架已经立了起来。聚落四下拴着不少马。

===Original===
It did not look like much, but she found it threatening, inimical. She wanted some sign; it would have been a relief if people had come buzzing out as they appeared over the brow of the rock, if there had been shouts of anger, anything. The houses were more than a mile away still. Would they be clever people or stupid, hostile, friendly, or resigned? Were they able opponents or could she conquer them? The quiet houses fascinated her. Just she against all those, against everything here, these rocks, these underfed trees, those far-off mountains, the little bushes. She had fought against worse, but this meant so much. The horses seemed barely to crawl.
===Chinese===
这地方瞧着并不起眼，她却觉出了它的威吓、它的敌意。她要一个兆头；方才他们打岩脊上露头的时候，倘若人们嗡地拥出来，倘若有一阵怒骂，无论什么，倒还让她松一口气。那些屋子离着还有一英里多地。他们是些伶俐人还是蠢人？是仇视，是亲善，还是认了命？他们是对付得了的敌手，还是她能降得住他们？那些安安静静的屋子把她迷住了。单是她一个，对着那边所有的人，对着这里的每一样东西——这些岩石，这些吃不饱的树，那些远远的山，那些小小的灌木。她跟更凶的都交过手，可这一场干系太重。马仿佛只是在地上爬。

===Original===
Ahead of her, Laughing Boy was singing a hymn, half aloud:
===Chinese===
她前头，笑娃半出声地唱着一支颂歌：

===Original===
“Dawn Boy Hill rises,
						

						Jewels Hill rises,
						

						White Corn Hill rises﻿ ﻿…
						

						Those people their fields, my fields, now they rise all beautiful before me!”
===Chinese===
“黎明少年之山升起，
						

						珍宝之山升起，
						

						白玉米之山升起……
						

						那些人的田野，我的田野，如今都在我面前美丽地升起！”

## Chapter XII / 第十二章

### I / I

===Original===
During the greetings, Laughing Boy took stock. With entire confidence in his wife’s ability to win over these people, he carried himself as though he had no faintest idea that there might be strained relations between them, but in his mind he was calling the roll of his family. Wounded Face, sitting apart in his blanket like a sleepy eagle, was against him. Spotted Horse, the younger uncle, was waiting; meantime he intended to be cordial. Spotted Horse would follow somebody’s lead, whoever spoke most commandingly; afterwards, if the issue were unpleasant, he would mildly deplore it. His mother was against him, but she too waited, not declaring herself, not closing her judgment yet. His sisters took no position, but welcomed the stranger and did their best to make her at home because she was his wife. Bow’s Son, his brother, and Bay Horse, his brother-in-law, both evidently thought he had made a fool of himself, and felt hostile towards her. Bay Horse could be discounted; he did not belong to the clan, and the taboo which forbids a man’s looking upon his mother-in-law kept him away from the family circle most of the time.

===Chinese===
寒暄问安的当口，笑娃（Laughing Boy）在心里掂量各人。他全然信得过妻子笼络这些人的本事，举手投足便装得丝毫不知彼此之间或许存着嫌隙，心里却把自家的名册一个个点过去。疤脸（Wounded Face）独自裹在毯子里坐着，像一只打盹的鹰，是反对他的。小舅舅花斑马（Spotted Horse）还在观望；眼底下他打算热络相待。花斑马总要跟从什么人的主张，看谁的话口气最硬；事后倘若结局不愉快，他也只会温温和和地叹息一番。他母亲反对他，可她也在等，不亮明态度，评断还没有落槌。几个姐妹不站边，只管欢迎这远来的客人，尽力让她安顿得像在自己家里，只因她是他的妻子。弟弟弓之子（Bow’s Son）和姐夫枣红马（Bay Horse）显然都认定他干了件糊涂事，对她怀着敌意。枣红马可以撇开不算；他不属于这个氏族，再说那桩忌讳——做男人的不得瞧见自己的岳母——平日里就把他挡在家门圈子外头。

===Original===
Out of the corner of his eye, without seeming to pay attention, he watched Slim Girl with relish as she said and did exactly the right things, giving an excellent impersonation of just any attractive woman.

===Chinese===
他用眼角余光瞟着，装出并不留意的样子，饶有兴味地看瘦姑娘（Slim Girl）一言一语、一举一动都拿捏得恰到好处，活脱脱扮出一个招人喜欢的寻常女子。

===Original===
Now Jesting Squaw’s Son dismounted before the hogan and stood beside him, looking anxious and hesitant. It was plain to see that he was concerned only lest there should be some estrangement between them, lest an alien life might have made a gulf. Friendship stood firm and true. So they embraced and wrestled and spoke loudly to each other.

===Chinese===
这时打趣婆娘之子（Jesting Squaw’s Son）在霍根屋前下了马，站到他身边，一脸的忐忑迟疑。看得出他挂心的只有一桩：生怕两人之间生了分，生怕外头那段日子在当中劈出一道鸿沟。友情依旧坚实如初。于是两人搂抱摔跤，大声说起话来。

===Original===
He faced his father last, and most anxiously. Two Bows had held back from the others; his was an awkward position in this matter. Long days of teaching the jeweller’s craft, hunts together, lessons in the trail and the bow, work shared, had brought them very near to each other. They were father and son, and they were close friends. Laughing Boy admired and emulated the old warrior, and he could confide in him. Two Bows saw some of himself bearing fruit anew in the young brave. And yet, in a matter like this, his rights were only those of courtesy﻿—to Laughing Boy’s own clansmen, to his mother and her brothers, was the decision. He could only watch for the time when his purely personal influence might turn the scales. Now, he said nothing. His son could feel fondness and sympathy there, but whether approval went with it he could not tell.

===Chinese===
他最后才去面对父亲，也是顶提心吊胆的一回。双弓（Two Bows）先前一直避开众人；在这件事上，他的处境左右为难。多少个日夜教儿子银匠手艺，一同打猎，传授辨路和弓箭的功夫，同担共扛的活计，把爷儿俩拉得极近。他们是父子，也是知交。笑娃敬慕这位老战士，处处学他，有什么心事也能向他吐露。双弓则看着自己身上的某些东西，又在年轻的勇士身上重新开花结果。然而在这一类事情上，他的权利只剩客气二字——作数的，是笑娃自家的氏族亲人，是他母亲和舅舅们。他只能瞅准时机，让自己这份纯属私人的情分去拨动天平。此刻他一言不发。儿子在他身上感觉得到疼爱和体恤，只是这疼爱里头带不带首肯，就说不准了。

### II / II

===Original===
Laughing Boy had been half-afraid lest, like Friend of the Eagles, or Reared in a Mountain, he would find that his own people seemed dirty and smelled badly when he returned to them. Secretly, even a little shamefacedly, he considered the life that he was living perhaps not so far removed from that of ordinary Earth People as the Eagles’ home in the sky, or the mother-of-pearl and turquoise dwellings of the Divine Ones, but still something apart, like the magic country at the end of Old Age River. He had waited somewhat anxiously for his first impression, and found that his home was delightfully as he had imagined it. Everything was the same; it seemed a miracle. That which had been intimate and dear was so still, only now nothing was taken for granted, but every commonest detail leapt to him with new vividness.

===Chinese===
笑娃原先还有几分担心，怕自己像鹰之友（Friend of the Eagles），或是山中养大（Reared in a Mountain）那样，回到自己人中间，会觉得他们又腌臜、气味又难闻。他心里暗暗地、甚至有点儿难为情地想，自己眼下过的日子，也许不像鹰在天上的家、不像神圣之灵（Divine Ones）珠母和绿松石的宫室那样，同寻常地上之民（Earth People）的日子隔得那么远，却终究自成一重天地，就像暮年河（Old Age River）尽头的奇境。他怀着几分焦灼等那第一眼的印象，结果发现家里喜滋滋地正同他想象的一模一样。什么都没变样；简直是桩奇迹。原本亲昵珍贵的东西依旧亲昵珍贵，只是如今没有一样是理所当然的了，顶平常琐碎的细节，也带着崭新的鲜明朝他扑面而来。

===Original===
There were constant little surges of delight in his heart over trivial, minor things﻿—a shadow across a cliff, the bend of a cottonwood, the sheep coming in at evening, their silly, solemn faces all about the hogan﻿—why should they have changed? A man does not realize that he has changed himself, or only partially recognizes it, thinking that the world about him is different; a familiar dish has become no longer enjoyable, a fundamental aphorism no longer true; it is a surprise, then, when his eyes and ears report unchanged, familiar impressions. So the wonderful sameness of things, the unfailing way in which expectation was fulfilled, were proofs of something beautiful in the order of the world. It was glorious to pick up the threads of talk where he had dropped them, discussing the old, well-worn subjects casually and in detail, as though they were still inlaid in his life, with just a little seasoning of the attitude of one who has been farther and seen more.

===Chinese===
一些琐碎细微的小事，总在他心里激起一阵一阵小小的欢喜——一道掠过岩壁的影子，一株棉白杨弯下的身姿，傍晚归圈的羊群，那些又傻气又一本正经的脸挤满霍根屋四周——它们凭什么要变呢？一个人并不知道自己已经变了，或者只认出一半，倒以为是周遭的世界不同了；一道熟悉的吃食不再有味，一条顶要紧的老话不再灵验；于是等到耳目照旧报来熟悉的印象，反倒成了意外。万物这般美妙地如常，期望桩桩不落空地应验，便成了世界秩序里某种美好之物的凭证。把先前撂下的话头从撂下的地方接起来，随口而又细致地谈论那些谈旧了的老题目，仿佛它们依然镶嵌在自己的日子里，再稍稍带上一点走得更远、见得更多的人才有的口气——这真是痛快极了。

===Original===
One could see that his family had expected some outlandishness. Now they were puzzled; some disappointed, and some pleased to see how normal and Navajo were Laughing Boy and his wife. Her blankets spoke for them with many tongues, and the solid evidences of their prosperity, all Navajo, nothing bizarre or American, but good honest silver, turquoise, coral﻿—“hard goods”﻿—and handsome Indian ponies.

===Chinese===
看得出，他家里原是备着要看些洋怪事的。如今他们反倒纳闷了；有的失望，有的见笑娃两口子这样本分、这样纳瓦霍，心里欢喜。她的毯子替他们说了千言万语，还有那份殷实家业的种种实证，全是纳瓦霍的物事，没有一样古怪美国派，只有地道厚实的银子、绿松石、珊瑚——“硬货”——外加几匹体面的印第安小马。

===Original===
He watched Slim Girl, seeing the shutters closed behind her eyes, correct, sure, in hand, doing just the perfect thing. He was swept by constantly recurring waves of pleasure in her, and felt, as he sometimes did, a faint fear of that detached self-command. Slowly they were being forced to accept her as really belonging to the People. It pleased his dramatic instinct, as well as the strong sense of privacy he had concerning their relationship, to play up, being very normal, and letting no look or gesture suggest that they two came from a land of enchantment.

===Chinese===
他看着瘦姑娘，看见她眼睛后面落下了一道道百叶窗，妥帖，笃定，一切拿捏在手，做的桩桩件件恰到好处。一阵接一阵的欢喜在他心里漫过；同时，如同他偶尔会有过的那样，对那份置身事外的自制，他生出一丝隐隐的惧意。家人们正被逼着一点点承认：她当真算是迪内（The People）的人了。他把戏做足——一派寻常样子，不让一个眼色、一个手势透露出他们俩来自一片施了魔法的土地——这既满足他天生的戏子本能，也合乎他把两人关系捂得严严实实的那份私心。

===Original===
Knowing her well, he could see that she was at high tension, and secretly watchful. He had no idea that that strain, that painful vigilance, was above all for himself.

===Chinese===
他深知她的为人，看得出她绷得紧紧的，暗地里时时提防。他却万万想不到，那份紧张、那份苦楚的警醒，归根结底是为了他自己。

===Original===
When he was alone with his father, he showed him the silver-mounted bridle and some of his other jewelry. Two Bows turned over the harness, feeling the surface with his fingertips.

===Chinese===
趁单独同父亲在一处，他把镶银的笼头和另外几件首饰拿给父亲看。双弓把那副辔头翻来覆去，用指尖细摸表面的手工。

===Original===
“I have nothing more to teach you﻿—that is well done.” He tapped the cheek-strap. “I should not have thought of using that design that way.”

===Chinese===
“我没有什么可再教你的了——这活儿做得好。”他敲了敲颊革。“我就想不到能把那个花样这么用。”

===Original===
From Two Bows, such praise made it hard to keep a quiet, modest face.

===Chinese===
从双弓嘴里听来这样的夸赞，要维持一张沉静谦和的脸，可真不容易。

===Original===
Jesting Squaw’s Son came back in the late afternoon. They drifted off together, with arms over each other’s shoulders, until they came to rest under the scrub oaks behind the peach trees. They discussed this and that, vaguely, trailing off into silence, playing with twigs and pebbles, running their fingers through the sand, occupying their hands. At length Laughing Boy looked at his friend and spoke:

===Chinese===
傍晚时分，打趣婆娘之子回来了。两人搭着肩膀晃出去，一路晃到桃树后面那些矮栎底下才安顿下来。他们有一搭没一搭地说些闲话，说着说着就沉进了沉默里，摆弄树枝石子，手指在沙里划来划去，让手上有事可做。末了，笑娃望着朋友开了口：

===Original===
“I do not talk to those people. Some of them have their minds made up, some of them will not understand. I do not think you will know what I am talking about, but you understand me. I want you to know.

===Chinese===
“我不同那些人讲。他们有的主意已定，有的不会懂。我想你听不明白我要说的是什么，可你懂得我这个人。我要你知道。

===Original===
“I have been down Old Age River in the log, with sheet-lightning and rainbows and soft rain, and the gods on either side to guide me. The Eagles have put lightning snakes and sunbeams and rainbows under me; they have carried me through the hole in the sky. I have been through the little crack in the rocks with Red God and seen the homes of the Butterflies and the Mountain Sheep and the Divine Ones. I have heard the Four Singers on the Four Mountains. I mean that woman.

===Chinese===
“我乘着木头顺暮年河而下，一路片闪、彩虹、软雨，两岸都有神明引导。众鹰把闪电蛇、阳光和彩虹铺在我身下；他们驮着我穿过了天上的洞。我跟着红神（Red God）钻过岩石间的小裂缝，见过蝴蝶、大角羊和神圣之灵的家园。我在四座圣山上听见过四位颂唱师唱。我说的是那个女人。

===Original===
“It sounds like insane talk. It is not. It is not just because I am in love. It is not what I feel when I am near her, what happens to my blood when she touches me. I know about that. I have thought about that. It is what goes on there. It is all sorts of things, but you would have to live there to see it.

===Chinese===
“这些话听着像疯话。不是疯话。也不单是因为我坠了情网。不在我挨着她时心里是什么滋味，不在她碰我时我血里翻起什么。那个我清楚。那个我想过。我说的是那边过的日子本身。那是种种的一切，可你得住到那儿去才看得见。

===Original===
“I know the kind of thing my uncle says. It is not true. We are not acting out here, we are pretending. We have masks on, so they will not see our real faces. You have seen her blankets and my hard goods. Those are true. Those are just part of it.”

===Chinese===
“我舅舅说的那套话，我知道。那不是真的。我们在这儿不是本来面目，是假扮。我们戴着面具，好叫他们看不见我们真正的脸。她的毯子、我的硬货你都见过了。那些是真的。那些不过是其中一角。”

===Original===
Jesting Squaw’s Son answered, “I have seen the blankets and the hard goods; they sing. I am happy about you.”

===Chinese===
打趣婆娘之子答道：“毯子和硬货我都见了；它们会唱歌。我为你欢喜。”

===Original===
He felt better after that, he cared that his friend should know, and, in contradistinction to the others, telling him did not lessen the rare quality of the thing described. He returned to the hogans feeling better able to act his part.

===Chinese===
这以后他觉得松快了些；他在乎让朋友知道，而且恰恰同对旁人相反，说给他听，并不折损那件被说的事的稀罕成色。他回到霍根屋那边，觉得这出戏更有本事演下去了。

===Original===
He found the evening meal most enjoyable as he watched the good ways and mannerisms of his family. Among them he could make out a growing perplexity. What had that old man told them to expect? A word slipped from his brother to his younger uncle gave him the cue, filling his heart with glee. When he got a chance he whispered to Slim Girl,

===Chinese===
晚饭桌上，他看着家里众人那些可爱做派和积年的习惯，吃得津津有味。他听得出众人中间有一种渐渐加深的困惑。那老头子到底嘱咐他们等着看什么来着？弟弟对小舅舅漏出的半句话给了他提示，乐得他心花怒放。得了个空子，他悄声对瘦姑娘说：

===Original===
“What my uncle said﻿—they expected you to have no manners. They were waiting for you to act like an American, and give them something to talk about.”

===Chinese===
“我舅舅说了那些话——他们只当你半点礼数也不懂。他们正等着你拿出美国人的做派来，好有桩事拿去说嘴。”

===Original===
He lolled back on the sheepskins, laughing inside himself. A smile shadowed the corners of his wife’s mouth.

===Chinese===
他歪在羊皮上，肚子里暗笑。他妻子的嘴角浮起一丝若隐若现的笑影。

### III / III

===Original===
Slim Girl watched the ceremony with interest, feeling in a pronounced fashion the mixed emotion she had towards so much that was going on about her. It might be a weapon to destroy her, for the very reason that it was a summation and a visible expression of many things in her people’s life that mattered to her. She had a sneaking suspicion that the family had gone to the expense of a full Night Chant largely because of the effect they thought it might have on the erring member.

===Chinese===
瘦姑娘怀着兴味观看仪式，心里分外清楚地觉出自己对周遭种种事体的百般滋味。这仪式也可能成为毁掉她的兵器，正因为它集其大成、活生生摆出了她族人生活中许多于她要紧的东西。她心里藏着一丝疑影：这家人肯破费办一场全本的夜颂仪（Night Chant），多半是看中了它或许能在那个走岔了路的人身上起的作用。

===Original===
It was sometimes absurd and sometimes quite beautiful. The masked dancers were grotesque, but there were moments by firelight when their shapeless heads and painted bodies, their rhythmic, intent movements, became grand and awe-inspiring. The long and repetitious prayers were often monotonous, chanted to dull, heavy music, but in the worst of them there were flashes of poetic feeling. Her American education had dulled her sensibility to the quick, compact imagery of a single statement, leaving to the hearer, the evocation of the picture intended that forms the basis of Navajo poetry. Still, she caught it sometimes,

===Chinese===
它有时荒唐，有时又美得很。戴面具的舞者样子怪诞，可火光里有那么几个瞬间，他们那没有形状的头脸、彩绘的身子、专注而合着节拍的动作，会忽然变得雄浑庄严，令人敬畏。冗长重复的祷词往往单调，配着沉滞的重乐唱诵，可就在最沉闷的段落里，也闪过诗意的火光。她受的美国教育磨钝了她对那种一语道破、以少胜多的意象的感受力——只把一句话撂给听的人，让他自己在心里唤出那幅图景，纳瓦霍诗歌的根基正在于此。尽管如此，她有时还是接得住，

===Original===
“By red rocks the green corn grows,
						

						Beautifully it grows﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
“红岩旁边，绿玉米生长，

						美美地生长……”

===Original===
She saw it, and the terse implication that takes for granted all that the Indians feel about corn, contenting itself with merely calling forth that feeling.

===Chinese===
她看见了那幅图景，也看见那话里短促的余意——它把印第安人对玉米怀有的一切视作当然，自己只管把那份情唤起来，再不多添一字。

===Original===
She tried to think that these things were native and close to her, but found that she could only observe them objectively. She was foreign now. She could sympathize with their spirit, but not enter into it. A door had been closed to her, and at times, even standing here among the other spectators, in the heart of the Navajo country, she was swept again by a hopeless nostalgia for the country and the people, forever lost, of her dim childhood.

===Chinese===
她试着把这些东西当作生来就有、贴着自己心窝的，却发现只能客观地看着它们。她如今是外人了。她能体谅其中的精神，却进不去了。有一扇门对她关死了；有时候，即便站在这里，站在看客中间，站在纳瓦霍乡土的腹地，她也会重新被一种无望的乡愁卷走——为那片土地和那群人，她蒙昧童年里那片永远失掉了的土地和人。

===Original===
When she had been a very little girl, she had trembled with terror and awe at the sight of the very gods coming into the circle of people. Out in the darkness one heard their distant call, repeated as they came nearer, until with the fourth cry they entered the firelight. They danced and sang there, majestic and strange; then they vanished again to return to their homes in the sacred places. Now they were just Indians whom she knew, dressed up in a rather silly way. Like many unreligious people, she kept slipping into the idea that these worshippers were pretending to be taken in by the patently absurd. Most of the adult spectators had been through the Night Chant initiation; all of them knew that the gods were no more than men in masks; how could they be so reverent? What was her devout husband’s ecstasy, or his devoutness, when he himself put on the painted rawhide bag trimmed with spruce and feathers, pretending to be Talking God?

===Chinese===
她还是个小丫头的时候，一见真真切切的神明走进人群的圈子，就会又惊又惧地发抖。黑暗深处听得见他们遥远的呼喊，一声一声应和着近前来，到第四声呐喊，他们便踏进了火光。他们在那里歌舞，威严而奇异；随后又隐没不见，回圣地的家去了。如今他们不过是她认得的印第安人，穿戴得有几分可笑。同许多没有信仰的人一样，她总是不由自主地滑进一个念头：这些礼拜的人是在假装被那些明摆着的荒唐玩意儿哄住。看客里的成年人多半行过夜颂仪的入仪礼；他们全知道神明不过是戴面具的人；他们怎么能这样虔敬？她那虔敬的丈夫，等他自己套上那只缀着云杉枝和羽毛的彩绘生皮袋子、假扮说话神（Talking God）的时候，他的忘形，他的虔敬，又算什么？

===Original===
She remembered the sacrament at school when she had been Christian. She had known that the wine came from the vineyard of an Italian who was a Catholic﻿—something vaguely wicked﻿—and that the bread was just bread. She knew the minister for a nice man whose wife rather bullied him. Yet she had believed that Christ’s blood appeared in the wine, or something like that, and had been uplifted when she partook of it.

===Chinese===
她想起上学时、自己还信基督那阵子的圣餐礼。她早知道那酒是一个信天主教的意大利人的葡萄园里来的——隐隐透着点邪性——也知道那饼不过是饼。她知道那位牧师是个和善人，太太颇有些欺压他。可她当时竟信着基督的血会显现在酒里，或是差不多的意思，领受的时候，整个人还被一股庄严提了起来。

===Original===
A Klamath girl had cried bitterly before her first communion. It came out that she feared that eating Christ would make her conceive. In a legend of her own people, Raven had made a woman conceive that way. The minister had been very patient with her, and afterwards the other girls had made fun of her.

===Chinese===
一个克拉马斯（Klamath）姑娘头一回领圣餐前哭得死去活来。后来才弄明白，她是怕把基督吃下去会怀上身孕。在她自己部族的传说里，渡鸦就叫一个女人这样怀过孕。牧师待她极有耐心，过后别的姑娘们却拿她取笑了好一阵。

===Original===
The casual way in which the minister handled the jug of wine when it came used to shock her, yet when he raised the chalice, his face would be inspired. He knew it was just the Italian’s wine and himself, but he had not been pretending.

===Chinese===
牧师拿起那坛酒来随手摆弄的做派，总叫她暗暗吃惊；可待他举起圣杯，那张脸便焕发出神采。他明知那不过是意大利人的酒和自己这个人，却并不是在假装。

===Original===
These Navajos were just like that. She couldn’t make it seem reasonable to herself, but she understood it. And what effect would it have on Laughing Boy?

===Chinese===
这些纳瓦霍人正是这么回事。她没法在自己的道理里把这想周全，可她懂得它。而这又会落在笑娃身上，起什么作用呢？

===Original===
During the day she occupied herself with the women’s work of preparing the semi-sacramental ceremonial foods. She knew very little, indeed, about the ancient ways of cooking, but her sisters-in-law taught her. They were prepared to like her. Her bad reputation had reached them only vaguely, and already they were discrediting it, so that she became to them someone somehow belonging to a larger world, said to be dangerous, hence superior. Now they found her ignorant in this matter, humble, and anxious to learn. She was normal, then, what their slight experience had taught them to expect of returned schoolgirls, who were always to be pitied. They were delighted to make her their protégée and have the feeling of taking this woman of the world under their wing. Her warm response was not all acting, either; it was not often that women of any race were friendly to her without reservations.

===Chinese===
白天她跟着妇人们操持制备那半带圣餐性质的仪式吃食。古法烹调她实在懂得极少，妯娌们便一样样教她。她们原是存心要喜欢她的。她的坏名声传到她们耳朵里只剩个模模糊糊的影子，她们早就在替它打折扣了，于是在她们眼里，她成了个不知怎么归属更阔大天地的人物，据说危险，因此高人一等。如今她们见她在这上头一窍不通，肯低头，急着要学。这么说她是正常的，正合她们那点阅历教她们料想的学堂归来的姑娘——那种姑娘总是叫人可怜的。她们高高兴兴把她收作被护着的人，享受把这位见过世面的女人拢进翅膀底下的感觉。她那热络的回应也不全是做戏；不论哪一族的女人，能不带保留地对她好，这样的时候本来不多。

===Original===
Their mother, she saw, was merely conscientiously fulfilling the ceremonial requirement that everyone should feel kind towards everyone else during the days of the dance. That atmosphere of *hozoji* pervaded the whole camp with a sweetness that was saved from being laughable by the deep devotion behind it. The time of trial was not yet. Slim Girl had some cause to be happy, and so fell in with the general frame of mind, finding a certain reality of meaning in the eternally repeated “trail of beauty,” “walking in beauty,” of the ceremony.

===Chinese===
至于她们的母亲，她看得出，不过是在尽仪式的规矩——舞蹈的日子里，人人须对人人怀善意。那种霍卓吉（hozoji，美）的气氛带着一种甜味漫透整个营地，全靠底下那深深的虔敬撑着，才不至于落到可笑。考较的时辰还没有到。瘦姑娘自有她欢喜的道理，便也随了大流的心境，在仪式里永恒反复念诵的“美之踪迹”“在美之中行走”中，咂摸出几分实在的意味来。

===Original===
In a sentimental way she played at believing her people’s religion, and indeed began to find some truth in its basic doctrine, but when she attempted to extend acceptance to the forms which she observed, her sense of the grotesque made it a farce. Meantime she was conquering these people; some were her friends already; her enemies were checked and nonplussed. The opening skirmishes, at least, were hers. She was moving ever more in the stream of Navajo life. She did have cause to be happy. The religion might remain meaningless to her, and probably always would, but the underlying concept of the active force of *hozoji* became real.

===Chinese===
她以一种多愁善感的方式玩着相信族人宗教的游戏，也果真开始在那根本教义里咂出几分真味来；可她一旦想把这认可推广到眼前这些形式上，她那对怪诞的敏感便把它变成一出闹剧。与此同时，她正在征服这些人；有的已经是她的朋友；与她作对的也已被遏制，乱了方寸。至少，开场几仗是她的。她在纳瓦霍生活的河流里越走越深。她确实有欢喜的道理。这宗教对她也许终究是无意义的，多半永远如此，但霍卓吉那股能动的力量，这个底下的观念，在她心里成了真。

### IV / IV

===Original===
The men who took part in the dance kept pretty well by themselves. For several days she did not speak to her husband. It was during the fifth afternoon that, seeing him go over where the sun warmed a rock to snatch some sleep, she followed and sat down beside him. She dreamed, watching his face. She loved him so much. There was that love, enough in itself, and then there was so much more. As she had hoped, after all, he was the means of returning to the good things of the Navajo, the good things of life. She could not lose him. What would happen when the dance was over, when it was time to leave, when old Wounded Face showed his hand? She was dependent on this man, her husband; she could not lose him.

===Chinese===
参加舞蹈的男人们大致自聚在一处。接连几天她没有同丈夫说话。到第五天下午，她望见他往太阳晒暖的一块岩石那边去打个盹，便跟过去在他身旁坐下。她端详着他的脸，出了神。她这样爱他。有那样的爱，本来尽够了，可后头还有那么多指望。正如她当初盼的，他到底是她回到纳瓦霍种种好东西、人生种种好东西去的路径。她不能失去他。等舞蹈完了，到该动身的时候，等老疤脸亮出他的牌来，会怎么样呢？她倚仗着这个男人，她的丈夫；她不能失去他。

===Original===
She smoked and waited. At length he woke. She reached out and drew her fingers across the back of his hand.

===Chinese===
她抽着烟等。他终于醒了。她伸出手，手指在他手背上轻轻划过。

===Original===
“You must not do that.”

===Chinese===
“你可不能这样。”

===Original===
“Why not?”

===Chinese===
“为什么不能？”

===Original===
“I am thinking about the Holy Things. I have to concern my mind only with them. You should not have come here.”

===Chinese===
“我心里正想着神圣之物。我的心思只许放在它们上头。你不该到这儿来。”

===Original===
“Is it bad to think about me? Are your thoughts of me not﻿—*hozoji*?” She smiled.

===Chinese===
“想着我就不好吗？你对我的念头难道不是——霍卓吉？”她笑了。

===Original===
He remained grave. “They are *hozoji*, but they are not all of it. When I think about the whole, I am thinking about you, too. I give thanks for you. But I must not just think about you and forget all the rest. Now, go away.”

===Chinese===
他依旧正色：“它们是霍卓吉，可它们不是全部。我想着整体的时候，也想着你。我为你感恩。可我不能光想着你，把其余一切都忘了。好了，走吧。”

===Original===
“I see.”

===Chinese===
“我明白了。”

===Original===
She went softly. Two voices spoke within her; one, that this was the beginning of destruction; the other, that this meant nothing; indeed, that it was a good sign that her presence could disturb him so. Overriding both opinions was a feeling that, unless she was the whole for him, she could not be sure of holding him, and her imperiousness rebelled at being ever subordinate.

===Chinese===
她轻轻走开了。心里有两个声音在说话；一个说，这是毁败的开头；另一个说，这算不了什么；不但算不了什么，她的存在竟能这样搅扰他，反倒是个好兆头。压过这两种意见的，是一种感觉：除非她对他就是全部，否则她拿不准拴得住他；而她那股专横气，一想到永远要屈居人下，便起来造反。

===Original===
And still the ceremony was only half over. What would the remainder bring? She watched the changing rites. The ninth night passed, and the tenth day. She marvelled at the men’s endurance; they had periods of rest, but there were night vigils, and for Mountain Singer, endless preparatory prayers. He did not seem tired; rather one would say that he drew rest and strength from his songs. She was sorry for the sick girl, a passive bundle of blankets inside the medicine hogan, sadly in need of quiet and fresh air.

===Chinese===
而仪式才过了一半。余下的还会带来什么？她看着仪节一变一变地演下去。第九夜过去了，第十日也过去了。男人们的耐力叫她惊叹；他们有轮番的歇息，可夜里要守夜，对山地颂唱师（Mountain Singer）来说，还有没完没了的预备祷词。他不像疲乏的样子；倒不如说，他是从自己的歌里汲取歇息和力气。她可怜那个生病的姑娘，一包任人摆布的毯子躺在医药霍根屋里，亟需安静和新鲜空气，却那样求不到。

===Original===
During that last day visitors began to arrive, until two or three hundred were camped in the valley. There was a slaughtering of sheep and wholesale boiling of coffee and tea. Slim Girl was kept gratefully busy helping in the preparations. The tenth night, with the rite of the Grandfather of the Gods, was the climax.

===Chinese===
最后那一天，客人们陆续到了，到后来谷地里扎下两三百人的营。宰羊的宰羊，大锅大锅地煮咖啡煮茶。瘦姑娘帮着操持，忙个不停，众人感激。第十夜有诸神之祖（Grandfather of the Gods）的仪式，是整个大典的极顶。

===Original===
It was a fine spectacle, the many dancing figures in the firelight, their strange masks and the dull earth-colours, blue, red, white, yellow, black﻿—a broad white zigzag across a black chest, a red figure on blue, outlined with white, standing out in the half-light of the fire. The dancers were never more intent, the chanting more ecstatic. There was real dramatic quality in the entrance of the Grandfather. She was interested, excited. These were her people, putting themselves in touch with eternal forces by means of voice, strength, rhythm, colour, design﻿—everything they had to use. They were creating something strong and barbaric and suitable, and still beautiful.

===Chinese===
好一场壮观的景象，火光里许许多多舞动的人影，他们奇异的面具和沉暗的土色，蓝、红、白、黄、黑——一道宽宽的白色之字纹横过黑色的胸膛，蓝底上一个红色的图形，勾着白边，在火光的半明半暗里凸现出来。舞者们的专注从没有这样深，唱诵从没有这样忘我。诸神之祖登场的那一刻，真有一种戏剧的分量。她看得有趣，看得兴奋。这些是她的人，用嗓音、力气、节拍、色彩、图样——用手头一切可用的东西——把自己同永恒的力量接通。他们正在造出一种强有力的、蛮野而又相称的东西，而且依然是美的。

===Original===
“In beauty it is finished,

					In beauty it is finished,

					In beauty it is finished,

					In beauty it is finished!”

===Chinese===
“在美之中告成，

					在美之中告成，

					在美之中告成，

					在美之中告成！”

### V / V

===Original===
The next day was one of letdown and much sleeping. By dusk, most of the visitors had ridden away. After supper, Laughing Boy’s mother and uncles went over to one of the deserted summer hogans. He finished his cigarette and followed. Wounded Face returned and spoke to Two Bows, who went back with him. Mountain Singer rode in, dismounted, and joined them.

===Chinese===
第二天是泄劲的一天，人人都在补觉。到黄昏，客人大半骑马散去了。晚饭后，笑娃的母亲和舅舅们踱到一座空出来的夏季霍根屋那边去。他抽完烟卷，跟了过去。疤脸回来了，同双弓说了句什么，双弓便随他折回去。山地颂唱师骑马过来，下了马，加入他们。

===Original===
So she was not to be allowed to fight for herself. None of the others at the fire paid any attention; not even casting an extra glance towards Slim Girl. She remembered vaguely that, when a marriage-contract was under discussion, it was the correct thing for the girl concerned to go well away from the house. She supposed that some such etiquette could be invoked to cover this occasion. It would have to do. She slipped out into the darkness, watching to see if her going caused any commentary of exchanged looks. Then she went swiftly away from the hogans, past the corral, where she deliberately startled a herd-dog into barking.

===Chinese===
这么说，是不容她自己出阵了。火堆边其余的人一个也不理会，连多瞟瘦姑娘一眼的都没有。她模模糊糊记起，家里议婚约的时候，照规矩当事的姑娘要躲得离屋子远远的。她想，大约可以援引这样一条礼数来遮盖眼下这场合。也只好如此了。她悄悄溜进黑暗里，留意自己的离去有没有惹起人们递眼色的议论。随后她快步离开霍根屋，经过畜栏，还存心惊动一条牧犬，教它吠了起来。

===Original===
She circled behind to come up on the summer hogan, carefully now, thinking of the silent feet and quick ears of her people, feeling herself clumsy, her limbs managed by indirect control. She crouched in the shadow of the back wall, clutching her blanket about her for warmth, praying that her teeth would not chatter. She was clearly conscious of the beauty of the night, its stars and sharp cold, the smell of sage and sand, the faint rustle of leaves on the hogan.

===Chinese===
她兜到后面，摸近那座夏季霍根屋，这会儿放轻了手脚，想着族人生来无声的脚步和灵敏的耳朵，只觉得自己笨手笨脚，四肢像被一只看不见的手迂回地操控着。她蹲在后墙的阴影里，把毯子裹紧在身上取暖，一路祷告牙齿可别打颤。她清清楚楚觉出这夜的美，它的星星和凛冽的寒气，鼠尾草和沙土的气味，霍根屋顶上叶子轻微的沙沙声。

===Original===
They were lighting a fire. When it was burning well, they gathered close to it, so that peering between the leaves and branches she saw them as dim, significant masses with their faces faintly shown, identifying them. Mountain Singer was in the place of honour, with his back to her; on his left sat Walked Around, Laughing Boy’s mother; on his right was Spotted Horse. Wounded Face was next to him, facing Laughing Boy, and Two Bows sat a little bit back, near the door. She summed them up to herself, wishing she could be present to use her skill and have her share in the approaching conflict. It was not fair. She wished she could see Mountain Singer’s face; that old man’s influence would be emphasized, now that he was just through conducting the chant, and her husband with serving him as acolyte. He was the leader of the Tahtchini Clan in this section; his importance was shown by his seat of honour in this conclave of people to whom he was only distantly related. Spotted Horse did not amount to much. Walked Around hated her, personally and with fear. Wounded Face was set against her for more general, but weighty reasons. As for Two Bows, she could not tell. He had a quality of understanding which might make him her friend or her most dangerous enemy. In any case, he was here only as a privileged outsider.

===Chinese===
他们在生火。火烧旺了，众人围拢过去，她隔着树叶枝条窥望，只见他们成了一团团模糊而意味深长的影子，脸孔隐约可辨，一个个认了出来。山地颂唱师坐在尊位上，背朝着她；他左手边坐着走四方（Walked Around），笑娃的母亲；右手边是花斑马。疤脸挨着他，正对着笑娃；双弓坐得靠后一点，挨着门。她在心里把他们逐个掂量，恨不得亲身在场，施展出手段，在临近的交锋里占上自己的一份。这不公平。她但愿能看见山地颂唱师的脸；这老头子刚主持完颂仪，她丈夫又刚侍奉他做过助祭，他这时候的分量只会更重。他是这一带塔奇尼氏（Tahtchini）的首领；在这一屋跟他只是远亲的人们的聚议里，他坐了尊位，他的分量由此可见。花斑马成不了什么气候。走四方恨她，是切身的恨，掺着惧怕。疤脸反对她，为的是更宽泛却也更沉的缘故。至于双弓，她说不准。他有一种善解人意的品性，这品性可以叫他做她的朋友，也可以叫他做她最危险的敌人。无论如何，他坐在这里，只是个蒙了特权的局外人。

===Original===
The fire began to make warmth, and tobacco was passed round. Nobody spoke for several minutes. Then Mountain Singer said:

===Chinese===
火渐渐有了暖意，烟丝传了一圈。好几分钟没人说话。然后山地颂唱师开口道：

===Original===
“We are thinking about my younger brother here; we are thinking about what he should do. We have come here to talk it over with him.”

===Chinese===
“我们惦记的是我这儿这位弟弟；惦记的是他该怎么办。我们到这儿来，是要同他把这事谈一谈。”

===Original===
They went on smoking. They were sombre bundles of shadow, in their blankets, with faces of people faintly seen. Wounded Face spat out a grain of tobacco. “My nephew, we do not think it is good, this thing you are doing. We have talked about it a long time among ourselves. We know about that woman, that she﻿—”

===Chinese===
众人接着抽烟。一个个裹在毯子里，成了黑沉沉的一团团影子，人脸依稀可辨。疤脸吐出一粒烟丝。“侄儿，你眼下做的这件事，我们觉得不好。我们自己人当中已经商议了很久。那个女人的底细我们知道，她——”

===Original===
Laughing Boy raised his head. “You have said those things once, uncle, and I have heard them. Do not say them again, those things. If you do, there will not be any talk. Tell yourself that I have heard them, and know what I think of them. They were said in Killed a Navajo’s hogan. I heard them there. Now go on from that.”

===Chinese===
笑娃抬起头来。“舅舅，那些话你说过一遍了，我听见了。别再说第二遍，那些话。你若再说，这桩谈话就没有了。你就想成我已经听过，也知道我对它们的看法。那些话是在杀纳瓦霍人（Killed a Navajo）的霍根屋里说的。我在那儿听见的。就打那儿往下说吧。”

===Original===
They talked, watching the end of their cigarettes, or with the right hand rubbing over the fingers of the left, as though to bring the words out, or touching each fingertip in turn, with their eyes upon their hands, so that the even voices seemed utterly detached, the persons mere media for uttering thoughts formed at the back of nowhere.

===Chinese===
他们说着话，眼睛瞅着烟卷的火头，或是用右手摩挲左手的指头，仿佛这样能把话引出来，又或是一根指尖一根指尖地挨个碰过去，眼睛盯在手上；于是那一条条平稳的嗓音听上去全然超然，说话的人不过是些媒介，传达着从无何有之乡深处生出的念头。

===Original===
“Perhaps you are mistaken, I think, but I do as you say. You are making unhappiness for yourself, you are making ugliness. You are of The People, the good life for you is theirs. It is all very well now while your eyes and your ears and your nose are stopped up with love, but one day you will look around and see only things that do not fit you, alkali-water to drink. You will want your own things, and you will not be able to fit them, either, I think.

===Chinese===
“兴许是你想岔了，我想，不过我照你说的办。你是在给自己造苦，你是在造丑。你是迪内的人，你的好日子是他们的日子。眼下你的眼、你的耳、你的鼻子都叫情爱堵住了，怎么都好说；可有一天你四下里一望，见到的净是跟你合不拢的东西，喝的也是碱水。到时你会想念自己原来的东西，可那些你也对不上了，我想。

===Original===
“It is all very well that you deceive the younger people with your clothes and hard goods and manners, but we can see that all the time you are apart. And you are just a light from her fire, just something she has made. She has acted and spoken well here, that one. She speaks above and below and before and behind, but she does not speak straight out forward, I think.”

===Chinese===
“你拿穿戴、硬货和做派哄哄年轻人，怎么都好；可我们看得出来，你从头到尾隔着一层。你不过是她火堆里映出的一点光，不过是她造出来的一个东西。她在这儿行事说话都漂亮，那一个。她的话上上下下、前前后后都说，就是不照直往前说，我想。”

===Original===
“We live like other People.”

===Chinese===
“我们同旁的迪内过一样的日子。”

===Original===
“Even your beginning was like Americans. You talked about it with each other, you two arranged it face to face. You had no shame. She caused that. Have you been married?”

===Chinese===
“连你们的起头都是美国式的。你们俩自己商量，面对面把事情讲定。你们不知羞。这是她带出来的。你们行过婚礼没有？”

===Original===
“Yes.”

===Chinese===
“行过。”

===Original===
“Who sang?”

===Chinese===
“谁唱的？”

===Original===
“Yellow Singer.”

===Chinese===
“黄颂唱师（Yellow Singer）。”

===Original===
“Did you look at him? No, I think. You looked at him with your eyes, so as not to fall over him when you walked past; did your mind see him? No, I think. If you think now about him, you will see him, perhaps. You will see what is left of a man when he leaves our way, when he walks in moccasins on the Americans’ road. You have seen other People who live down there. Some of them are rich, but their hearts are empty. You have seen them without happiness or beauty in their hearts, because they have lost the Trail of Beauty. Now they have nothing to put in their hearts except whiskey.”

===Chinese===
“你端详过他吗？没有，我想。你用眼睛看了他，那不过是为了走过他跟前别绊着；你的心看见他了吗？没有，我想。你现在若想想他，就会看见他了，兴许。你会看见一个人离开我们的路、穿着鹿皮靴走美国人的大道之后，剩下个什么。住在下面那边的别的迪内你见过。有的富，心却空了。你见过他们心里没有欢喜也没有美，因为他们失了美之踪迹。如今他们心里除了威士忌，再没别的可放了。”

===Original===
Slim Girl winced.

===Chinese===
瘦姑娘缩了一下。

===Original===
“Those people cannot dance in a chant and do any good. You would not want Yellow Singer to hold a chant over you, it would not bring you *hozoji*.

===Chinese===
“那些人进颂仪跳舞，舞不出好处。你也不会要黄颂唱师为你办颂仪，它带不来霍卓吉。

===Original===
“You say live like The People. Why do you live apart, then? Does she not like to be with The People, that woman?

===Chinese===
“你说要像迪内一样过活。那你们为什么躲得远远的？她不喜欢同迪内在一处吗，那个女人？

===Original===
“I have spoken.”

===Chinese===
“我说完了。”

===Original===
Laughing Boy made a gesture of brushing aside. His uncle threw his cigarette butt into the fire with an angry motion.

===Chinese===
笑娃做了个挥开的手势。他舅舅怒冲冲地把烟头丢进火里。

===Original===
Walked Around leaned forward. “What my brother says is good, but it is not all, what he has said. I have watched you, how you go about. This valley T’o Tlakai speaks to you with tongues, I think. When you look over to Chiz-na Hozolchi you hear singing, I think. You hasten to speak with your own people, you like to use your tongue for old names. You care more to talk about our sheep and our waterholes﻿—your waterholes﻿—than we do. You belong with us, and we want you. We want good for you. When you are gone, we know that you are away. That woman keeps you from us. Why does she do it? If she means good towards you and we mean good towards you, why should she be afraid of us? Perhaps because she wants to make you into something else, she does it. Perhaps because if you were among us you would see straight.

===Chinese===
走四方往前倾了倾身子。“我兄弟说的在理，可他没有说全。我一直在看着你，看你怎样四处走动。特奥·特莱凯（T’o Tlakai）这道山谷，会用千般言语对你说话，我想。你朝 Chiz-na Hozolchi 那边望过去，就听见唱歌，我想。你急着同自己人说话，你爱拿舌头去舔那些老名字。谈起我们的羊、我们的水坑——你的水坑——你比我们上心。你是我们中间的人，我们要你。我们盼着你好。你不在跟前，我们知道你走远了。那个女人把你从我们身边拉走。她为什么这样？若说她对你怀着好意，我们也对你怀着好意，她怕我们做什么？兴许因为她要把你改造成另一个东西，才这样办。兴许因为你若在我们中间，你就会看得直。

===Original===
“She has no parents, no uncles, that she should build her hogan near them. There are plenty of the Bitahni Clan here; let her come here. Come and live among us, your own people. Perhaps then, if she is not bad, we shall see that we are wrong, we shall learn to love her, my child.”

===Chinese===
“她没有爹娘，没有舅舅，没个可以在近旁盖霍根屋的人。这里比塔尼氏（Bitahni）的人多得很；叫她过来吧。你回来，住到我们自己人中间来。到那时，倘若她不是坏的，我们自会看见我们的错处，我们会学着爱她的，我的孩子。”

===Original===
Clever, clever, you bitch!

===Chinese===
好厉害，好厉害，你这个刁婆娘！

===Original===
Laughing Boy moved his hand again.

===Chinese===
笑娃又摆了摆手。

===Original===
Wounded Face took up the word. “You are young, you do not like to listen.”

===Chinese===
疤脸接过话头。“你年纪轻，不爱听劝。”

===Original===
His voice was level, but he was angry; there was tension in the hut. That was good; if they showed anger they would lose him forever.

===Chinese===
他声音平平的，人却在恼怒；屋子里绷着一股紧张。这是好事；他们若露出怒气来，就会永远失去他。

===Original===
“You do not intend to hear what we say.”

===Chinese===
“你是打定了主意不听我们的话。”

===Original===
Mountain Singer interrupted him. “His father taught him to hunt, to dance, and to work silver. His father knows him best of us all, I think. Grandfather, what is in your mind?”

===Chinese===
山地颂唱师打断了他。“打猎、跳舞、打银子，是他父亲教的。他父亲最知道他，我们众人里头，我想。老爷爷，你心里怎么想？”

===Original===
This was more important than anything heretofore.

===Chinese===
这比先前的一切都要紧。

===Original===
Two Bows spoke slowly. “We have all seen his silver, her blankets. We have seen him dance. We know, therefore, how he is now. We know that, now, all is well with him.

===Chinese===
双弓缓缓地说。“他的银子、她的毯子，我们都见了。他的舞我们也看了。因此，他如今是什么光景，我们知道。我们知道，眼下他一切都好。

===Original===
“A man makes a design well because he feels it. When he makes someone else’s design, you can tell. If he is to make someone else’s design, he must feel it in himself first. You cannot point a pistol at a man and say, ‘Make heat-lightning and clouds with tracks-meeting under them, and make it beautiful.’

===Chinese===
“一个人花样打得好，是因为他心里有那个感受。他打别人的花样时，你是看得出来的。他若要打别人的花样，得先在自己心里感受到它。你可不能拿枪指着一个人说：‘给我打一片热闪和云，云底下兽迹相交，要打得美。’

===Original===
“My son is thinking about a design for his life. Let him tell us, and if it is not good, perhaps we can show him.”

===Chinese===
“我的儿子正在为他的日子打一个花样。让他说给我们听；倘若不好，兴许我们指点得他。”

===Original===
“You have spoken well, Grandfather.”

===Chinese===
“你说得好，老爷爷。”

===Original===
“Yes, you have spoken well.” It was Spotted Horse’s only contribution.

===Chinese===
“是，你说得好。”这是花斑马全场唯一的一句话。

===Original===
They all shifted slightly, watching Laughing Boy. He spoke without hesitation, but selecting his words precisely.

===Chinese===
众人都微微挪了挪身子，望着笑娃。他说得不迟疑，字句却拣得极准。

===Original===
“I had not spoken, because I thought all your minds were made up. Now I shall tell you. I heard what my uncle told me that time; I saw Yellow Singer and those others down there. I have thought about all those things. I have not just run in like a crazy horse. Everything has been new, and I have watched and thought.

===Chinese===
“我先前不说，是因为想你们的主意都定死了。如今我说给你们听。我舅舅那回跟我说的话，我听见了；黄颂唱师和下面那边那些人，我也见了。这些事我全想过。我不是像匹疯马似的横冲直撞闯进去的。样样都是新的，我一路看，一路想。

===Original===
“I have been with that woman many moons now. I tell you that I know that those bad things are not true. Hear me.

===Chinese===
“我跟那个女人过了这么多个月亮了。我告诉你们，我知道那些坏话不是真的。听我说。

===Original===
“It is true that our life is different, but we are not following the American trail. Do not think it, that thing. She is different. She does everything as we do, more than most schoolgirls; but she is different. You have seen our silver, our blankets; if you come to us you will see how everything is like that. It is beautiful. It is the Trail of Beauty. You will just have to believe me, it is something I never imagined, we have nothing here to compare with it, that life. We do only good things. Everything good that I have ever known, all at once, could not make me as happy as she and her way do.

===Chinese===
“我们的日子确实不同，可我们没有走美国人的道。别那么想，那件事。她不一样。样样事她都照我们的规矩做，比多半的学堂姑娘做得还到；可她不一样。我们的银子、我们的毯子你们都见了；你们若到我们那儿去，会看见样样东西都是那样。那是美的。那就是美之踪迹。你们只消信我，那样的日子是我从来没有想象过的，我们这里没有任何东西比得上它。我们只做好事。我这辈子知道的所有好事加在一处，一下子全给我，也不及她和她的日子让我这样欢喜。

===Original===
“Look at me. I am older than when I left here, I know what I say. My mind is made up. I do not want you to be angry with me; I do not want you to be unhappy about me; I do not want you to tell me not to come back. You may not believe me, but I want you to wait.

===Chinese===
“看看我。我比离开这里的时候老了，我知道自己说的是什么。我主意已定。我不要你们对我动气；我不要你们为我不痛快；我不要你们叫我别回来。你们也许不信我，可我要你们等一等。

===Original===
“It does not matter. I know. I have spoken.”

===Chinese===
“不要紧。我知道。我说完了。”

### VI / VI

===Original===
Her triumph was real and urgent, but now was no time for indulging it. She walked back to the fire circle as though, from her waiting place apart, she had just seen the counsellors returning. It was time to go to bed; she found her place on the sheepskins inside the hogan. It was stuffy and warm in there save for a faint draft of air in under the blanket that closed the door and out the smoke-hole, and a coldness that seeped through the ground from outside, where the fingertips of one hand had touched the floor.

===Chinese===
她的胜利又真又烫，可眼下不是受用它的时候。她走回火堆那圈人里，那样子，像是她一直在旁边守着，这会儿才望见议事的人们回来。该睡了；她在霍根屋里的羊皮上找到自己的铺位。屋里又闷又暖，只有一丝风从挡门的毯子底下钻进来、又从排烟孔出去，另有一股寒气从屋外渗过地面，漫到她一只手按着地板的指尖上。

===Original===
That is how he feels, then. All mine. I can do anything. *Ya*, Wounded Face! Then, if I am so sure of him, why not come to live here? It is dangerous there. What a strange idea: when I am most sure I can do as I want, I give it up. Hunh! We have made almost a thousand dollars in ten months, counting the horses he has now. Everything is going perfectly. George eats out of my hand. I am strong.

===Chinese===
原来他是这么个心思。全是我的人了。我什么事都能做。呀，疤脸！这么说，我既然这样拿得稳他，为什么不搬回来住？那边是危险的。怪念头：偏偏在我最有把握随心所欲的时候，我倒把它放弃了。哼！连他如今手头的马算上，十个月我们挣了将近一千块。样样都顺顺当当。乔治（George）乖乖吃我手心里的食。我强大。

===Original===
She was becoming drowsy and making pictures. There was a story she remembered faintly, how Nayeinezgani did not kill the Hunger People. An allegory; her Slayer of Enemy Gods could not kill them, either. She could do away with them.

===Chinese===
她渐渐困了，眼前浮起一幅幅图景。有个故事她记不真了，说的是 Nayeinezgani（诛敌神者）没有杀死饥饿之民（Hunger People）。一个寓言；她自己的诛敌神者同样杀不死他们。她却能把他们清除干净。

===Original===
I have seen more than you and all you People, I know more. I shall lead you on the trail.

===Chinese===
我见过的比你多，比你们迪内所有的人都多，我知道得更多。我要领着你们上道。

===Original===
I, Slim Girl, Came With War.

===Chinese===
我，瘦姑娘，携战而来。

## Chapter XIII / 第十三章

### I / I

===Original===
They rode away from T’o Tlakai in gay company﻿—Bay Horse and Bow’s Son, Tall Brave from T’ies Napornss and his wife, and half a dozen others, men and women, returning towards T’o Tlikahn, Tsébitai, and Seinsaidesah. It had turned sharply cold, the ponies went well; they played and raced, showing off their jewelry and best clothes and horsemanship﻿—all young people. Bay Horse smoked on a dead twig, blowing out clouds of breath.

===Chinese===
他们一伙人欢欢喜喜地骑马离开特奥·特莱凯（T’o Tlakai）——枣红马（Bay Horse）、弓之子（Bow’s Son）、从蒂斯纳波恩斯（T’ies Napornss）来的高勇（Tall Brave）两口子，还有半打别的男女，一路朝着特奥·特莱凯、特塞比泰（Tsébitai）和塞恩赛德萨（Seinsaidesah）各自回去。天气骤然冷了下来，小马们脚下走得正欢；众人一路玩耍赛马，炫耀着自己的首饰、最好的衣裳和骑术——全是年轻人。枣红马叼着一截枯枝当烟抽，一口一口喷出大团白气。

===Original===
“See my new magic! I take this twig, and it is a lighted cigarette.”

===Chinese===
“瞧我的新法术！我拿起这根小枝儿，它就是一支点着的香烟。”

===Original===
“*Ei-yei*, Grandfather; see if you can swallow all your smoke.”

===Chinese===
“咿-耶，老爷爷；看你能不能把你那口烟全吞下去。”

===Original===
They came to the foot of the slope leading to Gomulli T’o trading post.

===Chinese===
他们来到那道坡的坡脚下，坡顶上去就是戈穆利·特奥交易站。

===Original===
A man said, “Let us go buy some crackers and canned fruit.”

===Chinese===
有个汉子说：“咱们去买点饼干和水果罐头吧。”

===Original===
“It’s too cold. Some coffee would be good, I think.”

===Chinese===
“天太冷了。来点咖啡才好，我想。”

===Original===
“Maybe Yellow Mustache will give us some,” Laughing Boy said. “Why did he not come to the dance?”

===Chinese===
“兴许黄胡子会请咱们喝点儿，”笑娃说。“他怎么没来赶舞会？”

===Original===
“Yellow Mustache is not there any longer; he has gone to Chiezb’utso. The man there now is called Narrow Nose.”

===Chinese===
“黄胡子不在那儿了；他上奇兹布佐（Chiezb’utso）去了。如今在那儿的掌柜，叫窄鼻子（Narrow Nose）。”

===Original===
“What is he like?”

===Chinese===
“他是个什么样的人？”

===Original===
“He is no good. When we put things in pawn, he sells them before we can buy them back. He is small; inside himself he is small.”

===Chinese===
“他不成。我们把东西押在他那儿，还没等我们赎回来，他就给卖了。他小气；他心里头也是小的。”

===Original===
“He tries to be smart with us, but he is not good at it. His word is not good.”

===Chinese===
“他爱跟咱们耍精，可他耍不好。他的话不作数。”

===Original===
“He thinks we are fools. He ought to look at himself.”

===Chinese===
“他当我们是傻子。他该照照自己。”

===Original===
Laughing Boy broke into the chorus of information﻿—“Wait a moment!”

===Chinese===
笑娃插进这场你一言我一语——“且慢！”

===Original===
He rode over to Jesting Squaw’s Son and whispered in his ear. His friend smiled.

===Chinese===
他骑马凑到打趣婆娘之子身边，对着他耳朵咬了几句。他的朋友笑了。

===Original===
“I am thinking about coffee. I can make him give us all coffee free, I think. Who will bet?”

===Chinese===
“我正想着咖啡呢。我能叫他白白请咱们大伙喝咖啡，我想。谁来打赌？”

===Original===
“I know you,” said Bay Horse, “I won’t.”

===Chinese===
“我还不知道你，”枣红马说，“我不赌。”

===Original===
Bow’s Son whispered to Slim Girl, “He is like this. They are like this, those two, when they are together. They are not for nothing, their names.”

===Chinese===
弓之子悄悄对瘦姑娘说：“他就是这个样。那两个凑到一块儿，就是这个样。他们的名字，可不是白起的。”

===Original===
“I will bet two bits, just to make a bet,” Tall Brave said.

===Chinese===
“我押两毛五，就图押个注，”高勇说。

===Original===
A stranger offered fifty cents. Laughing Boy gave each of them his stake.

===Chinese===
一个外乡人押了五毛。笑娃把注钱一人一份递给他们。

===Original===
“Now, you all go up to the post. Go in. Do not buy, not anything. None of you know me; if anyone else is there tell him not to know me; but you all know my grandfather here. You, little sister,” he looked at his wife, “stay here.”

===Chinese===
“现在，你们全都上站里去。进店去。别买，什么都别买。你们谁也不认得我；要是店里还有别人，也叫他不认得我；只是我这位老爷爷，你们都认得。你，小妹妹，”他看了自己的女人一眼，“留在这儿。”

===Original===
They rode away while he advised with his friend. Then he explained to Slim Girl, and took her silver bridle. After a slow cigarette he said,

===Chinese===
众人策马走开，他跟朋友合计了一番。随后他向瘦姑娘交代清楚，接过她的银笼头。慢悠悠抽完一支烟，他说，

===Original===
“When that shadow reaches that stick will be time, I think. I go.”

===Chinese===
“等那道影子爬到那根棍子上，就是时辰了，我想。我去了。”

===Original===
Gomulli T’o trading post stood on a flat, bare shoulder of sand and rock, a level space of half a dozen acres, rising to the west, falling to the east. There was a corral with sides six feet high, and the L-shaped one-story house of stone and adobe with a corrugated iron roof. Around it was nothing green, nothing varied, just sand and rocks, some tin cans, part of a rotted blue shirt. There was no relief. In summer the drenching sun searched out its barren walls; in winter the wind was bleak around it. It was just something dumped there, a thing made by man, contributing nothing, in the midst of majestic desolation. Beyond its level were red-brown cliffs, dull orange bald-rock, yellow sand, leading away to blend into a kind of purplish brown with blue clouds of mountains for background. This did not belong; it was crushed and empty.

===Chinese===
戈穆利·特奥交易站立在一面平坦、光秃的沙石肩坡上，一片方圆六来英亩的平地，向西缓缓抬升，向东缓缓沉降。有一座围栏六英尺高的畜栏，还有一栋L形的单层石坯屋子，屋顶铺着瓦楞铁皮。四下里没有一点绿色，没有一点变化，只有沙子和石头，几只空铁皮罐，半件糟烂了的蓝衬衫。毫无调剂。夏天，倾泻而下的烈日把它光秃秃的墙垣搜刮个遍；冬天，寒风在它周遭刮得凄清。它不过是随手倾倒在那儿的一件东西，一件人造出来的物件，什么也添补不上，兀立在一片雄浑的荒凉正中。出了这片平地，是红褐色的峭壁、暗橙色的秃岩、黄沙，一路引开去，融成一种泛紫的褐色，背后衬着蓝色云霭般的群山。它与此地格格不入；被压得灰溜溜的，空落落的。

===Original===
Besides the ones belonging to his own party, Laughing Boy noted two other ponies hitched by the corral. He made his fast with a bowknot, the animal being rather unenterprising and not having learned how to untie them. He looped the reins over the saddle-horn and sauntered to the door of the store, trailing the bridle carelessly, and adjusting his recently acquired hat. It was a stiff-brimmed felt, with the crown undented and the string tied under the chin, Indian fashion, becoming him well. He gave it a wicked slant.

===Chinese===
除了自己一伙的马，笑娃还留意到畜栏边拴着另外两匹小马。他把自己的马打了个蝴蝶结拴牢——这牲口颇不求上进，还没学会怎么解这样的结。他把缰绳盘上鞍角，晃晃悠悠朝店门走去，手里漫不经心地拖着那副笼头，一面正了正新近置办的帽子。那是一顶硬檐毡帽，帽顶没压过凹痕，帽带照印第安人的做派系在下巴底下，衬他衬得很好。他把帽檐斜出一副促狭的角度。

===Original===
The store was a square room with a counter around three sides; in the fourth the door and a small window. Another door in the back led to the rest of the house. Now the room was rank with tobacco smoke and the heat of an iron stove. The Indians lounged along the counter, leaning on it with their elbows, talking or staring at the goods on the shelves. He recognized the owners of the two ponies﻿—Stinks Like a Mexican, an old rogue with his hair cut short to the level of his ears, who had worked for the railroad, and Long Tooth, the policeman from T’ies Napornss.

===Chinese===
店面是方方正正一间屋，三面绕着柜台；第四面是店门和一扇小窗。后头另有一道门，通向屋子其余的部分。这会儿屋里烟草味呛人，再加上铁炉子的烘热。印第安人沿着柜台懒懒散散地倚着，胳膊肘支在台面上，有的说话，有的盯着货架上的货色。他认出了那两匹小马的主人——臭得像墨西哥人（Stinks Like a Mexican），一个头发齐着耳朵剪短的老滑头，给铁路上做过工；还有长牙（Long Tooth），从蒂斯纳波恩斯来的警察。

===Original===
He stood in the door.

===Chinese===
他在门口站定。

===Original===
Bow’s Son regarded him blankly.

===Chinese===
弓之子木然地打量他。

===Original===
“Where to, tell?”

===Chinese===
“上哪儿去，说？”

===Original===
“To T’o Tlakai, for the dance.”

===Chinese===
“去特奥·特莱凯，赶舞会。”

===Original===
“The dance is over; we come from it.”

===Chinese===
“舞会已经完了；我们打那儿来。”

===Original===
“*Chiendi!*”

===Chinese===
“奇恩迪（Chiendi）！”

===Original===
“Where from, tell?” Tall Brave asked.

===Chinese===
“打哪儿来，说？”高勇问。

===Original===
“Chiziai.”

===Chinese===
“奇扎伊。”

===Original===
“That’s far!”

===Chinese===
“那可远！”

===Original===
“Yes. You tell, where do you live?”

===Chinese===
“是啊。你说说，你住在哪儿？”

===Original===
“T’ies Napornss.”

===Chinese===
“蒂斯纳波恩斯。”

===Original===
He drifted to the far end, where the trader sat, feet on the counter, chewing listlessly. The man was partly bald, with drooping, pepper-and-salt mustaches and a stupid, narrow face. He looked stingy and ignorant, not bad.

===Chinese===
他慢慢荡到柜台那头，交易商坐在那儿，两只脚翘在柜台上，没精打采地嚼着什么。这人有点秃顶，两撇花白八字胡耷拉着，一张蠢相的窄脸。看模样又抠门又无知，人倒不算坏。

===Original===
An unsuccessful dry farmer, he had bought a poor post, sight unseen, and come out to make quick money from the ignorant Indians. Somehow it didn’t work. They fooled him and exasperated him until he strove frantically to outcheat them, and that didn’t work either. He had no idea of how to attract their trade, nor of how to circumvent their sharpness. It was always like this. Two men had been there since he opened the store in the morning, making one nickel purchase, and now none of these others wanted to buy. They just wanted to talk. They thought he was running a goddamned club.

===Chinese===
他原是个种旱田种败了场的，没亲眼看过就盘下这么个寒碜的站，出来指望从无知的印第安人身上快快发财。不知怎么就是不成。他们捉弄他，气得他发疯般地想反骗他们一手，那也不成。怎么招揽他们的生意，怎么破他们的精明，他一概没数。回回都是这个样。打早上开了店门，就有两个人蹲在这儿，统共做了桩五分钱的小买卖，眼下这一大帮又没有一个想买。他们只想说话。他们只当他开的是个他妈的俱乐部。

===Original===
Laughing Boy sprawled against the counter, clicking a quarter against his teeth. His face was vacuous while he studied the ranks of tin cans. This part came natural to him. He thought idly that it was six months since he had been in a store. It was too bad Yellow Mustache was gone. Yellow Mustache would have welcomed him, and probably given him some candy.

===Chinese===
笑娃懒懒地倚住柜台，拿一枚两毛五的银币抵着牙齿磕得叮当响。他一脸空茫，眼睛却把一排排铁皮罐头打量个遍。这一手他天生就会。他心不在焉地想，自己有半年没进过铺子了。黄胡子走了，真是可惜。换了黄胡子，准会热络地招呼他，多半还会塞给他几块糖。

===Original===
“What kind of candy have you?” He spoke in the babytalk Navajo that they use with Americans.

===Chinese===
“你有什么糖？”他操着他们跟美国人打话时用的那种娃娃腔纳瓦霍话。

===Original===
“Round-soft-ones, hard-clear-ones, and brown sweets.”

===Chinese===
“圆的软糖，硬的亮糖，还有褐色的糖。”

===Original===
The man was not really at home even in the trade language. He was a little hard of hearing; it hampered him in learning.

===Chinese===
这人连交易行话都说不到家。他耳朵有点背，学话就受了绊。

===Original===
“How much are the round-soft-ones?”

===Chinese===
“圆的软糖什么价？”

===Original===
“Two for a yellow.”

===Chinese===
“黄的（五分镍币）两颗。”

===Original===
Laughing Boy examined his change carefully, and put a dime on the counter. “Give me a blue’s worth.”

===Chinese===
笑娃把自己的零钱仔细点验了一遍，把一枚一毛银币搁上柜台。“给我来一份蓝的（一毛）。”

===Original===
The trader let four gum drops roll toward the customer. “Give it to me.” He reached for the dime.

===Chinese===
交易商撒手放了四颗软糖，滚向顾客。“钱拿来。”他伸手来够那枚银币。

===Original===
Laughing Boy held onto it. “Haven’t you any twisted-sticks?”

===Chinese===
笑娃按住不放。“你们没有拧棍糖吗？”

===Original===
“No.”

===Chinese===
“没有。”

===Original===
“I don’t want those.” He put his money back in his pocket. “Give me a smoke.”

===Chinese===
“那我不要了。”他把钱收回口袋。“给我弄口烟抽。”

===Original===
Narrow Nose eyed him for a moment, as though he would like to see him shrivel. Policy was policy. He slid a half-empty sack of Stud and some papers along the counter.

===Chinese===
窄鼻子拿眼打量了他片刻，那眼神活像巴不得看他缩瘪下去。生意经终归是生意经。他把半袋开了封的“种马”牌（Stud）烟丝和几张卷烟纸顺着柜台推了过来。

===Original===
“Match, brother-in-law.”

===Chinese===
“火柴，姻兄弟。”

===Original===
“You have some.” He pointed to the Indian’s shirt pocket.

===Chinese===
“你自己有。”他指指这印第安人的衬衫口袋。

===Original===
“I need those.”

===Chinese===
“我要留着用。”

===Original===
“Well, you go to hell!” Narrow Nose swore in English with that fatuous confidence of not being understood.

===Chinese===
“哼，你下地狱去吧！”窄鼻子用英语骂道，仗着人家听不懂，一脸自作聪明的笃定。

===Original===
“*Juthla hago ni*,” Laughing Boy paraphrased softly, half as though interpreting to himself, half as though throwing it back. The insult, in Navajo, is serious. There was a laugh.

===Chinese===
“Juthla hago ni（你下地狱去吧）。”笑娃轻声换了个说法，一半像传译给自己听，一半像把话掷还回去。这句骂人的话，用纳瓦霍语说出来可是要紧的。有人笑了。

===Original===
He lolled against the counter, lit his cigarette, and puffed at it critically.

===Chinese===
他斜倚着柜台，点着烟，煞有介事地咂了几口。

===Original===
“I think I buy that saddle. Let me see it.”

===Chinese===
“我想买那副鞍子。取下来我看看。”

===Original===
“I’ll take it down if you really want it.”

===Chinese===
“你要真想买，我就给你取下来。”

===Original===
It hung from a rafter, among other saddles, quirts, bridles, pots, Pendleton blankets, ropes, silk handkerchiefs, and axes.

===Chinese===
鞍子挂在一根椽子上，夹在别的鞍子、短马鞭、笼头、锅、彭德尔顿（Pendleton）毯子、绳子、绸手绢和斧头中间。

===Original===
“Let me see it. My saddle is worn out. I need a new one. I want that blanket there, I think, and four red cans of tobacco, the kind with the preacher in the long black coat on it.”

===Chinese===
“拿来我瞧瞧。我的鞍子磨坏了。我需要一副新的。那边那条毯子我也要，我想，还有四罐红皮烟丝，要印着穿长黑外套的传道士的那种。”

===Original===
“Can you pay for all that?”

===Chinese===
“这些你都付得起？”

===Original===
“I give this in pawn.”

===Chinese===
“我拿这个作押。”

===Original===
He clanked the bridle onto the counter. Stinks Like a Mexican drew nearer.

===Chinese===
他把笼头哐啷一声撂上柜台。臭得像墨西哥人凑近了些。

===Original===
“I want that handkerchief there, I think.” He nodded toward a silk one. “And a knife that shuts.”

===Chinese===
“那边那条手绢我也要，我想。”他朝一条绸的点点头。“再来一把能折上的刀。”

===Original===
The trader got up, feigning reluctance. The way the man had made up his mind to buy was typical. He hefted the bridle﻿—ninety to a hundred dollars. Things were looking up. If he got his hooks in this, in return for thirty dollars’ worth of goods﻿—

===Chinese===
交易商站起身，装出一副老大不情愿。这人拿定主意要买货的路数，就是这么个典型样儿。他掂了掂那副笼头——值九十到一百块。行情看涨。要是能把这物件钩进手，只消找出去三十块钱的货——

===Original===
“Where do you live?”

===Chinese===
“你住哪儿？”

===Original===
“Chiziai.”

===Chinese===
“奇扎伊。”

===Original===
“Where’s that?”

===Chinese===
“那在哪儿？”

===Original===
“Down there.”

===Chinese===
“在下头那边。”

===Original===
Indians edged up to handle the silver. Narrow Nose turned to the policeman, who spoke a little English.

===Chinese===
印第安人挨挨蹭蹭凑上前来，上手摸那银子。窄鼻子转向那个会说几句英语的警察。

===Original===
“Where’s Chiziai?”

===Chinese===
“奇扎伊在哪儿？”

===Original===
“Lo Palo. Mebbe-so lailload dack side him sit down. Him come flom dere now, me sabbey.” He didn’t quite know what was up, but he wasn’t going to spoil it.

===Chinese===
“洛帕洛。没准儿铁道边上那头他住下。他这会儿打那边来，我的明白。”他不大清楚这里头唱的是哪一出，可他不想去搅了它。

===Original===
“Los Palos, hunh? I know.”

===Chinese===
“洛斯帕洛斯，嗯？我知道。”

===Original===
“I came up for the dance, now I go back. In Eagles’ Young Moon I shall come back and take out my bridle.”

===Chinese===
“我是上来赶舞会的，如今回去。到鹰雏月（Eagles’ Young Moon）我再来，把笼头赎出去。”

===Original===
That sounded good. Five﻿—six months, likely he’d forget it. Likely it wouldn’t be here.

===Chinese===
这话听着顺耳。五——六个月，多半他早忘了。多半那东西也不在这儿了。

===Original===
“Is he speaking true?” The trader asked the store in general.

===Chinese===
“他说的是真话？”交易商冲着满店的人问。

===Original===
Bow’s Son held up the bridle. “This is the kind of work they do down there. It is not like the work up here, not like my father’s jewelry,” he lied. Bay Horse and Tall Brave agreed. Narrow Nose knew them and Two Bows well. He believed them.

===Chinese===
弓之子举起笼头。“下头人做的活就是这个路数。跟上头的活不一样，跟我父亲的银活也不一样。”他扯了个谎。枣红马和高勇随声附和。窄鼻子认得他们，也认得双弓（Two Bows）。他信了。

===Original===
“Good, I take your bridle.” He reached for it, wanting to feel its possession.

===Chinese===
“好，你这笼头我收了。”他伸手去拿，巴不得摸到手里才踏实。

===Original===
“Wait a moment; put down the goods.”

===Chinese===
“且慢；先把货摆出来。”

===Original===
He assembled them laboriously. “Forty-one dollars and one blue.”

===Chinese===
他慢吞吞地把货归拢齐。“四十一块零一个蓝的。”

===Original===
“How?”

===Chinese===
“怎么讲？”

===Original===
“Saddle, twenty-seven; blanket, ten﻿—thirty-seven; tobacco, six blues; handkerchief, two dollars; knife, twelve bits; forty-one dollars and one blue. I make it forty-one dollars.”

===Chinese===
“鞍子，二十七；毯子，十——三十七；烟丝，六个蓝的；手绢，两块；刀，一块五；四十一块零一个蓝的。我给你算四十一块整。”

===Original===
Laughing Boy strung out the bargaining stubbornly, until he heard singing outside. The trader had stuck at thirty-five dollars.

===Chinese===
笑娃磨着性子，把讨价还价抻得老长，直到听见外面起了歌声。交易商在三十五块上咬死不松口。

===Original===
“Good, I take them.”

===Chinese===
“好，我买了。”

===Original===
He started to push over the bridle; Narrow Nose had his fingers on the heavy silver. Jesting Squaw’s Son and Slim Girl entered together.

===Chinese===
他正要把笼头推过去；窄鼻子的指头已经搭上那沉甸甸的银子。打趣婆娘之子和瘦姑娘一道走了进来。

===Original===
“*Ahalani!*”

===Chinese===
“阿哈拉尼！”

===Original===
“*Ahalani, shichai!*”

===Chinese===
“阿哈拉尼，老爷爷！”

===Original===
