The two men strode up to each other, Laughing Boy still clutching the harness. The trader’s hands felt empty. They hugged each other, wrestled, went through a huge pantomime of friendship.

===Chinese===
两条汉子大步迎面走近，笑娃手里还攥着那副笼头。交易商的两只手落了个空。两人又是搂抱又是角力，演足了一出老友重逢的大戏。

===Original===
“It is good to see you, my friend!”

===Chinese===
“见到你真好，我的朋友！”

===Original===
“Very much it is good to see you!”

===Chinese===
“见到你，非常地好！”

===Original===
“*Hozhoni!*”

===Chinese===
“Hozhoni!（美啊！）”

===Original===
“*Aigisi hozhoni!*”

===Chinese===
“Aigisi hozhoni!（真是美啊！）”

===Original===
“What are you doing here?”

===Chinese===
“你在这儿做什么？”

===Original===
“I came up for the dance, but I am too late, they say. What are your news?”

===Chinese===
“我上来赶舞会的，可他们说，我来得太迟了。你有什么新闻？”

===Original===
“I have just got married. This is my wife, she comes from Maito.”

===Chinese===
“我刚成了亲。这是我老婆，她是从迈托（Maito）来的。”

===Original===
“Good!”

===Chinese===
“好！”

===Original===
Narrow Nose thought he must be progressing in the language, he could understand most of what they said. Usually when they talked among themselves, he could not follow, they seemed to mess it around so. He had no idea that they were using babytalk for his special benefit, any more than it occurred to him as unusual that a man should be bringing a bride to live in his settlement, instead of going to hers.

===Chinese===
窄鼻子心想，自己的纳瓦霍话必有长进了，他们说的事他多半都听得懂了。往常他们自个儿说话，他就跟不上，他们像是把话搅来搅去的。他全然不知他们是在特意说娃娃腔给他一个人听，正如他全然不觉得，一个男人把新娘带回自己聚落来住、而不是住到新娘家去，有什么反常之处。

===Original===
Jesting Squaw’s Son shook hands with his other friends there, as though he had just come back from a trip.

===Chinese===
打趣婆娘之子又同店里别的朋友一一握手，俨然刚出一趟远门回来。

===Original===
“I have just finished building our hogan, over towards T’ies Napornss. We are going to make the House Prayer in a little while. I want you all to come now, we shall make a feast afterwards. You, my friend, you must come. Come now.”

===Chinese===
“我们的霍根屋刚盖好，在朝蒂斯纳波恩斯那边去的路上。过不多时，我们就要行屋祷（House Prayer）。我要你们这会儿都去，回头我们大摆筵席。你，我的朋友，你务必来。这就走吧。”

===Original===
He nodded at Tall Brave, who started to the door with a couple of the others.

===Chinese===
他朝高勇点了点头，高勇便带着另外两三个人朝门口走去。

===Original===
“But I am making a trade here. I must finish it.”

===Chinese===
“可我在这儿正做着一桩买卖。总得把它了结。”

===Original===
“You can make a better trade with the trader at T’ies Napornss. He is a good man.”

===Chinese===
“跟蒂斯纳波恩斯那边的交易商做买卖，更划算。他是个好人。”

===Original===
Narrow Nose swore to himself. He wanted that bridle, and he wanted that new couple’s custom. Jesting Squaw was well-to-do; she would give her son plenty of sheep.

===Chinese===
窄鼻子心里暗暗咒骂。他想要那副笼头，也想揽下这对新人的生意。打趣婆娘家境殷实，会给儿子一大群羊的。

===Original===
“I give you a good trade. Stay here and finish it.”

===Chinese===
“我给你桩好买卖。留下来把它做完。”

===Original===
“I go with my friend to feast, I think. All these people are going.”

===Chinese===
“我看，我还是跟朋友赴宴去。这些人都去了。”

===Original===
“Yes,” Tall Brave struck in from the doorway, “it is time to eat.”

===Chinese===
“是啊，”高勇从门口插嘴，“该吃饭了。”

===Original===
“Why don’t you buy food and feast here?”

===Chinese===
“干吗不买点吃的，就在这儿摆席呢？”

===Original===
“I have food there, coffee and meat and bread. Why should they buy food here?” Jesting Squaw’s Son told him.

===Chinese===
“我家里有吃的，咖啡、肉、面包都有。他们干吗要在这儿买吃的？”打趣婆娘之子回他道。

===Original===
The trader made a quick calculation, involving about a dollar and a quarter.

===Chinese===
交易商飞快打了个算盘，这笔账进出约莫一块二毛五。

===Original===
“I will give you coffee and crackers and some canned plums. How is that?”

===Chinese===
“我请你们喝咖啡、吃饼干，再添几罐李子罐头。怎么样？”

===Original===
“*Ei-yei!* Then we shall stay.”

===Chinese===
“咿-耶！那我们就留下。”

===Original===
“He must be a good man to deal with,” Laughing Boy said solemnly.

===Chinese===
“跟他做买卖，这人想必是个好人。”笑娃一本正经地说。

===Original===
Narrow Nose called through the back door,

===Chinese===
窄鼻子冲着后门喊，

===Original===
“Make about three quarts of coffee, quick, and put jest a little sweetnin’ in it. Bring out ten cups.” He set out two boxes of saltines and four cans of plums. “Now, give me the bridle.”

===Chinese===
“烧上三夸脱上下的咖啡，快点儿，里头就搁一丁点儿糖。端十个杯子出来。”他摆出两盒咸饼干、四罐李子罐头。“现在，笼头给我。”

===Original===
“I think I get something more, a rope, perhaps. You are a good man to buy from.” He laid the bridle on the counter, but hung onto the reins.

===Chinese===
“我想再添一样东西，兴许来根绳子。跟你买货，你真是个好人。”他把笼头搁上柜台，手里却攥着缰绳不放。

===Original===
Narrow Nose climbed onto the counter and pulled down a length of rope. “This is good.”

===Chinese===
窄鼻子爬上柜台，扯下一段绳子。“这根好。”

===Original===
“No, I want horsehair.” With his mouth full.

===Chinese===
“不，我要马鬃的。”他嘴里塞得满满的。

===Original===
“No horsehair.”

===Chinese===
“没有马鬃的。”

===Original===
“Rawhide, then.”

===Chinese===
“那就来生皮的。”

===Original===
He had to search under the counter for hide ropes that Indians had made. Laughing Boy went over them minutely. The coffee came. The Indians wolfed down the food and drink, and tipped up the cans to drain the fruit juice.

===Chinese===
他只好钻到柜台底下去翻找印第安人自制的皮绳。笑娃一根一根细细检看。咖啡来了。印第安人连吃带喝，风卷残云，又把罐头仰起来，沥尽里头的果汁。

===Original===
Laughing Boy said, before he had swallowed his last mouthful,

===Chinese===
最后一口还没咽下去，笑娃就开了口，

===Original===
“I do not think I want those things.”

===Chinese===
“那些东西，我看我不要了。”

===Original===
“Hunh?”

===Chinese===
“嗯？”

===Original===
Drawing at the reins, he made the bridle seem to walk off the counter.

===Chinese===
他一带缰绳，那笼头就像自个儿从柜台上一步一步走了下来。

===Original===
“Hey, stop!”

===Chinese===
“嘿，站住！”

===Original===
He turned at the door. “Another time, perhaps.”

===Chinese===
他在门口回过头。“下回再说吧，兴许。”

===Original===
“Hey, by Gawd!”

===Chinese===
“嘿，天杀的！”

===Original===
All the Indians streamed out, with the trader after them. Laughing Boy was off at a gallop, his wife and Jesting Squaw’s Son close behind. The rest followed, whooping and swinging their ropes and whips. Narrow Nose stood in the sand.

===Chinese===
印第安人一股脑涌出门去，交易商紧跟在后面。笑娃已催马奔出去，他的老婆和打趣婆娘之子紧随其后。其余的人跟了上来，一路呼喊，抡圆了绳子和鞭子。窄鼻子站在沙地里。

===Original===
“Hey!”

===Chinese===
“嘿！”

===Original===
Inside the store, Stinks Like a Mexican collected some tobacco and a handkerchief. He slid through the door, and vanished around the corner of the house.

===Chinese===
店里头，臭得像墨西哥人收拢了些烟丝和一条手绢。他侧身溜出门，绕过屋角，人就没影了。

===Original===
“God damn a red devil!”

===Chinese===
“该死的红皮魔鬼！”

===Original===
The Indians went fast; already their singing was distant. It was cold. He stuck his hands into his pockets and stared after them.

===Chinese===
印第安人跑得飞快，歌声已经远了。天冷。他把双手插进口袋，望着他们的背影直发怔。

===Original===
“God damn a red son of a bitch!”

===Chinese===
“该死的红皮狗娘养的！”

## Chapter XIV / 第十四章

### I / I

===Original===
It began to snow on the morning of the third day of their trip home, not far from Kintiel. The ground, where it had any dampness in it, had been frozen since the night before, and they had hurried under a threatening sky, having still a good day’s ride before them. The storm came down like timber-wolves, rushing. A mountaintop wind sent the dry flakes whirling past, stinging their ears and the sides of their faces; there was no sun, they could see only a few yards ahead of them. Pulling their blankets up over their heads, they guided themselves by the wind at their backs.

===Chinese===
回家行程的第三天早晨，离金提尔（Kintiel）不远，天下起雪来。地面但凡存着几分潮气的，前一夜里就冻住了；他们顶着一派凶险欲变的天空匆匆赶路，前面还有整整一天的好路程。暴风雪压将下来，像成群的林狼，奔突急袭。一股山巅之风卷着干雪片打着旋扫过，刺扎着他们的耳朵和脸颊两侧；没有太阳，往前只望得见几步远。两人把毯子拽起来罩在头上，凭着背后吹来的风辨着方向。

===Original===
An Indian takes the weather passively, accepting and enduring it without the European’s mental revolt or impatience. Comfort and fat living had changed this to some degree in Laughing Boy; he was unusually aware of discomfort, and resentful, rating the blizzard as colder than it was. Slim Girl was simply miserable. They did not speak, but jogged on, punishing their horses.

===Chinese===
印第安人对待天气是被动的：领受它，捱过它，心里没有欧洲人那种抗拒和不耐。安逸的日子、肥甘的吃喝，在笑娃身上把这性情改变了几分；他对苦楚格外知觉，心怀怨怼，把这场暴风雪估摸得比实际的更冷。瘦姑娘则干脆是苦透了。两人不说话，只顾一路颠行，狠使着他们的马。

===Original===
Time passed and the wind slackened, so that the snow about their ponies’ hooves stayed still, although the fall of flakes continued. Laughing Boy was preoccupied with thoughts of the road, but his wife contrasted this ride with the other time when they had ridden this way together. First it is the top of a stove and then it is an ice-machine, she thought; yet I am beginning to love it.

===Chinese===
时间过去，风势松了下来，小马蹄边旋起的雪落定了，虽然雪片还在下。笑娃一心惦记着路径，他妻子却把这一趟同上回两人一路同行时比较着。前一会儿是炉子面，后一会儿又成了制冰机，她想；可我竟开始爱上这儿了。

===Original===
Cliffs loomed before them, duskily blue with snowflakes rebounding and zigzagging before they touched the rock. The snow was beginning to drift.

===Chinese===
崖壁在前面隐隐现出，带着一层昏昧的蓝，雪片未及触岩便弹起，折出之字。雪开始吹积起来。

===Original===
“These are not the right cliffs,” he said; “the wind must have shifted, I think. I was afraid it would.”

===Chinese===
“这些不是原先那几道崖壁，”他说；“风向准是变了，我想。我原本就怕它变。”

===Original===
“What shall we do, then?”

===Chinese===
“那咱们怎么办？”

===Original===
“I think this is Inaiyé Cletso’i; we follow to the left.”

===Chinese===
“我想这里是伊奈耶·克莱措伊（Inaiyé Cletso’i）；咱们往左边走。”

===Original===
“Why not camp here?”

===Chinese===
“干吗不在这儿宿营？”

===Original===
“We must find firewood. We might just sleep here and not wake up. Come along, little sister, perhaps we shall find a hogan.”

===Chinese===
“咱们得找柴火。在这儿睡下去，怕就再也醒不过来了。走吧，小妹妹，兴许能寻着一座霍根屋。”

===Original===
They continued, he fully occupied, she miserable with nothing to do save follow. Sometimes the snow whirled up at them, sometimes a flaw would sting their faces with fine, white dust. Their heavy blankets felt thin as cotton over their shoulders.

===Chinese===
两人接着走，他全副心神都在路上，她一腔苦楚，除了跟随无事可做。雪有时打着旋扑上他们，有时一阵斜风挟着细白的雪粉刺他们的脸。厚重的毯子披在肩上，却薄得像棉布一般。

===Original===
“There’s a hogan.” She pushed forward.

===Chinese===
“那儿有座霍根屋。”她催马赶前。

===Original===
“*Hogay-gahn*, bad. Do not stop here!”

===Chinese===
“霍盖-冈（Hogay-gahn），不祥。别在这儿停！”

===Original===
“What do you mean?”

===Chinese===
“你这话什么意思？”

===Original===
“Don’t you see it is deserted? Don’t you see the hole in the north side? Someone has died here. Come along.”

===Chinese===
“你没瞧见这屋子是废弃了的吗？没瞧见北面墙上那个窟窿？有人死在这屋里。走吧。”

===Original===
She sighed in anger, gritted her teeth, swore under her breath, and turned her horse back. Nothing on earth would make a Navajo stop there; he would not even use the dry timbers for firewood to save his life. Well, it was part of the rest.

===Chinese===
她愤懑地叹了口气，咬紧牙关，压着嗓子咒骂了一句，拨转马头。天底下任凭什么也休想叫一个纳瓦霍人在那儿停留；就是救命，他也不会拿那些干木去生火。罢了，这也是这一切里应有的一部分。

===Original===
“We are coming somewhere now,” he called to her.

===Chinese===
“咱们这就要走到有人烟的地方了。”他向她喊道。

===Original===
“How?”

===Chinese===
“怎么说？”

===Original===
“I smell smoke. There, you can see.”

===Chinese===
“我闻见烟味了。喏，你也看得见。”

===Original===
It was a well-built hut beside a corral. Smoke issued from the hole in the roof. The dome of daubed mud and untrimmed logs looked beautiful just then. Laughing Boy shouted at the door, and a middle-aged man crawled out.

===Chinese===
那是一座修得齐整的小屋，旁边带一道畜栏，烟从屋顶的孔洞里冒出来。抹泥的穹顶、未削整的原木，这会儿看上去竟很美。笑娃朝门口喊了一声，一个中年汉子爬了出来。

===Original===
“Where are you going?”

===Chinese===
“你们上哪儿去？”

===Original===
“To Chiziai.”

===Chinese===
“去奇扎伊。”

===Original===
“You are out of the trail; it is far.”

===Chinese===
“你们偏离了大路；还远着呢。”

===Original===
“This snow confused us.”

===Chinese===
“这场雪把我们搅糊涂了。”

===Original===
“Where from?”

===Chinese===
“从哪儿来？”

===Original===
“To Tlakai.”

===Chinese===
“特莱凯。”

===Original===
“Where’s that?”

===Chinese===
“那在哪儿？”

===Original===
“Between Seinsaidesah and Agathla.”

===Chinese===
“在塞恩赛德萨和阿加斯拉之间。”

===Original===
“*Ei-yei!* You come far! Just beyond, there, is a box canyon. There is shelter and feed. Put your horses there, Grandfather. Drop your saddles here, I shall bring them in. Come in, Grandmother.”

===Chinese===
“咿-耶！你们来得可远啦！就在那边过去一点，有个箱形峡谷，能避风，也有草料。把马安置在那儿吧，老爷爷。鞍子卸在这儿，我来搬进去。进来吧，老奶奶。”

===Original===
They lost no time over the horses, and crawled gladly into the smoky, fetid, warm hogan. There were the man, two women, four children between eight and fifteen, and two dogs. The space was a circle some twelve feet in diameter﻿—the average size; with the people, the fire in the middle, saddles, cooking utensils, a loom and blankets, it was well filled.

===Chinese===
两人在马身上不敢耽搁，随即欢欢喜喜地钻进那烟腾腾、气味浊、暖烘烘的霍根屋。屋里有一个男人、两个女人、四个八岁到十五岁之间的孩子，外加两条狗。地面是一块直径约十二英尺的圆——这是寻常的尺寸；再算上当中那堆火、鞍子、炊具、一架织机和些毯子，已经填得满满当当。

===Original===
“You live at T’o Tlakai?”

===Chinese===
“你们住在特奥·特莱凯？”

===Original===
“No, at Chiziai. My parents live there. There was a Night Chant; for that we went. It was a full ten days’ chant. Mountain Singer conducted it.”

===Chinese===
“不，在奇扎伊。我父母住在那儿。那边办了一场夜颂仪，我们就是为这个去的。是一场足足十天的颂仪，山地颂唱师主持的。”

===Original===
“Beautiful!”

===Chinese===
“美啊！”

===Original===
“Yes.”

===Chinese===
“是啊。”

===Original===
The elder wife served them a pot of boiled mutton and corn, with a chunk of the usual tough wheat bread. They ate readily. It flashed through Laughing Boy’s mind that he had not enjoyed a meal so much since his arrival at Tsé Lani, but then he thought that that was silly. The foods to which he was accustomed!

===Chinese===
年长的那位妻子端给他们一锅羊肉煮玉米，还有一大块家常的那种硬麦面面包。他们吃得畅快。笑娃心头闪过一个念头：自从到了特塞拉尼，他还从没一顿饭吃得这么香过；可转念又想，这念头真傻。这些本是他吃惯了的饭食呀！

### II / II

===Original===
They were storm-bound for all the next day. He was anxious to be home again, now that the restraint of the ceremony and after-ceremony was ended. He wanted to have Slim Girl to himself, at leisure, and to enjoy their own special kind of life once more. So he was impatient and ready to find fault.

===Chinese===
转天一整天，他们都被风雪困住了。如今仪式和仪式后那些拘束既已完结，他急着要回家。他想把瘦姑娘独占下来，从容自在，再享受一回他们两个特有的那种生活。于是他等得不耐烦，动辄就要挑剔。

===Original===
It was a long time since he had been confined in a winter hogan, with its crowded things and people and close-packed smells. Their house at Los Palos was always aired. At T’o Tlakai it still had been warm enough to leave the door unblanketed during the day, and he had spent most of his time in the brush-walled medicine-lodge. He found it too close here, and was made self-conscious by fearing what she might think of it.

===Chinese===
他已经很久没有困守在过冬的霍根屋里了——物挤、人多、气味稠密。他们在洛斯帕洛斯的屋子总是透气的。在特奥·特莱凯，白天还暖和得用不着拿毯子遮门，他的时光大半都消磨在那枝条围壁的医药霍根屋里。他嫌这里太气闷，又心里发窘，生怕她看了这地方不知会怎么想。

===Original===
The modern Navajo diet, boiled mutton and tough bread, tough bread and boiled mutton, a little corn and squash, coffee with not enough sugar, tea as black as coffee, had none of the delicacy of the old ceremonial dishes. He went outside only on rising, when they all rolled in the snow (it had never occurred to him to warn Slim Girl of that custom, but she followed suit without a sign), and again for half an hour to look at his ponies. The thick air inside weighed upon him; he felt dull after a heavy breakfast, and had no more appetite.

===Chinese===
时下纳瓦霍人的吃食——煮羊肉就硬面包，硬面包就煮羊肉，一点玉米和南瓜，糖搁得不够的咖啡，黑得跟咖啡一样的茶——全没有古时仪式菜肴的那份精细。他只在起身时出去一趟，那时一家人都要在雪里打滚（他压根儿不曾想到要提醒瘦姑娘这个习俗，她却毫无异样地照做了），再有就是出去半个钟头看看他的小马。屋里浑浊的空气压在他身上；一顿早饭下肚，他便觉昏沉，再无胃口。

===Original===
Then there were the lice. His wife had rid him of them, conquering his sincere belief that they were a gift from Old Couple in the World Below to enable people to sleep. He had rated that as one of her minor magics. No new ones had got on to him at T’o Tlakai, but in this crowded place they stormed him. He was not used to being bitten, so he was tormented, and he scratched a great deal.

===Chinese===
再说还有虱子。他妻子早替他把它们除尽了，还降服了他一片虔敬的信念——虱子是下界老夫妇（Old Couple in the World Below）赐下来、好叫人睡得着觉的礼物。他一向把这一桩算作她的小法术之一。在特奥·特莱凯不曾有新的虱子上过他的身，可在这挤挤挨挨的地方，虱子朝他大举袭来。他不惯受咬，于是大受其苦，挠个不住。

===Original===
His host asked him naively, “You have many lice, Grandfather?”

===Chinese===
主人天真地问他：“老爷爷，你身上虱子多吗？”

===Original===
He caught his tongue in time, answering, “No, but they nearly froze yesterday. Now they have waked up again, and they are hungry.”

===Chinese===
他及时收住了舌头，答道：“不多，只是昨天它们险些冻死了。这会儿又都醒了，正饿着呢。”

===Original===
Slim Girl gave him a look of approval and sympathy, with a little gesture of scratching furiously at herself. He smiled.

===Chinese===
瘦姑娘朝他投来一记赞许又体恤的眼色，还悄悄做了个小手势，在自己身上狠狠挠了一把。他笑了。

===Original===
The afternoon and early evening were better, for his host recounted the second part of the Coming Up story to his children, the part about the Twin Gods, Slayer of Enemy Gods, and Child of the Waters, which Laughing Boy loved best. He noticed that Slim Girl listened intently. Some day he would be telling his children. It seemed a long time for them not to have had any, but he really did not know very much about these things. It was the woman’s business; the children were hers, after all. She would arrange it in due time, according to her wisdom. He drowsed and was soothed by the tale of the familiar, strange adventures, the gate of the Clashing Rocks, the trail over Boiling Sands, Monster Eagle and Monster Elk and Big God, lightning-arrows and cloud-blankets. After supper the close air drugged him; his eyes were nearly closed as he listened to the last of the myth.

===Chinese===
午后和傍晚初头好过一些，因为主人给他的孩子们讲起上升传说的第二部分，讲孪生神——诛敌神者与水之子（Child of the Waters）——的那部分，那正是笑娃最爱听的。他留意到瘦姑娘听得入神。有朝一日，他也要这样讲给自己的孩子们听。他们成亲这么久还没有孩子，日子似乎显得太长了些，不过这类事情他实在懂得不多。这是女人分内的事；孩子终究是她的。她自会依着她的智慧，到时候一一安排。他昏昏欲睡，被那些既熟稔又奇异的故事抚慰着——撞岩之门，沸沙之上的路，怪鹰、怪麋与大神，闪电之箭与云之毯。晚饭后，窒闷的空气像药力一样迷了他；听到神话末尾时，他的眼睛几乎阖上了。

===Original===
The snowflakes, drifting through the smoke-hole, fell into the fire with little hisses. The even voice went on, telling the end.

===Chinese===
雪片从排烟孔里飘下来，落进火中，嘶嘶地轻响。那平匀的声音还在往下讲，讲到结尾。

===Original===
“Slayer of Enemy Gods came to the Hunger People, they say﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
“诛敌神者来到了饥饿之民那里，他们说……”

===Original===
But it was not his dream, there was nothing portentous about this voice. Slim Girl had slain the Hunger People. He smiled and listened, cradled in drowsiness, distantly conscious of a louse biting him, and comforting himself with the thought that tomorrow all that would be attended to, tomorrow they would be home again. These poor people, they could not know. He half-opened his eyes, seeing his wife’s thoughtful, delicate face, and said, as sleepy people will, much louder than he realized,

===Chinese===
可这不是他的梦，这声音里没有任何凶兆。瘦姑娘早已把饥饿之民斩除净尽了。他含笑听着，困倦像摇篮一样摇着他，远远觉出有一只虱子在咬他，又宽慰自己说，明天这一切自会料理妥当，明天他们就又到家了。这些可怜的人，他们无从知晓。他半睁开眼睛，望见妻子那张若有所思的、秀致的脸，像所有犯困的人那样，说得远比他自己觉察的响亮——

===Original===
“*Hasché Lto’i!*”

===Chinese===
“Hasché Lto’i（狩猎女神）！”

===Original===
“What was that, Grandfather?” asked the man.

===Chinese===
“你说什么来着，老爷爷？”那人问。

===Original===
“Nothing.”

===Chinese===
“没什么。”

===Original===
“I thought you said something about Hunting Goddess.”

===Chinese===
“我当你说到狩猎女神的什么来着。”

===Original===
“No, I said ‘*hashké yei itei*,’ the gods are brave.”

===Chinese===
“不，我说的是‘hashké yei itei’，诸神是英勇的。”

===Original===
“Unh! That is well said.”

===Chinese===
“嗯！这话在理。”

===Original===
Slim Girl reflected. Hashché Lto’i was one of the few real goddesses, but she had nothing at all to do with the Coming Up story. He had covered his slip neatly, that man of hers. He was no child. They two would go far, far, under her direction.

===Chinese===
瘦姑娘心里寻思。Hashché Lto’i 本是寥寥可数的几位真女神之一，可她同上升传说毫不相干。他把口误遮掩得干净利落，她这个男人。他可不是个孩子了。他们两个，在她的指引之下，会走得远，远远的。

===Original===
The story-telling ended, and the flakes had ceased falling through the smoke-hole. Tomorrow would be clear. The banked fire became a dull redness, scarcely glowing.

===Chinese===
讲故事结束了，雪片也不再从排烟孔里飘落。明天会放晴。封压的火堆暗成了一团深红，几乎不吐光焰。

### III / III

===Original===
They covered the fifteen miles home at a racing pace, on a morning of clear, brilliant air and dry, fresh snow. They both felt glorious, released from the cramped hogan, glad to be approaching their goal. Though she had no great endurance, Slim Girl rode well, and now, with their ponies prancing in the cold, played tricks and frolicked on horseback as Indian men and women rarely do together. They both yipped and waved their arms; she snatched his hat, and threw it for him to pick from the ground on the run; he swung low under his pony’s neck and sent an arrow skimming ahead of them. Before they reached it, the special quality and privacy of their home reached out to them, and they were almost definitely conscious of reentering their own way of living as though one entered an enclosure.

===Chinese===
这天早晨天清气朗，空气明净，积雪干爽新鲜，他们一路竞驰，赶完了回家的十五英里。两人都觉得美妙极了——从那局促的霍根屋里放出来，又庆幸眼看就要到家。瘦姑娘耐力虽算不得出众，马却骑得好；这会儿两匹小马在寒气里昂首踏步，两人便在马背上耍起花样、嬉闹起来，这是印第安人的男女极少一道玩的。两人都尖声吆喝、挥舞胳膊；她一把抢过他的帽子扔出去，叫他跑着马从地上拾起；他侧身低荡到小马颈子底下，一箭嗖地掠出，送到他们前头去。还没赶到那支箭跟前，他们这个家独有的气韵与幽静已经迎面伸来，两人几乎分明意识到，自己正重新踏进自家那种过活里去，仿佛跨进一座围场。

===Original===
He admired anew the fireplace, with its smokelessness, its draft that set the flame quickly blazing, the heat thrown out from its shallow back. She prepared food while he tended to the ponies. The house became warm, but the air was sweet, the adobe walls and clay floor were clean, and now, with lively appetites, they smelt the good food cooking. She sat back from the fire while things stewed and bubbled. Kneeling beside her, he kissed her﻿—to him perhaps more than anything else the act symbolic of their life apart﻿—and they smiled at each other with grave pleasure. For a minute she was limp in his arms, then she pushed him away with an affectionate, scolding word and returned to a pot that was boiling over. He lay back on the sheepskins, watching. Domesticity, his wife, his home, perfection.

===Chinese===
他再度赞叹起那座火炉——不冒烟，风道一畅，火苗便迅速蹿旺，热力从浅浅的炉膛后壁发散出来。她备饭，他照料小马。屋子暖了起来，空气却是甜净的，土坯的墙、泥墁的地都干净；这时两人胃口正好，闻着饭食烹煮的香味。锅里的东西咕嘟咕嘟炖着滚着，她从火边退坐下来。他跪到她身旁，吻了她——在他心里，这一吻或许比什么都更是他们那离群生活的象征——两人带着庄重的愉悦相视而笑。有一分钟，她软软地瘫在他怀里，随后把他推开，嗔了一句亲昵的骂，回到那口正沸溢的锅边。他仰躺在羊皮上望着。居家的日子，他的妻子，他的家，圆满至极。

===Original===
The loom-frame hung near the door; on the other side was the anvil. The place was permeated with an excited happiness, fullness, completion. Had any religious-minded Navajo, sensitive enough to the reiterated doctrine of *hozoji* to feel it without words, entered that place, he would have felt that he had, indeed, entered the “house of happiness.”

===Chinese===
织机的木架挂在门边；另一侧是铁砧。这地方浸透着一种兴冲冲的幸福、充盈与圆满。倘若有哪个虔敬的纳瓦霍人，对那反复申说的“霍卓吉”义理敏感到不必借言辞便能觉出，走进这地方，他准会觉得，自己当真走进了一座“幸福之屋”。

## Chapter XV / 第十五章

### I / I

===Original===
The winter passed as swiftly as the summer; more so, in fact, for, feeling more sure of herself, Slim Girl consented to a social life. They went to various dances, becoming better known among the Southern Navajos, who began to accept her as entirely one of themselves. Learning with practice better and better how to avoid being different in a way that troubled others, she was able to be one of them without the fatal appearance of reserve and effort. By a slow process, she saw, Laughing Boy really was bringing her back into her own people. She consented out of policy to undergo the Night Chant initiation, the scourging with yucca leaves, the demonstration of the masks, and having done so found, surprised at her own naivete, that it was a genuine source of satisfaction to her. Knowing that something of the true substance was forever lost to her, she surrounded herself as much as possible with the trappings of Navajo-ism.

===Chinese===
冬天过得和夏天一样快；其实还更快些，因为瘦姑娘自觉心里有了底，松口应承起社交往来。他们去各处赴舞会，在南部的纳瓦霍人当中渐渐有了名声，人们开始把她完完全全当自己人看待。她随着一回回历练，越来越懂得怎样把自己的与众不同藏得不叫旁人心里犯堵，能够做他们中的一员，而不带那股致命的矜持和吃力相。她看得分明，凭着一个缓慢的过程，笑娃当真是在把她带回她自己的人中间来。出于权宜，她同意去受夜颂仪的入仪礼——受丝兰叶的鞭笞，观面具的示演——受完之后却发现，这竟是她一桩实实在在的满足，倒惊异起自己的天真来。她知道真髓里的东西已有几分永远与她无缘，便尽量拿纳瓦霍之道的种种外在排场，把自己围裹了起来。

===Original===
There were obstacles and interruptions: a double life carries heavy enough penalties, and a past is a past, particularly if its locale is much the same as that of the present. Red Man, the wrestler of Tsé Lani, sophisticated and self-willed, was present at many of those dances. Slim Girl had never given him more than hope, and even that, he felt, more because he served a purpose than for anything else. She had used him. Now she belonged to this rustic, who had humiliated him, and who obviously did not know what it was all about. Red Man was too good an Indian to bear much resentment for the wrestling defeat, but it served the purpose of preventing him from amusing himself by explaining to Laughing Boy just what he knew about his wife. Besides, he shrewdly suspected, such a recital would be dangerous in the extreme.

===Chinese===
障碍和搅扰也是有的：双重的日子本就代价够重，何况旧账终归是旧账，尤其当它上演的地方，同眼下这一出相去不远。红汉子——特塞拉尼那个摔跤的——见过世面，又自行其是惯了，那些舞会上好些场都有他。瘦姑娘给过他的从来不过是盼头；就连这点盼头，他自己也觉出来，多半因为他有用处，而不是别的什么。她是利用过他的。如今她归了这么个乡巴佬——当众羞辱过他、又明摆着不知内情的一个。红汉子是个够格的印第安人，不至于为摔跤那场败绩记多少仇，只是那笔账恰好起了一个作用：堵住了他的兴致，教他歇了那份心思，不去把他所知道的、关于笑娃婆娘的种种细数给笑娃听，拿这个取乐。再说，他精明地疑心，这样一席细数，是凶险到极处的事。

===Original===
So he adopted an attitude of smiling implications, of “I could and I would,” that was as effective as possible for making trouble. Laughing Boy remembered the dancing at Tsé Lani, and he felt disturbed. Watching Red Man, it came upon him that, remarkable though his wife was, she was subject to the same general laws as other people, and he was fairly sure that he was not the first man she had known in love. Many things suddenly aligned themselves in a new way to assume a monstrous form. He became very quiet, and thought hard.

===Chinese===
于是他摆出一副笑里藏话的架势，一副“我行，我也乐意”的神气——招惹是非，再没有比这更灵验的了。笑娃记起特塞拉尼那几场舞，心里乱了。他望着红汉子，忽然明白过来：婆娘再出奇，也逃不脱众人同受的那些通则；而且他相当拿得准，自己并不是头一个跟她相好过的男人。许多事情忽然换了个新排法凑到一处，显出一副狰狞的形状。他变得非常沉默，苦苦地想。

===Original===
Slim Girl saw it immediately, not knowing what he was thinking, but feeling the reality of her peril. At that dance, she paid no attention to it, continued as ever, and treated Red Man with cool friendliness. At home, she managed to bring him into the talk, told Laughing Boy how he had sought to marry her once, and described with entire truth an ugly scene with him at Tsé Lani. Her husband listened, and was gladly convinced.

===Chinese===
瘦姑娘立刻看了出来，不知他想的是什么，却觉出自己那份凶险是实打实的。那场舞会上，她不露一点口风，一切照旧，待红汉子只一副淡淡的亲热。到了家里，她设法把这个人引进话头，告诉笑娃他从前如何求过亲，又原原本本、句句是真地讲了他在特塞拉尼同她闹的那一场难堪。丈夫听着，乐得信了。

===Original===
Her past was her past, he thought; he knew enough of her to know that it had been more than unhappy, and that she had put it resolutely behind her. There was much suffering, many bad things, of which she never spoke. Some day, perhaps, she could tell him. In any case, he believed what she did say, and even had the case been otherwise, that was all dead.

===Chinese===
她的过去就是她的过去，他想；他了解她够多，知道那段日子不止是不幸，也知道她已经毅然把它甩在了身后。有许多苦楚，许多坏事情，她从来不提。总有一天，也许，她能说给他听。无论如何，她说出口的他都信；就算事情不是这样，那一切也都死了。

===Original===
The next time they met, he contemplated the man, and guessed at the dimensions of his soul. Taking an opportunity when they both were taking horses to water, he rode up beside him, sitting sideways on his barebacked pony, one hand on the mane, one hand on the rump﻿—a casual pose for a careless chat. Red Man greeted him noncommittally.

===Chinese===
再见面时，他把这人细细打量，揣度他魂魄的斤两。趁两人都在饮马的当口，他骑马凑到那人身边，侧身坐在自己那匹光背的小马上，一只手搭着鬃毛，一只手按着马臀——一副说闲话的散漫姿势。红汉子不置可否地招呼了他。

===Original===
“Grandfather, let us not run around things, let us not pretend,” he said. “You have not said anything, but you have said too much. Do not pretend not to know what I mean. If you like what you are doing so much that you are willing to fight about it, go on. If not, stop it. I say, not just do less of it, or do it differently, but stop it entirely. That is what I mean. I have spoken.”

===Chinese===
“老爷爷，咱们别绕着事情转，也别装假，”他说。“你什么也没有说，可你说得太多了。别装不懂我的意思。你要是中意自己干的事，中意到肯为它干一仗，那就接着干。不中意，就住手。我说的是住手——不是少干些，也不是换个干法，而是整个儿住手。我就是这个意思。话说完了。”

===Original===
Red Man studied him; he was plainly in deadly earnest. He might just as well have acted instead of spoken﻿—those men from up there have not yet realized the power of police and law. Among Navajos, the reasonable and acceptable way to have done, had he acted, would have been from ambush. Red Man felt he had had a narrow escape. He emphatically did not like what he was doing that much. Time would inevitably bring sorrow to the fellow.

===Chinese===
红汉子端详他；他分明是动了真格的。这人满可以不说而做——从上头那一面来的人，还没认清警察和法律的分量。在纳瓦霍人中间，他若真要动手，说得过去也叫人服气的做法，本该是打埋伏。红汉子觉得自己险险躲过了一场。他实在并没有那么中意自己干的那档子事。时光早晚免不了教那家伙伤心。

===Original===
“I hear you, Grandfather.”

===Chinese===
“我听见了，老爷爷。”

### II / II

===Original===
These occasional absences of from three days to over a week made complications in Slim Girl’s arrangements with her American. His trips in to town from his ranch were made on business that was, as often as not, conjured up to excuse himself to himself for seeing her. Each rendezvous would be arranged the time before, or by a note left in the little house, which she was supposed to visit at fixed intervals. Now it was occurring, as never before, that he would demand her presence on a certain date, only to be told it was impossible. Increasingly, as her love for her husband gained upon her, he suspected part of the truth, and tormented himself with jealousy. That husband, whom he had always regarded as rather mythical, seemed in the past few months to have become exacting. In moments of honesty towards himself, he writhed at the acid thought of being used by a squaw for the benefit of herself and some low, presumably drunken, Indian.

===Chinese===
这种三天到一周还多的偶尔外出，把瘦姑娘同她那美国人的安排搅出了麻烦。他从牧场进城，回回说是办事；那些事多半还是现编的，为的是叫他对自己的良心有个交代，才好去看她。每回相会，或是上一次先约下，或是留张条子在小白屋里——按规矩她该隔一阵去那屋看一趟。如今竟有了前所未有的事：他定下日子要她来，得到的答复却是不行。她对丈夫的爱一日深似一日，他也越发疑到了真情的一角，拿醋意煎熬自己。那个丈夫，他一向只当是个半虚半实的人物，这几个月里仿佛变得苛求起来。在肯对自己说实话的当口，他想到自己被一个婆娘拿来利用，去成全她自己和某个下等的、多半还醉醺醺的印第安人，这念头酸得他浑身发拧。

===Original===
He rode into Los Palos through the bottomless mud and wet of a spring thaw, only to find a note on the table:

===Chinese===
他骑马进洛斯帕洛斯，一路蹚着春日解冻深不见底的泥泞和水湿，到头来只在桌上见到一张条子：

===Original===
Dear George

My husband make me go too dance I will come day after tomorrow afternoon, pleas not mind.

love

						**Lillian**

===Chinese===
亲爱的乔治

老公叫我去看跳舞我后天下午来请不介意。

爱你的

						莉莉

===Original===
The poor fool cursed, got drunk, and waited over.

===Chinese===
这可怜的傻子咒骂了一通，喝得大醉，索性住下不走，干等着。

===Original===
That had been a very pleasant dance; they had ridden part of the way home with as likable a crowd as the one that rode from T’o Tlakai to the trading post. She still tasted the flavour of it as she changed into her Sears-Roebuck dress and set out for Los Palos. Laughing Boy had surprised on her face, once or twice, that look of triumphant hatred when she returned. He would have been astonished could he have seen her now.

===Chinese===
那是一场很痛快的舞会；回家路上有一程，他们是同一伙招人喜欢的伴当同骑的，就像当年从特奥·特莱凯去交易站的那一伙。此刻她换上那身西尔斯·罗巴克的裙子，动身去洛斯帕洛斯，嘴里还品着那场舞的滋味。她回来的时候，笑娃有一两回撞见过她脸上那种得胜的恨意。他若是能看见她此刻这副样子，准会大吃一惊。

===Original===
She looked back on their house, on the corral and the still leafless young peach trees, visualizing the dance, her people, and him. Her face was tender, almost yearning. Then she turned away towards the town, and braced her shoulders. For a moment she smiled, a warpath smile, and she was hard. Her upper lip curled back, showing her small, even, white teeth. Then her expression was blank; that passive look upon her oval face that made one turn to it again and again, wondering what deep, strong thoughts were going on behind the lovely mask.

===Chinese===
她回头望他们的屋子，望栏圈，望那些还没抽叶的小桃树，心里映着那场舞、她的人、还有他。她的脸温柔，几乎带着渴念。随后她转身朝镇子去，把肩膀一挺。有一刻她笑了，一副出征路上的笑，她硬了起来。上嘴唇朝后翻起，露出细小齐整的白牙。随后她的神情便空了；鹅蛋脸上一副被动的木然，教人一遍又一遍回头去看，纳闷那可爱的面具底下，正进行着什么样深沉而有力的心思。

===Original===
He was in the house before her. She braced herself again at the door, then blotted everything from her eyes, becoming a happy, pretty woman with nothing on her mind. He rose as she entered. He did not answer her smile or move to touch her; that meant there would be a scene. Oh, well!

===Chinese===
他比她先到了屋里。她在门口又把自己撑了一把，随即把一切都从眼里抹掉，变成一个心里不装事的快活标致女人。她进门时他站起身来。他不回应她的笑，也不动身上前碰她；这就是说，要有一场风波了。唉，也罢！

===Original===
“Look here, Lillian, this is getting too thick. Here I come in here just to see you﻿—we made the date, didn’t we?﻿—and you’ve gone prancing off to some dance. It won’t do. I don’t ask so much of you, but you’ve got to keep your dates, do you see? Don’t make me suspect you﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
“你听着，莉莉，这也太不像话了。我大老远进来，就为看你一眼——日子是咱们定的，是不是？——你倒颠儿颠儿跑去看什么舞会了。这不行。我要求你的不算多，可你定了的日子就得守，明白吗？别招得我疑心你……”

===Original===
She hated scenes, loud voices, turmoil, protestations. God damn this man. *Juthla hago hode shonh.* She sat still, looking at him with wide, hurt eyes and drooping mouth. By and by he ran down.

===Chinese===
她恨风波，恨大嗓门，恨乱糟糟，恨一迭声的表白。这个男人，遭天谴的。Juthla hago hode shonh（叫这人下地狱去吧）。她一动不动地坐着，睁大一双受了委屈的眼睛望着他，嘴往下耷拉着。渐渐地，他泄了劲。

===Original===
“You tink lak dat about me! You tink I forget everyting! What for you tink dose tings, hey? I’m sorry I go away. I do it because I got to, you see? My husban’, he tink someting bad, I tink. So he act mean, dat man. But you know.”

===Chinese===
“你拿这种话想我！你当我把什么都忘掉了！你干吗想这些个事，嗯？我走开，我心里也难过。我是不得不走，你懂？我老公，他起了坏心思，我想。所以他使坏，那个人。可你是知道的。”

===Original===
“The trouble is I don’t know. I wonder about you. I wonder if you try at all, or just do what’s handiest for you. I’ve got some consideration coming to me, you know.”

===Chinese===
“麻烦就在于我不知道。我拿不准你这个人。我拿不准你到底是肯上心，还是只拣最省事的做。我总该得着几分体谅，你是知道的。”

===Original===
The man was truly jealous, he was miserable, she had him right in the palm of her hand. She didn’t have to say much, just let him do it. After he’d got rid of all this, the fact remained that he loved her, and that was all that mattered.

===Chinese===
这男人是真吃醋，他难受，而她把他整个儿攥在掌心里。她用不着多说，只消由他去发作。等他把这一腔倒完，剩下的事实还是：他爱她——那才是顶要紧的。

===Original===
He drew her towards him, she sat on his knees, her hands on his shoulders. He bent her face back and stared into her eyes. They were deep, deep, and swimming. There was a look in them that thrilled him, a look that must be true. Now there was an imprint of real truth in her words and gestures, and the fierceness of her kiss.

===Chinese===
他把她拉向自己，她坐在他膝上，双手搭着他的肩。他把她的脸往后一仰，盯进她的眼睛。那双眼深得很，深得很，水汪汪地漾着。里头有一种叫他心颤的神色，一种必定做不得假的神色。这一刻，她的话语和姿态里都盖着真的印记，还有她那个吻的狠劲。

===Original===
She was not acting any longer, she did not have to pretend this. There was no more falseness in it than there is in an arrow leaving a bow. She hated him. On him she had concentrated all her feelings towards Americans in general, everything that she had ever suffered. In him she was revenging herself upon them all. Her kisses were weapons, her tendernesses were blows struck in the full heat of battle. She was revenging herself, and she was acquiring the means to her perfect life.

===Chinese===
她不再是演了；这样的情致她不必装。里头的假，不比一支离弦的箭更多。她恨他。她把对美国人的全部心绪、把此生受过的一切苦，都集注到他一个人身上。她在他身上，向他们全体复仇。她的吻是兵器，她的温存是鏖战正酣时挥出的打击。她报着仇，同时也在攒她那份圆满日子的本钱。

===Original===
Bound by those hours of happiness, he could not break away. These days, he gave her more money than he ever had before, more than he could well afford, seeking to bind her to him, knowing that that was no way to arrive at truth, but craving, if she were lying to him, to be lied to so well he would be convinced. He made many efforts to improve her, feeling how remarkable a woman she was. He wanted her to read books, but her distaste for them was deep and sincere; he wanted to make a superior American out of her. He would have liked to raise her to a position in which he could respect himself if he married her.

===Chinese===
他被那些欢乐的时辰缚住，脱不开身。这些日子，他给她的钱比以往任何时候都多，多到超出他从容负担的地步，想以此把她拴在自己身上；他明知这不是通向真情的一路，却又渴求着——她若在骗他，就求她骗得高明，高明到叫他深信不疑。他费了许多心思造就她、提点她，觉出她是怎样一个非凡的女人。他要她读书，可她对书本的厌恶又深又真；他想把她造就成一个上等的美国人。他巴不得把她抬到一个位分上，好教他若娶了她，自己也能敬重自己。

===Original===
She was afraid always that he would ask her that, but he was not quite so lost. She kept him at tension, administering happiness and unhappiness carefully, accepted his increased gifts, and in her mind shortened the time of waiting. But it did not make a smooth road to travel.

===Chinese===
她无时不怕他开口提那件事，好在他还没有昏到那步。她把他吊在紧张里，仔细地配给着欢喜与不欢喜，收下他加了码的馈赠，心里把等待的期限一缩再缩。可这条路，走起来并不平顺。

### III / III

===Original===
She was beset by difficulties and entanglements, but she was conquering them one by one, even turning them to her use, as though she were taking weapons from her enemies. She could shape and bend men, she could control her destinies in theirs. In her thoughts, she tasted in advance the happiness of the goal towards which she aimed, and she felt her power, power, power; and so, as far as she could tell, she was happy.

===Chinese===
她被难题和纠缠团团围住，可她正把它们一一降服，甚至转来为己所用，仿佛从敌人手里缴械。她能拿捏男人、折弯男人，能在他们的命数里掌住自己的命数。心思里，她预先尝到了那个目标尽头的欢喜，她觉出自己的力量，力量，力量；于是，就她自己能判断的而言，她是快活的。

===Original===
Her weaving was winning Laughing Boy’s unstinted praise, and, to her surprise and great satisfaction, becoming a source of income. The trading post in Los Palos would pass on to her occasional orders from tourists or people in the East. If she had been willing to weave the entirely un-Indian pictures of actual objects that so many tourists demand, she could have had all the work she could handle at fancy prices; but she refused to do anything, or use any colours not purely Navajo, and she strengthened her husband in his natural reluctance to stamp shapeless strings of swastikas, thunderbirds, and other curiosities on his silver. She was precious about it, as she was about all Navajo things. It was one piece with her eagerness to speak familiarly of everything familiar to them, to participate in every phase of their life, to acquire completely the Navajo gesture. When they were rich and lived in the North, she thought, she would make herself an influence for preserving all native ways; she would use any power they acquired in combating Christianity, short hair, shoes, ready-made trousers, and the creeping in of American-derived words. Already she had amused her husband by insisting on calling coffee by its old, cumbrous name of little-split-round-ones, instead of the much easier *coghwé*, that had been taken into the language.

===Chinese===
她的织工赢得笑娃毫无保留的夸赞，而且——叫她吃惊、又叫她大大满足——成了一项进项。洛斯帕洛斯的交易站不时把游客或东部人的零星订活转给她。她要是肯织那么多游客要的、全然不印第安的实物图样，本可以把活计接个没完，价钱还开得高高的；可她什么都不肯做、什么颜色都不肯用，除非是纯之又纯的纳瓦霍货色，而且她丈夫天生不愿往银器上砸那些不成形的成串卍字、雷鸟和别的新奇图样，她还在一旁加固了他这份不愿。她在这上头看得金贵，一如她待一切纳瓦霍事物。这同她那股热切原是一体：凡他们熟悉的东西，她都要说得像自家人一样熟；他们生活的每一面，她都要有份；那纳瓦霍的举手投足，她要完完整整学到手。等他们富了、住到北边去，她想，她要让自己成一股势力，专为保存一切本土的旧道；他们挣下的任何势力，她都要拿去斗基督教，斗剪短的头发，斗鞋子，斗现成的长裤，斗那些从美国话里渗进来的字眼。她已经拿一桩事逗乐过丈夫：她坚持管咖啡叫它那个又老又累赘的旧名——小圆裂粒，而不肯用容易上口得多、已经进了话里来的 coghwé。

===Original===
Laughing Boy’s reputation was spreading. The Harvey agent had made her a tentative offer for them to come to Grand Canyon. In the beginning of spring, at planting time, they moved forge and loom outdoors again. At sunset, laying aside tools, or coming in, tired and at peace, from working in the soft earth, they sang together.

===Chinese===
笑娃的名声传开了。哈维公司的采办试探着提过一回，请他们上大峡谷去。开春，到了下种的时节，他们又把锻炉和织机搬到屋外。日落时分，放下家什，或是从松软的泥土里做完工、又乏又安宁地进屋来，两人便一起唱歌。

===Original===
Now was the time when ponies began to grow fat, and the desert was all one mass of flowers. Remembering a good thing from her school days, Slim Girl brought in great bunches, Indian paint brush, fireweed, cactus blooms, and a hundred others, and stuck them in tin cans about the house. This puzzled Laughing Boy at first, but later he caught on, and enjoyed grouping them, with a good feeling for arrangements of masses of colour, but little interest in the blossoms as such.

===Chinese===
这时节，小马们开始上膘，荒漠开成了一整片花。瘦姑娘记起学堂年月里学来的一桩好东西，大把大把采了花进来——印第安画笔花、柳兰花、仙人掌花，还有上百样别的——插在铁皮罐头盒里，屋里四处摆开。起初笑娃让这弄得莫名其妙，后来开了窍，喜欢上摆布这些花：大块大块的色彩怎么安置，他很有感觉，对花朵本身倒没多大兴致。

===Original===
There was movement in the desert. Horse-trading picked up again. The first sprouts of corn came through the ground, the peach trees began to put out leaves; one of them triumphantly produced a blossom. The days slipped by. Life was settled, serene, monotonous; there was no detail of it that one would wish to change.

===Chinese===
荒漠里有了动静。贩马的买卖又兴旺起来。玉米头一茬小苗钻出地皮，桃树开始抽叶；其中一棵得意洋洋地开出一朵花来。日子一天天溜过去。生活安顿、宁静、单调；里头没有一个细节，是人会想改动的。

## Chapter XVI / 第十六章

### I / I

===Original===
Any married couple, no matter how perfect the match, will undergo a critical period of strain, and these two were no exception. For all the dances, winter was a hemmed-in time; repetitious days indoors were a searching test of companionship. Slim Girl went into town, Laughing Boy sallied forth to watch over the herd; but they moved out of the home atmosphere together only for those eight or nine ceremonies.

===Chinese===
任何一对成了亲的夫妻，无论般配得多么完美，都要经历一段吃紧的关口，这两个人也不例外。舞会尽管多，冬天终究是个困守的时节；屋里那些千篇一律的日子，是对两人相处功夫的严苛检验。瘦姑娘进城去，笑娃出门照看畜群；可两人一同走出这个家的氛围，只有那八九回仪式。

===Original===
They were attempting a difficult thing. They needed not only to see occasional outsiders when they were apart, new faces made attractive by the mere fact that they break the sameness, but also the presence of a third person when they were together, that their solitude might retain its value, and their unity refresh itself from the sense of the outsider’s foreignness.

===Chinese===
他们是在尝试一桩难事。他们不但需要在分开时偶尔见见外人——新面孔单单因为打破了单调，便显得动人——还需要在一起时有第三个人在场，好让两人的独处保得住它的价值，让两人的相依借着外人那份异质感重新焕发。

===Original===
This same life, so closely together, will make people unusually sensitive to each other’s moods; sometimes, if they are fond of each other, almost morbidly so. He did not answer that question; perhaps he thinks it was stupid. She handed me that cup of coffee abruptly; perhaps I have offended her in something.

===Chinese===
这样紧紧厮守的日子，会让人对彼此的情绪变得异常敏感；有时，倘若两人恩爱，甚至会敏感到近乎病态。他没有回答那个问题；也许他觉得那问题蠢吧。她方才把那杯咖啡递给我递得那么急；也许我在什么事上招了她。

===Original===
They came through it remarkably well, and still deeply in love. But Slim Girl, watching her husband with close attention, felt him change and was troubled. Feeling less sure of herself, she was overcareful, and betrayed more than ever that reserve of something withheld that belonged inevitably to her double life. Each increased the other’s uneasiness; it was a circle.

===Chinese===
他们相当出色地熬过了这一关，依旧深深地相爱。可是瘦姑娘细细留意着丈夫，觉出他在变，心里烦乱。她自己没了把握，行事便过分小心，也就比以往任何时候都更露出了那股有所保留的藏掖——那是她的双重生活必然带在身上的东西。两人各自加重了对方的不安；这成了一个循环。

===Original===
He did not read himself. The melting snows refreshed the pasturage, the grass grew tall. He gathered his scattered horses, shifted them, and watched them fatten. His peach trees grew, his corn was well above the sand. All these were good things, and in each he rejoiced as he enjoyed each detail of his day, the far riding and the loom before the house door, his wife’s talk, the ring of his hammer. Each thing was good, and yet the whole was dull and devoid of savour.

===Chinese===
他读不懂自己。融雪滋润了牧场，草长得高高的。他把散在各处的马收拢起来，转移了地方，看着它们上膘。他的桃树长起来了，玉米早已高高出过沙地。这些都是好事，他也为每一件欢喜，一如他享用着一天里的每个细节：远出的骑行，家门前的那架织机，妻子的谈笑，铁锤的叮当。每一件都是好的，然而整个儿加在一起却沉闷寡味，没了滋味。

===Original===
Laughing Boy knew well enough that people wore on each other, and that every couple underwent a period of adjustment. He knew that in many households, when the man became seriously restless, his first wife would arrange for him to take a second, to preserve the home. But such was hardly the case here. He was by habit one who faced issues squarely and thought them out tough-mindedly, but now in the back of his head were many thoughts, safely hidden from himself, from which unease, like an infection, flowed through his system.

===Chinese===
笑娃很明白人是会彼此消磨的，也明白每一对夫妻都要经过一段磨合。他知道在许多人家，男人当真烦躁难安起来时，大老婆会张罗着替他再娶一个，好保住这个家。可这里压根谈不上那种情形。他素来惯于直面难题，硬起心肠把事情想透，如今他后脑勺里却压着许多念头，稳稳当当瞒过了他自己的眼睛；不安便像一场感染，从那里渗出来，流遍他的周身。

===Original===
He did not realize that he was studying his wife critically, as one might an opponent. Once or twice, to his own surprise, he caught himself about to become annoyed with her over little or nothing; once or twice, away from her, building up a quite unreasonable sense of wrong. Then he would be disgusted with himself, and alarmed. The process was really natural enough; being profoundly dissatisfied with something in her which he refused to recognize, the feeling sought to give itself outlet by picking causes of annoyance which could be admitted.

===Chinese===
他没有意识到，自己正带着挑剔审视妻子，像审视一个对手似的。有一两回，他吃惊地发觉自己眼看要为她动气——为些鸡毛蒜皮，甚至无缘无故；有一两回，离开她时，心里又攒起一股全然无理的委屈。过后他便厌恶自己，又感到惊慌。这个过程其实再自然不过：他对她身上的某样东西深为不满，却拒绝认它，那情绪便要给自己寻个出口，专挑那些说得出口的懊恼事由下手。

===Original===
She had always foreseen a period of difficulty and settling down, and was prepared to adapt herself to it, but now she did not know what was needed. She thought she was sophisticated, she thought she knew all about men, and all about herself. She thought she had penetrated to the ultimate truth. She knew only a little of life, not all of herself, and of men there was a half which she knew through and through, and a half which she was just beginning to discover. She wondered if the time had come at last to give up her American and go North. But this was a bad year for them; wool, and hence the sheep which they would have to buy, had risen, while horses, blankets, and jewelry sold badly. The tourists were unusually few. And here she had her one sure source of income.

===Chinese===
她一向预见到会有一个艰难与安顿的时期，也预备着去适应它，可如今她不知道究竟需要什么。她以为自己老于世故，以为自己把男人摸了个透，也把自己摸了个透。她以为已经参透了终极的真理。其实她对人生只识得一点点，对自己也不尽识得；至于男人，有一半她了如指掌，另一半她才刚刚开始发现。她寻思，丢开她那个美国佬、往北去的时辰，是不是终于到了。可今年对他们是个坏年头；羊毛涨了价，他们要买的羊跟着也贵了，马匹、毯子和银饰却又卖不动。游客少得出奇。而在这里，她有她唯一稳当的进项。

===Original===
Then she had a fatal thought. She was learning, from herself and from Laughing Boy, how much more there is to love than what is covered by its lowest terms. She was thinking things out by herself﻿—particularly when she was weaving﻿—like a philosopher. With the realization of the other things that are needed to make love worthy of itself, the bare fact that her husband and herself were in love with each other ceased to be sufficient. She wondered if, by falling in love when she had thought to make a deliberate choice, she had really known what she was doing. She wondered if life with this man, who was sometimes silent and strange, sometimes stupid, and sometimes irritating, might not be dismal in that wild homeland of his.

===Chinese===
接着她起了一个致命的念头。她正从自己身上、从笑娃身上学到：爱情比起约到最简的名目所涵盖的，还要多得多。她独自把事情想个透——尤其是在织布的时候——像个哲学家似的。一旦认识到还需要别的东西，爱情才配得上它自己，那么丈夫和她彼此相爱这个光秃秃的事实，便不再够用了。她怀疑，自己原本打算的是细细拣选，却一头坠入了情网，那时可真的明白自己在做什么？她怀疑，同这个男人一起过日子——他有时沉默而古怪，有时愚钝，有时惹人恼——在他那片荒野的故乡，会不会终究是灰暗凄惶的。

===Original===
She did not really believe in her own doubt; it was purely an intellectual concept; but the dominant motive in her life for so many years had been the determination to move coolly towards a predetermined, sure success. Had she studied Napoleon in that California school, she would have admired him, and she might have been warned by him. Now, looking back on her past triumphs, she decided to wait until she made surely sure. Just a few months more, a year at the most, and George was making a lot of money in sheep. Some of that would come in handy.

===Chinese===
她并不真信自己的疑心；那纯粹是个脑子里的概念；可多年来主宰她一生的原动力，一直是那个决心——冷静地朝着一个预先定好的、万全的成功走去。假若她在那所加利福尼亚学校里研读过拿破仑，她会钦佩他，也说不定会得到他的警戒。此刻，回顾着自己过往的一连串凯旋，她决定等到十拿十稳再说。再等几个月，至多一年，而乔治正靠羊挣着大钱。那里头总有些能派上用场。

### II / II

===Original===
As summer approached, Laughing Boy became restless and more worried at his own condition. Had he offended a god, he wondered. He took a sweat-bath, sang, and tried a fast. It did not seem to make much difference.

===Chinese===
夏天将近，笑娃烦躁起来，对自己的光景愈发忧心。他寻思，自己是不是触犯了哪位神明。他做了汗浴，唱了歌，还试了斋戒。似乎都没什么起色。

===Original===
He made up his mind one morning when he was leaving to round up three ponies for sale. Slim Girl had seemed abstracted; he had noticed her watching him curiously, seeming nervous. She had been like that various times lately, yet what could he say about it? It was just an impression. He felt sullen, snapped at her. Her hurt surprise made him miserable. As he mounted his horse, he thought, I must surely find a singer.

===Chinese===
一天早晨，他出门去圈三匹要卖的小马，就在这时拿定了主意。瘦姑娘显得心不在焉；他留意到她好奇地打量自己，似乎有些紧张。近来她时常这样，可他又能说什么呢？那不过是一种感觉。他闷着火，抢白了她几句。她又受伤又吃惊的神情，让他好不难受。跨上马时，他想：我说什么也得寻一位颂唱师。

===Original===
There could be no doubt that he had done some unconscious wrong, deserting the Trail of Beauty. Forces of evil were preying upon him, he was no longer immune from bad thoughts. Stated in the American idiom, he decided he must be sick.

===Chinese===
毫无疑问，他准是犯了什么自己都未曾察觉的过失，背弃了美之踪迹（Trail of Beauty）。邪恶的力量正在作祟害他，他再也免不了种种恶念。用美国人的话来讲，他断定自己是病了。

===Original===
It was the merest chance that he met Yellow Singer walking along the trail with a bundle over his shoulder. Laughing Boy debated consulting him, and decided against it: not that ugly man.

===Chinese===
全凭一个凑巧，他遇上了黄颂唱师（Yellow Singer），那人正背着个包袱沿小径走来。笑娃掂量着要不要向他讨教，随即打消了主意：可别找那个丑八怪。

===Original===
“*Ahalani*, Grandfather,” the medicine man called to him; “wait a minute.”

===Chinese===
“阿哈拉尼（Ahalani），老爷爷，”那巫医冲他喊道；“等一等。”

===Original===
“*Ahalani*, Grandfather, I wait.”

===Chinese===
“阿哈拉尼，老爷爷，我等着。”

===Original===
The old rogue was standing straight and walking briskly; one saw that he was a tall man. Laughing Boy smelled whiskey.

===Chinese===
这老滑头站得笔直，脚下生风；看得出他是个高个子。笑娃闻到了威士忌的气味。

===Original===
“I see that you need medicine, little brother.”

===Chinese===
“我瞧得出你需要药，小兄弟。”

===Original===
“Unh! Why do you think that?”

===Chinese===
“唔！你怎么瞧出来的？”

===Original===
Yellow Singer noted the grunt and followed his lead. “I dreamed last night that when you were at the dance at Buckho Dotklish, you put those prayer cigarettes wrong. They fell down into the sand. Now they have put a spider’s web into your brain.”

===Chinese===
黄颂唱师听出这声闷哼，便顺着他的意思接下去。“我昨夜梦见，那回你在巴克霍·多特克利什（Buckho Dotklish）赶舞会，把那些祈祷烟卷插得不对。它们掉进了沙里。如今他们便把一张蛛网放进了你的脑子。”

===Original===
“You are right. I am not well.”

===Chinese===
“你说对了。我身子不对劲。”

===Original===
He nodded wisely. “So I went and got the remedy for you. I am ready to make you all right. You are a good young man; it will be my pleasure to make you all right.” He glowed with benevolence.

===Chinese===
他一副胸有成竹的样子，点了点头。“所以我去给你把药讨来了。我这就准备把你治好。你是个好后生；能把你治好，是我的乐事。”他满面慈祥，焕发着仁光。

===Original===
Evidently this man had more power than one would think. “How much will you want?”

===Chinese===
看来这人身上的法力比人们想的要大。“你要多少钱？”

===Original===
“Twenty dollars.”

===Chinese===
“二十块。”

===Original===
Laughing Boy considered. It was not a high fee. He counted out six dollars in coin, and pulled three plaques from his silver belt. “There, that is really worth more.”

===Chinese===
笑娃盘算了一下。这算不得高的价钱。他数出六块钱硬币，又从银腰带上解下三块银片。“喏，这些其实值得更多。”

===Original===
The old man hefted the metal. “All right.”

===Chinese===
老头掂了掂那银子。“行。”

===Original===
“What must I do?”

===Chinese===
“我该怎么做？”

===Original===
“You must go to a place alone, you must wash your hair. Then pray to the Divine Ones whose cigarettes you offended. Then take this remedy.”

===Chinese===
“你得独自到一个地方去，把头发洗净。然后向那些被你冒犯了烟卷的神圣之灵（Divine Ones）祷告。然后服这药。”

===Original===
Out of the bundle he took a bottle of red liquor, looked at it a moment, and then, benevolence conquering, took out a second and handed them over.

===Chinese===
他从包袱里掏出一瓶红色的酒，端详了片刻，随后——仁心终究占了上风——又摸出第二瓶，一并递了过去。

===Original===
“What is this?”

===Chinese===
“这是什么？”

===Original===
“It is a special kind of whiskey. It is very holy. The Americans drink it; it is so good they try to keep anyone else from having it.”

===Chinese===
“这是一种特别的威士忌。非常神圣。美国人喝的就是它；它太好了，他们想方设法不让旁人沾着。”

===Original===
“How do I take it?”

===Chinese===
“我怎么个服法？”

===Original===
“When you have prayed, just start drinking it. By and by you will feel your mind becoming all right, your heart will be high. Then you will sleep. When you wake up, you will feel badly, but if you take some more, you will feel all right. One bottle should be enough. Put the other away until something tells you you need it.”

===Chinese===
“祷告完了，只管喝起来。过不多时，你会觉得脑子变得清明，心气高涨。然后你会睡去。醒来时你会难受，可只要再服一点，就又没事了。一瓶应当够了。另一瓶收起来，等有什么告诉你需要它的时候再用。”

===Original===
“I see.”

===Chinese===
“明白了。”

===Original===
“I shall go on the trail to Buckho Dotklish, and make a charm there, to prevent any more bad things coming to you from those cigarettes. Tell no one about this, above all no woman. It is very holy and secret; if you speak of it, it will do you harm. It will make you jump into the fire.”

===Chinese===
“我要上路去巴克霍·多特克利什，在那儿设一道符咒，免得那些烟卷再降祸事到你身上。这事对谁也不要讲，尤其不能对女人讲。它非常神圣、非常隐秘；你要是说出去，它就会害你。会叫你跳进火里去。”

===Original===
“I see.”

===Chinese===
“明白了。”

===Original===
“If you need more, let me know. I may be able to get you some.”

===Chinese===
“要是还需要，捎个信给我。兴许我还能给你弄到些。”

===Original===
He rode to his usual camping place by Natahnetinn, and went solemnly about the prescribed ceremonial. Then he tasted the drink. It was unlike the white whiskey; not so bad, but still pretty bad﻿—low-grade, frontier tanglefoot rye, dear at a dollar a bottle.

===Chinese===
他骑马到纳塔内廷（Natahnetinn）旁边他惯常的宿营地，郑重其事地行了规定的仪节。然后他尝了尝那酒。它不像白人的威士忌；没那么难喝，可仍旧够呛——低档的边地劣酿黑麦威士忌，一块钱一瓶都算贵。

===Original===
After the first few drinks it came easier, but it did not make him feel very happy. As he grew drunk, he longed more and more for his own country, and for a truce from the constant feeling of the presence of alien things. About the time it grew dark, he stopped drinking and walked up and down. At first he sang, then he was silent.

===Chinese===
头几口下去，再喝就顺了，可它并没有让他痛快起来。他越醉，越渴望自己的家乡，渴望从那种异物无时不在之感里讨得一段休战。天快黑时，他停了杯，走来走去。起初他还唱着，随后便不作声了。

===Original===
Liquor, taken in solitude, sometimes has this effect. Along with a megalomaniac sense of his central position in the universe, a man grows bluntly honest with himself. All the secret, forgotten, stifled thoughts come out of the closet in his mind, and he must face them in turn, without a saving sense of proportion. This now was Laughing Boy’s portion.

===Chinese===
独自喝下的酒，有时会有这样的效力。伴随着一股自大狂的错觉——觉得自己稳居宇宙的中心——人会对自己生硬地诚实起来。所有那些隐秘的、被遗忘的、被捂死的念头，纷纷走出心里那间小黑屋，他必须挨个面对它们，却没有分寸感来搭救。这会儿轮到笑娃领受这一份了。

===Original===
I am not happy in the house at Chiziai. It is too lonely, too strange a life; no one ever comes. We see people only at dances. That American town, what is there there? What is this preacher’s wife? The look in her face when she returns﻿—I do not know. There is something wrong, always something hidden. She is always hiding something. Let us go North, go North, to T’o Tlakai! Oh, my mother!

===Chinese===
我在这奇扎伊（Chiziai）的屋子里过得不快活。这日子太冷清、太生分；从来没有人上门。我们只有赶舞会才见得着人。那座美国人的镇子，那里面到底有什么？那个女传教士又是怎么回事？她回来时脸上的那副神情——我说不透。有什么不对，总是有什么藏着的事。她总在藏什么。咱们往北去吧，往北去，去特奥·特莱凯（T’o Tlakai）！啊，我的母亲！

===Original===
When I told her about her weaving; when we rode together that time, then she needed me, then I, too, was strong. We were happiest then, both of us. She is stronger; it is she who leads me.

===Chinese===
我指点她织布的那几回；我们一道骑马出去的那一回，那时她需要我，那时我也强壮。那时我们俩最最快活。她更强；领着我走的是她。

===Original===
I am afraid to speak to her.

===Chinese===
我怕同她开口。

===Original===
He stopped short and clutched his hands together.

===Chinese===
他猛地收住脚，双手紧紧攥在一起。

===Original===
Why? I am afraid to lose her. Am I losing myself? Oh, I do not know, I do not know; this life she has had, this wisdom of hers. What went on before? Who was the man, and what does Red Man know? Perhaps if I spoke to her, she would say no. She makes her own life. I am losing myself. And I cannot leave her, Came With War, Came With War. Oh, no, can’t leave her. She would say “No,” and I should say “All right,” and then I should be dead.

===Chinese===
为什么？我怕失去她。我是在失去我自己吗？啊，我不知道，我不知道；她过的那种日子，她那一套聪明。从前出过什么事？那男人是谁，红汉子（Red Man）又知道些什么？也许我一开口，她就会说不。她自有她的活法。我在失去我自己。可我离不开她，携战而来（Came With War），携战而来。啊，不，离不开她。她会说“不”，我会说“好吧”，那我就完了。

===Original===
How long will it be before we are rich enough to suit her? Why will she not herd sheep? All women do. I do not know. This American life she has led, she will not leave such things. It is my enemy. Our life is not good enough for her.

===Chinese===
还要多久，我们才能富得合她的意？她为什么不肯放羊？女人个个都放。我不明白。她过的这种美国日子，她丢不下。它是我的仇敌。我们自己的日子，配不上她。

===Original===
She wants so much money. A year, another year, who knows? So long, long. When will there be children? We should have had children. I want children. I want to go home. What is happening to me? I am losing myself. She holds the reins and I am becoming a led horse. Two, three years, all like this, and Sings Before Spears, who was a warrior, will have ended, and there will just be that part of a man which worships a woman. Not the rest of him, just heat. A bowstring without a bow. Only good for a woman to tie something with.

===Chinese===
她要那么多钱。一年，又一年，谁知道呢？这么久，这么久啊。什么时候才会有孩子？我们本该早有孩子了。我想要孩子。我想回家。我这是怎么了？我在失去我自己。她攥着缰绳，我正在变成一匹被牵着走的马。两年，三年，全这样过下去，那个当过战士的矛前放歌（Sings Before Spears）就完了，剩下的只是男人身上拜倒于女人的那一部分。不是其余的他，只剩一股热。一根没有弓的弦。只配给女人拴点什么用。

===Original===
I need some more medicine.

===Chinese===
我还需要再服一点药。

===Original===
Another stiff drink sent him over the borderline into incoherent plans for performing wonders. Three or four more put him to sleep.

===Chinese===
又灌下一大口，把他送过了那道界线，跌进一堆语无伦次的奇迹大计里。再来上三四口，便把他放倒了。

===Original===
He was in pretty bad shape when he awoke, late, with the high sun beating upon him. He went down to the arroyo and dabbled in its shallow, unfresh water. He was not as sick as the other time, but he was sick.

===Chinese===
他醒来时已经很晚，高悬的太阳直晒在身上，那副光景相当狼狈。他下到旱谷里，在那浅浅的、不清爽的水里浸了浸。他没有上回那么难受，可还是难受。

===Original===
“When you wake up, you will feel badly, but if then you take a little more, you will feel all right.”

===Chinese===
“等你醒来，你会难受，可那时再喝一点点，你就没事了。”

===Original===
He would try it. The smell made him feel worse. He poured some into a cup, returned to the arroyo and weakened it with water. Then he downed it in one straining gulp. He did feel better. Perhaps he might take a little more, he thought, reaching for the bottle, and paused with it half-tipped for pouring.

===Chinese===
他要试一试。那气味让他更难受了。他倒了一些进杯子，回到旱谷边，兑水冲淡。随后他憋足一口气，一仰脖灌了下去。他确实觉得好受了些。也许可以再来一点，他想着，伸手去够瓶子，瓶身倾出一半、正待往下倒的当口，又停住了。

===Original===
No. He was remembering last night, and that had been terrible. He put it down, and stared at the ashes of his fire.

===Chinese===
不。他记起了昨夜，那滋味太可怕了。他把瓶子放下，呆呆望着火堆的灰烬。

===Original===
“Coffee,” he said aloud.

===Chinese===
“咖啡。”他出了声。

===Original===
He drank a lot more water when he went to fill his pot. The heat of the flames was unpleasant to him; he was beginning to feel badly again, and wanted a drink. He put a lot of coffee in.

===Chinese===
去灌水壶的时候，他又多喝了好些水。火焰的热气让他不好受；他又开始难受起来，想喝一口酒。他往壶里搁了很多咖啡。

===Original===
That had been all true, what he had thought last night, but incomplete and exaggerated. He was homesick, he was afraid of losing her, but what kind of man could not wait a few years, three at the worst, for so reasonable a cause. She was wise, she was right, and he was sure she loved him. Well, then?

===Chinese===
昨夜想的那些都是真的，只是既不完全，又过了头。他想家，他怕失去她，可为着这样正当的缘由，哪样的男人还等不得这几年呢——往坏里算三年。她是明智的，她是对的，而且他确信她爱他。那么，还愁什么？

===Original===
The whiskey now, this magic. It did drive the clouds out of his thoughts, but it made everything appear twisted.

===Chinese===
这威士忌，这所谓的法术。它确实把云翳从他脑子里驱了出去，却让一切都显得扭曲。

===Original===
He lifted the coffee off the fire. It was strong. Without waiting for the sugar, he tried to drink it, burning his tongue.

===Chinese===
他把咖啡从火上端下来。很浓。等不及放糖便往嘴里送，烫着了舌头。

===Original===
It was not magic. It was just something like jimpson-weed. Under its influence he had seen himself, but there was nothing holy about it. He remembered quite clearly how he had placed those cigarettes in a crevice in the rock. There had been nothing wrong about it. That old coyote had made a lucky guess, and followed it up with lies to make money, that was all.

===Chinese===
这不是法术。它不过跟曼陀罗草是一路东西。在它的作用下他看见了自己，可它身上毫无神圣可言。他清清楚楚记得，自己当时是怎样把那些烟卷插进岩缝里的。那没有任何差错。那头老郊狼不过是瞎猫碰上死耗子，又编下瞎话来挣钱，仅此而已。

===Original===
He saw a very clear picture of Yellow Singer and his wife as he had first met them, sober, and reaching for the bottle; he saw other scarecrow Indians he had met in this American’s country. He looked at them, and behind them saw incoherently the great, ominous cloud of the American system, something for which he had no name or description.

===Chinese===
他眼前清清楚楚映出初见时的黄颂唱师和他老婆——清醒着，却又伸手去够酒瓶；他又看见在这片美国人的地界上遇见过的别的那些稻草人般的印第安人。他望着他们，又在他们背后影影绰绰望见那团庞大而不祥的乌云——美国人的整套体制，一样他叫不出名目、也没法描摹的东西。

===Original===
That was another thing about which Slim Girl had been right, that drink. She knew how to tame it. She had the secret of how to prevent American knowledge from doing harm; she made it serve a good purpose.

===Chinese===
在那件事上瘦姑娘又是对的——那杯中之物。她懂得怎样驯服它。她有秘诀，能让美国人的学问不为害，还叫它派上好的用场。

===Original===
He set down his cup of coffee, picked up a rock, and deliberately smashed the bottle. The liquid ran into the coals of the fire, caught, and for a moment the dampened sand burnt with a blue flame. That startled him. To drink something like that! He threw in the fragments of the bottle, in the bottom of which were still a few drops, and watched the blue light flicker briefly above them.

===Chinese===
他放下咖啡杯，拾起一块石头，一板一眼地把酒瓶砸了。酒液流进火堆的炭里，腾地燃着了，湿沙地上有一刻烧起蓝色的火苗。那吓了他一跳。这样的东西，居然往肚里喝！他把瓶子的碎片——瓶底还挂着几滴——也扔了进去，望着蓝光在它们上面短短地闪了一阵。

===Original===
He drank another cup of coffee, with sugar, then unearthed the second bottle from its cache. That had cost money, much money. Well, he’d had his money’s worth. From now on he could think without the help of blue flames. He poured it over the fire, and the drenching put the fire out. Eh! This was strange stuff!

===Chinese===
他又喝了一杯咖啡，加了糖，然后把第二瓶从藏处刨了出来。那是花了钱的，花了大钱。也罢，这钱算没白花。从今往后，他用不着靠蓝色火焰帮着想事了。他把酒浇上火，那一大浇把火浇熄了。欸！这东西真邪性！

===Original===
To try to round up horses seemed out of the question. He stretched out in the shade of the rocks, craving sleep, his limbs feeling as though he had been through a furious wrestling bout. The sky was too blue, it hurt his eyes; the circling of a distant buzzard made his head ache. He turned over and fixed his gaze on a crack, studying it, sleepy, yet unable to keep his eyes shut.

===Chinese===
想去圈马，是想也别想了。他摊开手脚躺在岩荫里，渴想着睡眠，四肢的感觉像是刚打完一场凶猛的摔跤。天太蓝了，蓝得刺眼；远处一只秃鹫打转，看得他头疼。他翻过身去，盯住一道石缝细瞧，睡意沉沉，却又合不上眼。

===Original===
Yellow Singer and all his kind were bad. They were like an offensive smell. But a smell came from a carcass. Those people were the way they were because of the Americans. The town of Los Palos in the drenching sunlight, quiet, dead-looking beside its irrigated fields. What was it? Something in the air, something that perverted the world. Where they were was no place for Earth People. They had done something to Slim Girl, one could see that, but she seemed to have risen above it. But they were bad for her, too. It was beyond him.

===Chinese===
黄颂唱师和他那一类人都是坏的。他们像一股恶臭。可恶臭是腐尸上发出来的。那些人之所以成了那副样子，是因为美国人。洛斯帕洛斯（Los Palos）那座镇子泡在倾泻的日光里，守着它那片水浇地，安静，死气沉沉。它是什么？空气里的某种东西，某种把世界带坏的东西。他们待的地方，不是地上之民（Earth People）待的地方。他们对瘦姑娘做过些什么，这是看得出的，可她似乎已经超脱出来了。但他们对她也是有害的。这他参不透。

===Original===
He smoked, and at length slept fitfully through the noonday heat, wakened now and again by flies, to drowse delightfully and return to sleep. In the late evening he went to where a waterfall in the arroyo made a trickling shower bath. The water refreshed him; he was hungry once more, and felt better.

===Chinese===
他抽着烟，终于顶着正午的酷热断断续续睡去，不时被苍蝇闹醒，迷迷糊糊再惬意地睡回去。傍晚时分，他走到旱谷里一处小瀑布下，那里淌成一道细流的淋浴。水让他精神一爽；他又饿了，觉得好多了。

===Original===
What he had thought last night had been true, but unbalanced; and all this about Americans had been just because he felt sick. He had always known Americans, traders and such, they were all right, just people of a different tribe. He stretched out, fed, smoking, surprised at his desire to sleep again. It would be pleasant, it would be beautiful, returning to T’o Tlakai rich, very rich, with her, and to settle down somewhere near there and have children. They needed children. Meantime they would make their way together. Oh, beautiful.

===Chinese===
昨夜想的那些是真的，但有失偏颇；而那些关于美国人的念头，不过因为他身子难受。他一向认得美国人，生意人之流的，他们都还好，只是另一个部族的人罢了。他吃饱了躺着，抽着烟，惊讶于自己又想睡了。那会是愉快的，会是美的：带着她回到特奥·特莱凯，富有，非常富有，在那一带什么地方安顿下来，生儿育女。他们需要孩子。这期间，他们会一同把日子闯出来。啊，真美。

### III / III

===Original===
Next morning he felt better. The drunkenness and the emotiorial outburst had cleared his system. In pouring out the liquor, he felt that he had destroyed a bad thing; the enemies in his head had indeed proved to be nothing but cobwebs, and they were gone. Like the man who burned the tumbleweed when the Eagles were afraid of it, he thought.

===Chinese===
第二天早晨他觉得好些了。宿醉和那场情绪的大宣泄涤清了他的身子。把酒倒出去的时候，他觉得自己毁掉了一件坏东西；他脑袋里的那些仇敌，果然不过是一张张蛛网，如今都散了。就像那个人在群鹰惊惧时把风滚草烧掉一样，他想。

===Original===
He rounded up the three horses he wanted, good ones. Only the best horses sold this year, and they did not bring so good a price. He rode home contented, quiet, and determined to do better with himself.

===Chinese===
他圈齐了想要的那三匹马，都是好马。今年只有最好的马才卖得动，还卖不上好价钱。他骑马回家，心里满足，安安静静，打定主意要把自己调理得像样些。

===Original===
He found his wife waiting before the door.

===Chinese===
他看见妻子正在门前等他。

===Original===
“I did not know you were going to be gone so long; I have been lonely without you. Bring in your saddle while I get supper. I am glad when you come back.”

===Chinese===
“我不知道你会去这么久；你不在，我冷清得很。把鞍子搬进来吧，我去做晚饭。你回来，我就高兴。”

===Original===
“I am always glad to be back.”

===Chinese===
“我回来也总是高兴的。”

===Original===
“As long as you feel like that, I shall continue to be happy.”

===Chinese===
“只要你一直这样想，我就会一直快活。”

===Original===
Why should he worry himself about this woman? And why should he worry about anything else as long as he had this woman? He slapped the ponies’ flanks to make them run around the corral. He looked at his growing corn, and as he broke the little mud dam across his irrigation ditch, he felt the coolness of evening seep along his veins as the bright water spread through the narrow channels. Clay bluffs were not as fine as painted rock, there was too much adobe in this sand, but it was a fair place. The fire gleamed before his house, he heard the flow of water and the occasional stamp of a horse in the corral.

===Chinese===
他何必为这个女人操心？再说，只要有这个女人在，他又何必为任何别的事操心？他拍着小马的胁腹，赶它们绕着畜栏奔跑。他看着自己日渐长高的玉米；挖开横在灌溉沟上的那道小泥坝时，他觉出黄昏的凉意顺着血脉渗开，亮闪闪的水漫进一道道窄沟。黏土的崖壁不如彩绘的岩石好看，这沙土里黏土也太多，可这是个不坏的地方。火光在他屋前闪亮，他听得见水声，还有栏里马偶尔的一声跺蹄。

===Original===
Slim Girl brought out the bottle and an orange.

===Chinese===
瘦姑娘拿出了酒瓶和一只橙子。

===Original===
“Do not make the drink, little sister, I do not want it. I think I shall try not taking it.”

===Chinese===
“今儿别调那酒了，小妹妹，我不想喝。我想试试不沾它。”

===Original===
She kept herself from looking at him. She was troubled.

===Chinese===
她克制着不去看他。她心里乱了。

===Original===
“All right.”

===Chinese===
“好吧。”

===Original===
What was this? Probably nothing. When one walks on the sheer edge of a precipice, the meaningless fall of a stone over the side momentarily stops the heart. She studied him while they ate. She began to talk with him gravely of the life they were to make together, of the happiness that was in store for them.

===Chinese===
这是什么意思？多半没什么。人走在绝壁的刃口上时，一块石头没来由地从崖边翻落，也会叫人心口蓦地一停。吃饭时她细细察看他。她开始郑重地同他谈起他们将一同营造的生活，谈起等着他们的那份幸福。

===Original===
He said to himself, “She was bothered when I refused that drink, the way she looked at me afterwards. She is nervous about me. I have done that, by acting as I have. Now she is trying to tell me how she really is; she is talking true; I know. I have wronged her.”

===Chinese===
他心里想：“我回绝那杯酒时她不安了，看她随后望我的那个样子。她在为我揪着心。是我弄成这样的，全怪我这些日子的作为。如今她想把她真实的样子讲给我听；她说的是真心话；我知道。我委屈她了。”

===Original===
She saw the last trailing clouds pass from him. That evening was perfect, so perfect that, with his doubts banished and the feeling of intimacy upon him, he almost told her everything he had done and thought, but he postponed. It was the last of its kind for many days.

===Chinese===
她看见最后几缕残云从他身上飘散过去了。那个傍晚是完美的，完美到他把疑虑驱尽、亲密之情涌上心头，差一点把做过的事、想过的念头一古脑儿全告诉她，只是他推迟了。往后许多天里，那样完美的傍晚，这是最后一个。

===Original===
Like an ancient magician who, by saying the forbidden names, evoked genii whom he could not then drive back, Laughing Boy had given form to thoughts which were not to be forgotten. Unhappily for himself, he was no fool, and of an honest habit of thought. There was love in that place, and sometimes happiness, but if a religious-minded Navajo had entered there, he would have felt that the air was empty.

===Chinese===
就像一个古代的术士，念出了禁忌的名讳，召来了再也驱遣不走的精灵，笑娃已让那些念头成了形，再也忘不掉了。于他不幸的是，他不是蠢人，又有一副诚实的思维习惯。那地方有爱，有时也有幸福，可倘若一个心思虔敬的纳瓦霍人走进那里，他会觉出那空气是空的。

### IV / IV

===Original===
Slim Girl continued weaving despite the poor sales, because she found relief and, one might say, a confidant in her craft. And then, they two, working side by side, reconstructed at least the outward signs of the harmony that was gone.

===Chinese===
尽管销路不好，瘦姑娘仍继续织布，因为她在手艺里寻得了解脱，也可以说，寻得了一位知心人。再者，他们俩并肩劳作，也至少把那已经失去的和睦的外在形迹重建了起来。

===Original===
He eased his soul by shaping the half-stubborn, half-willing metal. It is a matter of patience, from the lump or the coins to the bar, from the bar to the bracelet. This, the most precious and beautiful of metals, is the easiest to work. That is a gift of the gods. Slow, slow, under successive light strokes the bar becomes longer, flatter, thinner: it is struck and it grows towards its appointed shape.

===Chinese===
他靠敲打那半倔半顺的金属来宽解自己的灵魂。这是一桩磨性子的功夫：从银坨或钱币到银条，从银条到手镯。这种金属，这最贵重、最美的金属，偏最容易打造。那是诸神的一份馈赠。慢来，慢来，一记接一记的轻锤之下，银条渐渐变长、变扁、变薄：它挨着敲打，朝它命定的形状长去。

===Original===
I am impatient these days, I get tired of the finishing. One must have one’s mind made up to it from the start, from four Mexican coins to the finely finished ornament; one must see it as it will be, and not stop short of what he has seen.

===Chinese===
这些天我沉不住气，收尾的功夫让我厌倦。人得从头就把主意拿定：从四块墨西哥银币到一件精工做就的饰物；人得看见它将来的样子，并且不见到自己所见成真，决不罢手。

===Original===
Having woven about a foot of blanket, the head-sticks of the loom are lowered, and the finished part is rolled around the foot-sticks, out of sight.

===Chinese===
织到一尺来毯面，织机的头杆便放低下来，织好的部分便卷上脚杆，收进看不见的地方。

===Original===
This is like time. Here, this little part showing, where I am weaving, is the present; the past is rolled up and gone; there are those empty warp cords above me. The weft is like handling a nervous horse; I lead the blue strand gently to the green, or it will break; I hate to break a strand, the knot where it is mended will always show, a blemish. But then, I pound the fork and the batten down hard, hard; they lock the weave and that much more is past.

===Chinese===
这就像时间。眼前露着的这一小段，我正织着的，是现在；过去卷起来了，去了；头顶上是一根根空着的经线。引纬像调弄一匹神经紧张的马；我把蓝线轻轻引到绿线旁，不然它会断；我不愿弄断一根线，接上的那个结会永远显在那里，是个疵点。可接着，我把打纬叉和打纬刀狠狠地、狠狠地砸下去；它们锁住织口，又多出一截成了过去。

===Original===
He was curving the strip of flattened silver. This bracelet is coming out just as I thought of it. One must know his design before he starts; when this strip was still four coins, I knew that there would be tracks pointing one way from each end to the centre, clouds at each end, and that stone where the tracks meet. How do I know it? Not all men can; what is it I have? The Mexicans are lazy, their money is pure, soft silver; the American coins have something in them to make them hard, they are hard to work with. Those Americans!

===Chinese===
他正把那条锤扁的银片弯出弧度。这只镯子正照我想的样子出来。动手之前就得知道自己的图样；这条银片还是四块银币的时候，我就知道它会有一行行足迹从两端各自指向正中，两端各有云纹，足迹相会之处嵌那颗石头。我怎么知道的？不是人人都会；我身上这点本事是什么？墨西哥人懒，他们的钱是纯的、软的银子；美国银币里掺了叫它发硬的东西，不好使唤。那些美国人呐！

===Original===
Her fingers were deft, and she pulled at the warp like a harp-player. I am not sure I like this pattern, but it is too late to change it. No, it is a good pattern. Should I unravel all I have done, when it grows so slowly? When the blanket has been started, it is too late to change. The man who is always coming back to where he started, to figure out another road, will never get far from home. I wish I could just think my design and have it woven at an American mill. No, I don’t; it is because I toiled over them that I love them.

===Chinese===
她的手指灵巧，拨动经线像弹竖琴的人。我说不好喜欢不喜欢这个花样，可要改已经迟了。不，这是个好花样。它长得这么慢，难道我该把织成的全拆了？毯子一经开织，再改就迟了。那种总要回到起头处、再另想一条路的人，永远走不出家门口多远。我倒想光把图样想好，交给美国人的工厂织出来。不，我不想；正因为我在它们身上下过苦功，我才爱它们。

===Original===
The turquoise is the important thing in this bracelet. I looked at it and saw the setting for it. But much of the time now I cannot think well, I am not myself. I have no design for myself, I do not know the nature of my jewel. I am hammering a piece of silver, and I cannot stop hammering, every day is another stroke; yet I do not know what it is to be. In the end it may be just a piece of good metal pounded flat. I do not know my design.

===Chinese===
这只镯子里，要紧的是那颗绿松石。我望着它，便看见了它的镶座。可如今我很多时候想不好事情，我不是我自己了。我没有自己的图样，我不知道我这颗宝石的禀性。我在锤打一块银子，我停不了手，每一天都是又一锤；可我不知道它要成为什么。到头来，它也许只是一块敲扁了的好金属。我不知道我的图样。

===Original===
This blanket is like the other things. I am always being uncertain now. It is all like this blanket. Shall I unravel it, when I have been so long in getting so far? The design is set, a blanket with a broken design would be absurd, a failure. The only thing to do is to carry it through, with softness and with strength. My design is set.

===Chinese===
这条毯子同别的种种一样。如今我总是拿不定主意。一切都像这条毯子。我熬了这么久才走到这一步，难道要拆了它？图样已经定了，一条图样中断的毯子会显得荒唐，是一件败笔。唯一的办法是把它织到底，用轻柔，也用气力。我的图样已经定了。

## Chapter XVII / 第十七章

### I / I

===Original===
As summer drew to a close, Laughing Boy took to spending more time than was necessary with his horses. Sometimes he would crave company, and, if he found it, would be sociable and garrulous; at other times he kept very much to himself. No one who met him then for the first time would have named him “Laughing Boy.” Locally he was known as “Horse Trader,” and latterly one Indian had applied to him, in jest, the name of the legendary character “Turns His Back,” which bade fair to stick. Partly he liked to be away from home because of the chance of a happy return. If she were waiting for him, if she had not been in to town, if he was tired from the long ride and at peace with himself, the old spell would surround them. If she was just back from that work of hers, or if she came in after he did, she overtired, brooding, and a little nervous, it was a failure. Once or twice they quarrelled; she had an amazingly sharp and clever tongue. The quarrels ended in reconciliation and passion which exhausted them both without bringing peace to either.

===Chinese===
夏天渐渐收尾，笑娃开始把越来越多的工夫花在马群里，多得超出必要。有时他也渴望有人作伴，真找到了，便谈笑风生、滔滔不绝；另一些时候，他又极是离群索居。那时候头一回见到他的人，谁也不会想到叫他“笑娃”。当地人管他叫“马贩子”，近来更有一个印第安人拿传说人物“转过背去”（Turns His Back）的名号安在他头上取笑，这名字眼看要叫开了。他乐意离家，一半也是贪图那份欢喜归家的可能：要是她正等着他，要是她没进城里去过，要是他长途骑罢、乏透而心里安宁，旧日那道魔咒便会把他们俩罩住。要是她刚从她那份活计里回来，或是比他晚归，过度劳顿、郁郁沉思、带着几分烦躁，那便是一场败兴。有一两回他们吵了嘴；她那条舌头尖利灵巧得惊人。争吵以和好与缠绵收场，把两人都耗得精疲力竭，却没给哪一个带来安宁。

===Original===
On one day, when autumn had begun to take the weight out of the noon sunshine, he sat basking on a hillock, smoking, with his pony beside him. He was lazily content, and comfortable enough within himself not to mind the sight of a human approaching. He felt like talking to someone.

===Chinese===
这一天，秋意已经开始抽走正午阳光的分量，他坐在一座小土丘上晒着太阳抽烟，他的小马立在身旁。他懒洋洋地舒坦，心里也够安适，望见一个人影走近并不嫌烦。他正想找个人说说话。

===Original===
The jogging dot drew nearer. Still looking black, the motion of the shoulders told that it was an Indian. Yes, and probably a Navajo, with a flaming scarlet headband. Laughing Boy sat up straight. He knew that bald-faced chestnut, he knew that swing of the whip hand. He made sure. He was surprised, curious, and delighted. What brought his friend so far from home? He rose to his feet. Jesting Squaw’s Son slowed from lope to trot, to a walk, and stopped beside him.

===Chinese===
那个颠簸着的小点越来越近。远看仍是一团黑，但从肩膀的动态已能认出是个印第安人。不错，多半还是个纳瓦霍人，扎着一条火焰般猩红的头带。笑娃坐直了身子。他认得那匹白脸的栗色马，认得那只执鞭的手甩动的架势。他看准了。他又惊又奇，又是欢喜。什么事把朋友引到离家这么远的地方来？他站起身。打趣婆娘之子把马从奔跑收成小跑，再收成慢步，在他身旁停了下来。

===Original===
“*Ei-yei*, my friend!” Laughing Boy took his hand. “It is good to see you!”

===Chinese===
“咿-耶，我的朋友！”笑娃握住他的手。“见到你真好！”

===Original===
“My friend.” He smiled, but he dismounted slowly, and his eyes were hurt. “I am glad to find you.”

===Chinese===
“我的朋友。”他笑了，可他下马下得很慢，眼里带着伤。“找到你，我很高兴。”

===Original===
“Sit down. A cigarette?”

===Chinese===
“坐下吧。来支烟？”

===Original===
“Yes.”

===Chinese===
“好。”

===Original===
“Where are you going?”

===Chinese===
“你上哪儿去？”

===Original===
“Just riding around.”

===Chinese===
“随便骑马转转。”

===Original===
“What is the news at T’o Tlakai?”

===Chinese===
“特奥·特莱凯有什么新闻？”

===Original===
“All well. Your people are all well. Your sister, the one who married Bay Horse, has a son. The other one has just married Yellow Foot’s Son.”

===Chinese===
“都好。你家的人都好。你姐姐，嫁了枣红马的那个，生了个儿子。另一个刚嫁了黄脚之子（Yellow Foot’s Son）。”

===Original===
“Good.”

===Chinese===
“好。”

===Original===
“There has been good rain, and the traders are paying twenty, twenty-five cents for wool.”

===Chinese===
“雨水下得好，商人收羊毛出两毛、两毛五。”

===Original===
“Good.”

===Chinese===
“好。”

===Original===
“And you, tell?”

===Chinese===
“你呢，说说？”

===Original===
“All well. Our goods sell well, the corn was fine this year. All well.”

===Chinese===
“都好。我们的货卖得好，今年玉米也好。都好。”

===Original===
They smoked.

===Chinese===
两人抽烟。

===Original===
“It is good to see you.”

===Chinese===
“见到你真好。”

===Original===
Jesting Squaw’s Son made no answer. Laughing Boy studied him; he was too quiet.

===Chinese===
打趣婆娘之子没有作声。笑娃端详着他；他太安静了。

===Original===
“What brings you so far from home?”

===Chinese===
“什么事把你引到离家这么远的地方来？”

===Original===
“Nothing, just riding around.”

===Chinese===
“没什么，随便骑马转转。”

===Original===
“You will come to our hogan?”

===Chinese===
“你要到我们霍根屋来吗？”

===Original===
“Yes.”

===Chinese===
“要的。”

===Original===
They finished their cigarettes, and sat looking at nothing. There was a pleasant, afternoon feeling that tended to make talk slow, the smell of the warm sand, quietness. After about five minutes, Laughing Boy said:

===Chinese===
两人抽完了烟，坐着，眼睛望着空处。午后有一种叫人舒坦的意味，话不由得慢了下来；暖沙的气味，四下里一片安静。约莫过了五分钟，笑娃说：

===Original===
“You might as well show me your true thought. It is all around you like a cloud. It is what you are thinking of all the time you are talking about anything else. You are hurt. What hurts you, whom I have called friend, hurts me.”

===Chinese===
“你不如把真心事亮给我看吧。它像一团云，把你整个围在里头。你嘴上谈着别的，心里一直想着的就是它。你伤着了。我拿你当朋友，你的痛就是我的痛。”

===Original===
“You are right. Give me tobacco.”

===Chinese===
“你说得对。给我烟丝。”

===Original===
He rolled another cigarette and smoked it through before he began to speak.

===Chinese===
他又卷了一支烟，把它从头到尾抽完，才开口说话。

===Original===
“You remember that joke we played on Narrow Nose at Gomulli T’o? Do you suppose we did anything bad by accident? Did we start any evil working?”

===Chinese===
“你还记得咱们在戈穆利·特奥捉弄窄鼻子那桩玩笑吧？你想，咱们会不会无意间做下了什么坏事？会不会牵动了什么邪性的事？”

===Original===
“What makes you think that?”

===Chinese===
“你怎么会这么想？”

===Original===
“You remember, I said I was bringing back a wife from Maito. That made me wonder. I went to Maito a little while ago.” He was looking at the tips of his fingers. Now he paused.

===Chinese===
“你记得的，我说过要从迈托领一个妻子回来。这就叫我犯了寻思。前些日子我去了趟迈托。”他望着自己的指尖。说到这里，他停住了。

===Original===
“We wanted to trade a cow for some sheep. Your brother and White Goat and I were riding along. We saw a Pah-Ute driving a cow he had taken from the Mormons, so we took it away from him. There is no pasture for cattle up there, but we heard of a man at Maito who kept a herd. So we took it down to him. His name is Alkali Water.

===Chinese===
“我们想拿一头牛换些羊。你兄弟和白山羊（White Goat），还有我，一路骑马走着。我们看见一个派尤特人赶着一头牛，那是他从摩门人手里拿的，我们便把它从他手里夺了过来。上头那边没有放牛的草场，可我们听说迈托有个人养着一群牛，就把它赶下山给了他。他名叫碱水（Alkali Water）。

===Original===
“The cow was pretty thin. We were there three nights trading on it.

===Chinese===
“那头牛相当瘦。我们围着这笔交易讨价还价，在那儿待了三夜。

===Original===
“I saw his daughter. At the end of the first day I knew that I had been born only for her, that that was what I had always been waiting for. I was all one piece, everything in me was to one purpose. I do not know how to say it.”

===Chinese===
“我见着了他的女儿。头一天过完，我就知道，我生下来只为她一个，知道这便是我一直等着的一切。我整个人打成了一块，我身上的一切都朝着同一个目的去。我不知道该怎么说。”

===Original===
“I know.”

===Chinese===
“我知道。”

===Original===
“Yes, you know. That is why I am here. I know now why good men sometimes have to do with other people’s wives. I have learned a great deal about myself.

===Chinese===
“是，你知道。所以我才到这儿来。我现在明白了，好人为什么有时候会同别人的婆娘有牵扯。我对自己懂得了许多。

===Original===
“I shall not try to say what she looked like. What would be the use? She was not small, like your wife; she was strong. Her eyes and mouth were beautiful, she was beautiful, and you could see beauty inside her by her eyes and her mouth.

===Chinese===
“我不去形容她长什么模样了。说了有什么用？她不像你妻子那样小巧；她壮实。她的眼睛和嘴生得美，她整个人美，从她的眼睛和嘴里，你能看见她心里的美。

===Original===
“We stayed there for three nights, for three nights and two days I was watching her and listening to her. I think she felt as I did; we did not speak to each other, hardly at all. When we went away she looked at me.

===Chinese===
“我们在那里待了三夜，三夜两天，我一直望着她，听着她说话。我想她怀着和我一样的心思；我们没有交谈，几乎一句也没有。我们走的时候，她望了我。

===Original===
“I waited a few days at home. I was very happy; I did not know such happiness could exist. Then I returned to Maito.
