I wanted to see her again, to be sure, and to find out her clan before I asked my mother to ask for her. I did not want anyone to be able to object, as they might, since she did not live near us.

===Chinese===
“我在家里等了几天。我快活极了；我不知道这样的快活竟能存在。随后我又回迈托去了。我想再见她一面，把心定下来，也想在请我母亲去提亲之前，先探明她的氏族。我不想给任何人留下反对的由头，像他们本来可能的那样，谁让她住得离我们不近。

===Original===
“I cannot make up songs as you do, but I made up a pretty good one. I sang all along the trail. I neared her hogan galloping and singing the Wildcat Song. She was coming out along the trail towards me. I galloped close to her and reined up short, in a handsome way. She came beside my horse and laid her hand on its neck.

===Chinese===
“我不像你会编歌，可我也编出了一支蛮好的。我一路沿小道唱着走。临近她的霍根屋时，我飞马奔驰，唱着那支山猫之歌（Wildcat Song）。她正顺着小道朝外迎面走来。我策马奔到她近旁，潇洒地猛一收缰。她走到我的马旁，把一只手搭在马颈上。

===Original===
“ ‘My friend,’ she said.

===Chinese===
“‘我的朋友，’她说。

===Original===
“I was so happy then that there is no name for it. There was no earth under me, I had no limits. Then she went on.

===Chinese===
“我那时快活得没有名目可称。脚下没有了大地，我也没有了边限。随后她接着说了下去。

===Original===
“ ‘You must go away, you must not see me again. I must not see you,’ she said.

===Chinese===
“‘你必须走开，你不该再见我。我不该再见你，’她说。

===Original===
“I asked, ‘Why?’

===Chinese===
“我问：‘为什么？’

===Original===
“She said, ‘What is your clan?’

===Chinese===
“她说：‘你是什么氏族？’

===Original===
“I told her, ‘I am an Eshlini.’

===Chinese===
“我告诉她：‘我是埃什利尼（Eshlini）。’

===Original===
“She lowered her head, then she looked up again. Her face looked calm, but her eyes were wounded. ‘I, too, am an Eshlini,’ she said.

===Chinese===
“她低下了头，随后又抬了起来。她脸上看着平静，眼睛却是受了伤的。‘我也是个埃什利尼，’她说。

===Original===
“We touched hands, and I rode away.”

===Chinese===
“我们碰了碰手，我便骑马走了。”

===Original===
Jesting Squaw’s Son bowed his head on his knees. Laughing Boy felt his throat hurt, and yet in a curious way he felt better than he had in a long time. He was taken out of himself; he needed something like this.

===Chinese===
打趣婆娘之子把头伏在膝上。笑娃觉得喉咙发疼，可奇怪的是，他倒觉得比许久以来都松快。他被从自身里拔了出来；他正需要这样一件事。

===Original===
“I could not go home then. I rode to T’o Atinda Haska Mesa, and went up to the top of it. I have been there a day and two nights. I did not eat. Why should I?

===Chinese===
“那时我没法回家。我骑马去了特奥·阿廷达·哈斯卡台地（T’o Atinda Haska Mesa），一直上到顶上。我在那里待了一天两夜。我没有吃东西。何必吃呢？

===Original===
“At first I did not even think. I was just wild at first. All I could do was remember that happiness, that had been for nothing. I felt like asking her to come with me even so. I frightened myself. Am I an animal? Would I sleep with my sister? I did not know what to do. Why could she not have been a Tahtchini or a Lucau or an Eskhontsoni? But it was not her fault. And could I curse my mother because she was not a Bitahni or a T’o Dotsoni or a Nahkai?

===Chinese===
“起初我连想都不会想。起初我只是疯了。我唯一能做的，就是回想那份欢喜，那份落了空的欢喜。我甚至想就那样求她跟我一起走。我把自己吓着了。我是头畜生吗？我要同自己的姐妹同床吗？我不知道该怎么办。她怎么就不能生在塔奇尼氏、卢考（Lucau）氏，或是埃斯孔佐尼（Eskhontsoni）氏？可这不是她的错。难道我母亲不是比塔尼氏、不是特奥·多佐尼（T’o Dotsoni）氏、不是纳凯（Nahkai）氏，我就能咒她吗？

===Original===
“Then I got myself calmer. I could not have her. I made up my mind to it. I accepted it. But I still loved her. I still do. I still remember that happiness.

===Chinese===
“后来我让自己静了下来。我不能得到她。我对此拿定了主意。我认了。可我仍然爱她。现在仍然爱。我仍然记得那份欢喜。

===Original===
“That is very bad, it is beastly. My heart must be bad. I am frightened. Perhaps I should kill myself. Why not?

===Chinese===
“这很坏，这是兽性。我的心一定是坏了。我害怕。也许我该杀了自己。为什么不该呢？

===Original===
“I came here to see you. I did not want to go home to all my people. Perhaps you can help me. That is all.”

===Chinese===
“我上这儿来看你。我不想回家去见我满家的亲人。兴许你能帮我。就是这些。”

===Original===
Laughing Boy stared into the ground. He was shocked, and his heart was wrung. He had never imagined that such a thing could happen; had it been told him of some unknown man, he would have supposed there was something bad about him to start with. It was such a disaster as an angry god might send, as though one heard in some legend, “He went mad and fell in love with a woman of his own dan.” But his friend was good, all good. He knew what he was suffering. He remembered his feelings those first days at the dance. He thought hard. They must have sat for half an hour there before he spoke.

===Chinese===
笑娃两眼盯进地里。他震惊，心像被拧了一把。他从没想过会有这样的事；这话若是说给他听、说的是个不相识的人，他准会认定那人起初就带着什么坏处。这灾祸就像一位发怒的神降下的一般，仿佛人在哪则传说里听到的：“他疯了，爱上了自己同氏族的女人。”可他的朋友是好的，彻头彻尾的好。他懂得他此刻受的是怎样的苦。他想起自己那些日子刚到舞会上时的心绪。他苦苦地想。两人必定在那里坐了半个钟头，他才开口。

===Original===
“Do not kill yourself. And do not feel ashamed, do not think you have sinned, or your heart is bad. No, you have shown it is good, I think. It would be bad if you kept on wanting to marry her, but what has happened to you is not something you do yourself. It is as though you were shot with an arrow.

===Chinese===
“不要杀你自己。也不要觉得羞耻，不要想你犯了罪，或是你的心坏了。不，你已经显出它是好的了，我想。坏的是你若一门心思想娶她不肯罢休；可落在你身上的这事，不是你自己做出来的。这就像你叫箭射中了。

===Original===
“I nearly went away with my wife without asking her clan. We spoke directly to each other, without shame, when we saw there was nothing else to do.

===Chinese===
“我差一点没问我妻子的氏族，就带她走了。等看出再没有别的法子，我们便直接同对方把话说了，谁也没觉着羞。

===Original===
“It is not your fault that you were shot. Suppose you had starved for a week, and some American, trying to be funny, the way they do, offered you fish to eat. If you ate it, it would be bad, but if your belly clamoured for it while you refused it, could you be blamed? No, you would have done a good thing, I think. You have done a good thing, a very hard thing. I think well of you.”

===Chinese===
“箭射中了你，不是你的错。比方说你饿了一个礼拜，有个美国人照他们那种样子存心逗乐，拿鱼给你吃。你要是吃了，那是坏事；可你一边拒着不吃，一边肚子叫唤着要，能怪你吗？不能，我看你反倒做了一件好事。你做成了一件好事，一件极难的事。我看得起你。”

===Original===
Jesting Squaw’s Son gazed at him searchingly, and saw that he meant what he said.

===Chinese===
打趣婆娘之子细细审视着他，看出他说的是真心话。

===Original===
“I think you are right. You have cured me of a deep wound. Thank you.”

===Chinese===
“我想你说得对。你把我一处很深的伤治好了。谢谢你。”

===Original===
“Let us start home. There are some of my horses in that little canyon, we shall get one, and turn yours loose. It looks thin. There is pasture there, it will not wander.”

===Chinese===
“咱们动身回家吧。我的几匹马就在那小峡谷里，咱们去牵一匹，把你这匹撒开。它看着瘦了。那儿有草场，它不会乱跑。”

===Original===
They caught fresh horses, and Jesting Squaw’s Son exclaimed at the height of the grass, which in some places grew over a foot, in clumps. There was some like that at Dennihuitso, and in Kiet Siel Buckho, but not at this time of year.

===Chinese===
两人捉了新马，打趣婆娘之子对着草的高度惊叹起来，有些地方一丛一丛，长过了一英尺。德内霍佐有这样的草，基特西尔·巴克霍（Kiet Siel Buckho）也有，可都不是在这个时节。

===Original===
They jog-trotted towards Chiziai, silent most of the time, talking occasionally.

===Chinese===
两人朝奇扎伊一路颠着小跑去，多半时候沉默，偶尔说上几句。

===Original===
“Up there, now, they do not call you by your old name,” Jesting Squaw’s Son said, and hesitated. Even when he is a close friend, one is not free about discussing a man’s name before him.

===Chinese===
“如今在上头那边，他们不叫你从前的名字了，”打趣婆娘之子说，迟疑着。哪怕是最亲近的朋友，当着本人的面议论他的名字，也不是能随便的事。

===Original===
“I am not surprised.”

===Chinese===
“我不意外。”

===Original===
“They call you ‘Went Away.’ Your uncle calls you ‘Blind Eyes.’ ”

===Chinese===
“他们叫你‘出走了’。你舅舅叫你‘瞎眼’。”

===Original===
“Unh! He would. Well, I am changed, it is right that my name should change.”

===Chinese===
“哼！他准会。也罢，我是变了，名字跟着变，正当如此。”

===Original===
Jesting Squaw’s Son trailed his rope to get the kinks out of it. Coiling it again, “But they miss you. You will always be welcome.”

===Chinese===
打趣婆娘之子把绳子拖在地上走，把绳里的扭劲顺开。再盘起来时，他说：“可他们惦记你。你什么时候回去都受欢迎。”

===Original===
“In the end, we shall return.”

===Chinese===
“到头来，我们会回去的。”

===Original===
“You live close to the Iron Trail?”

===Chinese===
“你们就住在铁道近旁？”

===Original===
“On the other side of it.”

===Chinese===
“在铁道那一边。”

===Original===
“*Ei-yei!* A good place?”

===Chinese===
“咿-耶！地方好吗？”

===Original===
“You will see, a fine place, but we cannot turn our horses out there, as it is Americans’ country.”

===Chinese===
“你会看见的，一块好地方，只是我们不能把马撒出去放，那是美国人的地界。”

===Original===
“But you are near the Zunis, too.”

===Chinese===
“可你们离祖尼人也近。”

===Original===
“About a day’s hard ride that way. I trade with them﻿—horses for turquoise.”

===Chinese===
“朝那边紧赶约莫一天马程。我跟他们做买卖——拿马换绿松石。”

===Original===
“Have you any children?”

===Chinese===
“你们有孩子了吗？”

===Original===
“Not now. We have a plan. We are making much money now, we are working as hard as we can. You would not believe how fast we make it. In a year or two we shall return to T’o Tlakai; we shall have perhaps fifty, perhaps sixty hundreds of dollars, in money and silver and horses, I think.”

===Chinese===
“眼下没有。我们有个打算。如今我们挣的钱很多，活干得不能再拼。你不会相信我们挣钱有多快。一两年内我们就回特奥·特莱凯；到那时候，兴许有五十个一百块，兴许六十个一百块，钱、银子和马合算在一起，我想。”

===Original===
“*Ei-yei!*”

===Chinese===
“咿-耶！”

===Original===
“We shall be very rich. With that to start on, we shall be rich all our lives. We shall have our children then, we shall have a beautiful life. It is her idea, she thought of it. She takes care of the money, she trades with the Americans. She is remarkable.”

===Chinese===
“我们会很富。有这个打底，我们一辈子都富。到那时我们要有自己的孩子，我们要过美的日子。这是她的主意，是她想出来的。钱归她管，同美国人做买卖也是她去。她了不起。”

===Original===
“You must be very happy.”

===Chinese===
“你们一定很快活。”

===Original===
“I am.” He meant it.

===Chinese===
“我是。”他说的是真心话。

### II / II

===Original===
Laughing Boy showed off the town, the irrigated strip, the railroad to his interested friend. Most delightfully, a passenger train went by; Jesting Squaw’s Son sat his bolting, bucking horse with his head over his shoulder, his eyes glued on the marvel. His presence changed everything. Laughing Boy led him through the narrow place between the clay bluffs to his adobe house, the corral, the ditches and the hummocks of the summer’s field, the sapling peaches. Jesting Squaw’s Son admired, and a pain ran through him that there was not his own house and fields, and his own wife waiting by the fire.

===Chinese===
笑娃领着兴致盎然的朋友，把镇子、水浇的地带、铁道一一显摆给他看。最开心的是正好有一列客车驶过；打趣婆娘之子骑在又惊又尥的马上，头扭过肩膀，眼睛牢牢粘在那奇景上。他一来，样样都变了样。笑娃领着他穿过泥崖间那道窄口子，去看自己的土坯房、栏圈、水渠，夏天庄稼地里的小土墩，还有那些幼桃树。打趣婆娘之子一路赞叹，一阵酸痛掠过心头——这不是他自己的屋子和田地，火边候着的也不是他自己的婆娘。

===Original===
Slim Girl came to the door. The autumn nights were already cold enough for the cooking to be done indoors. She greeted the visitor correctly, hospitably, and saw that her husband, although he seemed grave, was at peace with himself.

===Chinese===
瘦姑娘来到门口。秋夜已经凉得够呛，做饭都挪进屋里来了。她礼数周全、殷勤热络地迎了客，又看出丈夫虽然神色沉郁，心里却是安宁的。

===Original===
At the first moment when they were alone, Laughing Boy explained the situation, watching her anxiously. She nodded her head.

===Chinese===
一逮着两人独处的头一个空当，笑娃便把事情讲了，不安地望着她。她点了点头。

===Original===
“Poor man, I am sorry for him. We must help him. He is going to get over his love, I think. He is already reconciled to it. It is that in combination with the other that worries him, I think.” Her husband, after a moment’s thought, agreed. “Now this is what we must do; talk about what will keep him interested, talk about things you have done together, talk of what will remind him of the good taste of life in his mouth. Do not try to make him laugh, do not try to comfort him. We shall show him new things. I shall give him some of your drink, I shall talk about the Americans. Now, I think, he is keeping one thing in his mind all the time, we must make him let go of it. Do you see?”

===Chinese===
“可怜的人，我替他难过。咱们得帮帮他。他这份爱会过去的，我想。他已经同它讲和了。叫他发愁的是这份爱和那另一桩事缠在一处，我想。”她丈夫想了片刻，赞同了。“咱们要这么办：拣他感兴趣的说，说你们一起做过的事，说那些能把生活的好滋味重新带回他嘴里的事。别逗他笑，也别劝他宽心。咱们让他见识新鲜东西。我会给他一点你那种酒喝，我来同他讲美国人。眼下，我想，他心里一直攥着一件事不肯撒手，咱们得叫他把手松开。你明白吗？”

===Original===
“Yes, that is very good.”

===Chinese===
“是，这主意很好。”

===Original===
Truly, his wife was a remarkable woman, so wise, so right. Hearing his friend returning, he kissed her quickly.

===Chinese===
说真的，他妻子是个了不起的女人，这样有见识，这样样样在理。听见朋友回来了，他飞快地吻了她一下。

===Original===
That evening was blissful, so harmonious that in the middle of it Jesting Squaw’s Son excused himself, went down to the corral, and cried into the shoulder of the first available horse. A horse, warm and silky, is very nice to cry into when it stands still. The tears came readily. He had not cried before.

===Chinese===
那一晚过得极是美满，融洽到中途，打趣婆娘之子告了失陪，下到栏圈去，把脸埋进头一匹够得着的马的肩头哭了一场。一匹马，只要站着不动，暖暖和和、缎子似的，是顶好哭的地方。眼泪来得顺畅。他先前是没有哭过的。

===Original===
He stayed for three weeks, riding the range with Laughing Boy, watching the silversmithing, going down to see the trains pass by. He spent an entranced and delighted afternoon behind a bush, watching three negroes shoot craps, and nearly frightened them to death when he stood up suddenly, not five feet from them, bow in hand, to go away. He forgot about Alkali Water’s daughter for hours at a time, until she became a curious, sad memory. He gave much thought to his hosts.

===Chinese===
他一住住了三个礼拜，跟着笑娃骑马跑牧场，看他打银器，下山去看火车驶过。有一个下午，他躲在灌木丛后头，看三个黑人掷骰子赌钱，看得神魂颠倒、其乐无穷；后来要走，他手里提着弓，忽地在离他们不到五英尺的地方站起身来，险些把他们吓得魂飞魄散。他一连好几个钟头把碱水的女儿忘在脑后，到后来，她成了一段古怪而伤心的记忆。他对主人夫妇思量得很多。

===Original===
The novelty idea had been a good one, and they had plenty to offer, from the railroad and the cocktail, with its taste and surprising effect, to Slim Girl’s talk of Americans. At night she spoke of their ways, of California, and of the other nations of people like Americans of whom she had heard, across Wide Water, toning down the more amazing things to credibility. They compared her knowledge with their experience on the reservation, and discussed the Americans’ works, the good and bad things their coming had brought to the Navajo. They talked about the posse that hunted Blunt Nose, and stories of old times and the soldiers. That would lead to old wars with the Utes and the Jicarillas and the Stone House people, and they argued whether they gained or lost under the present enforced peace. Laughing Boy and Slim Girl enjoyed themselves enormously.

===Chinese===
用新鲜事开解的主意出得不错，他们的新鲜事也多得很，从铁道和鸡尾酒——连同那滋味和出人意料的后劲——到瘦姑娘讲美国人的种种。夜里她讲他们的风俗，讲加利福尼亚，讲她听说过的、宽水彼岸那些同美国人相仿的族类，把那些过于骇人的事说得合乎情理些。他们把她讲的见闻同自己在保留地上的经历两相比对，谈论美国人的种种作为，谈他们来了给纳瓦霍人带来的好处和坏处。他们谈起追捕钝鼻子的那队人马，谈起旧日的故事和当兵的。这就引到从前同尤特人、希卡里拉人（Jicarillas）和石屋人（Stone House people）打过的那些旧仗，又争辩在眼下这强按下去的太平底下，他们究竟是得了还是失了。笑娃和瘦姑娘自己也快活极了。

===Original===
It was cold enough for a blanket over the shoulder, the day that Jesting Squaw’s Son and Laughing Boy rode out to the pasture and caught his horse. Laughing Boy was sad at his friend’s departure. They mounted their animals and clasped hands.

===Chinese===
到打趣婆娘之子同笑娃骑马出去、到牧场上牵回他那匹马的那天，天已经凉得要往肩上披毯子了。朋友要走了，笑娃心里难过。两人跨上各自的牲口，紧紧握了手。

===Original===
“I shall wait for you in the North.”

===Chinese===
“我在北边等你们。”

===Original===
“We shall come, but I hope you will visit us here again.”

===Chinese===
“我们会来的，可我也盼你往后再来我们这儿做客。”

===Original===
“I hope you will come too soon for that. I have lived in your house, I have seen you. You are both happy, I think; you are both in love. But you are afraid. All the time you are enjoying yourselves you are watching for something over your shoulders, I think. I do not understand this. It is what I saw. This life of yours, it all looks like The People’s life; only her going into town is strange. But it is not just she, it is you both that are not living like us, I think. I do not know what it is, but you are wearing moccasins that do not fit you. The sooner you both come back to your own people, the better, I think.

===Chinese===
“我盼你们回来得比那更快。我在你们家住了这些天，我把你们看在眼里了。你们俩是快活的，我想；你们俩还相爱着。可是你们在害怕。你们一路快活着，一路却在提防着肩膀后头的什么，我想。这个我不懂。这是我亲眼见的。你们这日子，样样看着都像迪内人的日子；只有她进城里去这一桩透着怪。可又不止是她一个，是你们俩都过得不像我们了，我想。我说不清那是什么，可你们脚上穿的鹿皮靴不合脚。你们俩越早回到自己人当中去，越好，我想。

===Original===
“I shall be waiting for you. You have restored my life.”

===Chinese===
“我会等着你们。你们把我的性命还给了我。”

===Original===
“It will be a good day when we meet again.”

===Chinese===
“咱们再见面的那天，会是个好日子。”

===Original===
It was a pity he was gone; he had been such pleasant company. They had been too much alone, and he had cured that. He had misunderstood it, too. He wanted to see his wife and talk about their guest. He hurried home.

===Chinese===
他走了，可惜了的；他是个多么叫人愉快的伙伴。他们两口子先前太孤单了，这一层他治好了。可这一层他也看岔了。他要见妻子，同她谈谈他们这位客人。他急急忙忙往家赶去。

## Chapter XVIII / 第十八章

### I / I

===Original===
It had come round to the beginning of Little Snow Moon again, a time of year when horses, seeking feed, are likely to wander. Laughing Boy kept close watch on his herd, and was little surprised, on one day of high wind that covered the tracks, to find a stallion, a three-year-old, missing. It must have been gone for some time; he was unable to find it in the immediate district, and soon lost its trail completely. Returning to his house, he made preparations to be away for a week in search of it; the animal was valuable.

===Chinese===
又转回到小雪月（Little Snow Moon）开头的时候了，一年里这个时节，马儿四下觅食，最容易走散。笑娃把自家的马群看得很紧，所以那天风大、蹄印全叫风刮没之后，他发现少了一匹三岁的儿马，心里倒也没怎么吃惊。它想必已经跑出去有些时日了；近处一带他找不见，很快又把它的踪迹彻底跟丢了。回到家里，他备办起一应物件，准备出门寻它一个礼拜；这牲口值钱。

===Original===
Slim Girl procured chocolate and other dainties for him. The weather was no longer warm, he could not tell where he might camp, she felt that he would undergo hardships. But, as he said, one could not let as good a pony as that wander at will in a country entirely populated by connoisseurs and lovers of horseflesh.

===Chinese===
瘦姑娘给他张罗来巧克力和各样可口的小食。天气已经不算暖和，他又说不准会在哪儿落脚露宿，她觉得他这趟免不了要吃苦。可是，照他的说法，那样一匹好小马，由着它在一片满地住着相马的行家、爱马成痴之人的地方随意乱跑，总归是不成的。

===Original===
Four days passed in vain. On the fifth, acting on the tip of a Hopi mail carrier, he picked up its trail north of Winslow. The next morning he found it, scarcely fifteen miles from Los Palos.

===Chinese===
白跑了四天，一无所获。第五天，依着一个霍皮邮差指点的信儿，他在温斯洛（Winslow）以北重新寻到了它的蹄迹。第二天早上他找见了它，离洛斯帕洛斯（Los Palos）还不到十五英里。

===Original===
It had no mind to go back to the herd. At first sight of him it began walking as it grazed, then, seeing him draw slightly nearer, broke into a trot, and thus all morning, matching its pace to his, kept a quarter of a mile between them. He tried to edge it towards the left, but it seemed to guess his intention, taking advantage of a butte that prevented heading off to break sharply right and gallop furiously a mile in the direction of the railroad. It was never panicked, never too hurried, expending always just enough effort.

===Chinese===
它压根儿不想回马群去。一望见他，它便一边啃草一边往前蹭，待见他稍稍逼近，就碎步小跑起来；就这样兜了一上午，它拿自己的步子配着他的步子，同他始终隔着四分之一英里。他想法子往左边挤压它，它却像是猜中了他的心思，借一座教人绕不到前头去拦的方山一遮挡，猛地折向右边，朝铁路的方向狂奔了一英里。它从不惊慌失措，也从不慌不择路地赶急，费的力气总是不多不少，刚刚够用。

===Original===
As he pursued, Laughing Boy admired. The chestnut stallion was coming into its strength, gleaming, round quarters, bunched muscles at the juncture of the throat and chest, a ripple of highlight and shadow on the withers, arched neck, pricked small Arab ears, bony head, eyes and nostrils of character and intelligence. It was one of those ponies, occasionally to be found, in which one reads a page of the history of that country; a throwback to Spanish Conquistadores and dainty-hooved, bony-faced horses from Arabia.

===Chinese===
一路追着，笑娃心里暗暗喝彩。那匹栗毛儿马正当力气长足的时候，浑圆的后躯油光水滑，咽喉与前胸相接处肌肉一坨坨隆起，耆甲上流过一道道高光和暗影，颈项弯成一张弓，一双阿拉伯种的小耳朵直直立着，头颅瘦骨嶙峋，眼睛和鼻孔里透着性子，透着灵性。这是那种偶尔才能遇上一次的小马，从它身上能读出这片乡土历史上的一页；一次返祖，返回了西班牙征服者和那些蹄子玲珑、脸孔瘦削的阿拉伯骏马。

===Original===
Midday was warm, sandy dust rose from the trail in clouds. Laughing Boy munched raisins and chocolate as he rode, remembering when the men on the posse had offered him the same rations. That girl, she was a whole war-party in herself! The stallion balked at the railroad tracks, considered, and cleared them with a nervous leap.

===Chinese===
晌午暖洋洋的，一路的沙尘一团团腾起。笑娃边骑边嚼葡萄干和巧克力，想起当初追捕队上那些人也拿这同样的口粮款待过他。那个姑娘啊，她一个人就是整整一支出征队！儿马到了铁轨跟前犯了踌躇，掂量了一下，提心吊胆地一跃而过。

===Original===
Now Laughing Boy thought he had it; the dingy suburbs of the town, on the far side from his hogan, made a half-circle before them. He advanced cautiously. It was a question of getting it cornered so that he could dismount, for Navajos do not rope from the saddle. Now the stallion began to rush, and the work became fast﻿—a break to the right, Laughing Boy, pouring leather into his pony, headed it, then left, and the houses turned it again. A desperate race to prevent a desperate attempt to break back across the tracks; it wheeled again, straight between two houses, stallion and mounted man going like fury, to the admiration of an old Mexican woman and the clamorous terror of a sleeping cur.

===Chinese===
这会儿笑娃想着它逃不脱了；镇子边上那片灰扑扑的郊野房舍，在他霍根屋（hogan，纳瓦霍人泥木筑的小屋）的那一面，在人和马前方张成半个圆。他小心地逼上去。要紧的是把它逼进死角，好教他下得马来，因为纳瓦霍人不坐在鞍上甩套索。儿马这时开始横冲直撞，活计一下变快——它往右一挣，笑娃狠命催起小马，抢到前头把它拦住；它再往左，房屋又把它顶了回来。一场拼死的追逐，为的是拦下它一场拼死的、想要折回去再冲过铁轨的企图；它又猛地一转，笔直地从两座房子中间穿过，儿马和骑马的人一路狂飙，看得一个墨西哥老妇人啧啧称赏，吓得一条睡着的野狗汪汪乱吠。

===Original===
The stallion drew away from him, and he slowed his pace. It cantered past an adobe house standing alone under two cottonwoods, and, just beyond, fell to grazing in a little hollow. Laughing Boy advanced cautiously, using the house as cover. He figured that he could dismount behind it, and with a quick rush corner the animal in the angle of two wire fences protecting irrigated fields. The pony was already moving into the trap, unconscious of the wire.

===Chinese===
儿马把他甩开了一截，他便放缓了步子。它压着跑步经过一座土坯房，那房子孤零零立在两棵三角叶杨底下；刚过房子不远，它就在一处小洼地里低头啃起草来。笑娃拿房子作掩护，小心地凑上去。他盘算着可以在房后下马，再来一个疾冲，把牲口逼进护着水浇地的两道铁丝栅栏相夹的死角里。小马已经正朝那陷阱里走去，浑然不觉铁丝为何物。

===Original===
He rode at a walk, close along the mud wall from which the sun was reflected with a stuffy, muddy smell. As he passed the window, he looked in, and reined his horse so suddenly that it reared, while his heart stopped for a moment and his whole body was a great choking. An agonized, clear voice cried out, inside,

===Chinese===
他让马踏着慢步，紧贴泥墙走，太阳从墙上反照过来，带着一股窒闷的泥腥气。经过窗口时，他朝里望了一眼，猛地一带缰，马惊得直立起来，他的心跳停了一拍，整个身子化作一大团窒塞。屋里，一个痛楚而清亮的嗓音喊了出来——

===Original===
“*Sha hast’ien, sha hast’ien codji!*﻿—My husband, my husband there!” And a man said, “My God!”

===Chinese===
“Sha hast’ien, sha hast’ien codji！——我的丈夫，我的丈夫在那儿！”接着有个男人说了声：“我的天哪！”

===Original===
Before he had started thinking, he wheeled and rode madly for the door side. As he came around the corner, an American, hatless, came out, saw man and horse coming upon him, jumped aside and stood for a moment. His hands strung his bow without conscious willing. The man began to run towards the town. Arrow leaped to string almost of itself, hands and arms functioned, drew, released, but the excited pony would not keep still and the missile went wide, to the right. A second was in the air before the first landed, but it passed just over the man’s shoulder, hard by the ear, startling him into an amazing leap and burst of speed. There was something ridiculous about it which calmed Laughing Boy. He steadied his pony and shot with care. The arrow struck just below the shoulder, the American fell doubled up, almost turning a somersault, picked himself up, and with a last effort rounded the corner between the outermost houses at the end of the straggling street.

===Chinese===
他还没来得及转念头，已经拨转马头，朝门那一面疯也似的冲去。他刚绕过屋角，一个没戴帽子的美国人正好走出来，望见人和马当头压到，闪身跳开，怔立了一瞬。他的双手不等他念头转过来，已经自作主张给弓上了弦。那人朝镇子拔腿奔去。箭几乎自己跳上弦，手和臂膀自行运转，开弓，撒放，只是那匹兴奋的小马不肯安分，箭支偏右飞了空。第一支还没落地，第二支已在半空，紧擦那人的肩头掠过，险些碰着耳朵，惊得他爆出一跳惊人的纵跃和冲刺。这情景里有点什么滑稽的东西，倒叫笑娃定下心来。他稳住小马，凝神一箭。箭正中肩胛稍下，那美国人蜷着身子栽倒，险些翻了个跟头，又撑起身来，拼出最后一把力气，拐过了那条拖沓长街尽头最外两座房子之间的屋角。

===Original===
Calmly, he waited before the house. Afterwards there were going to be terrible feelings and thoughts, but now he knew what was to be done. His face showed no particular age, young or old; it was hardly the face of an individual, rather, of a race.

===Chinese===
他在屋前平静地等着。往后自会有种种可怕的滋味和念头，可此刻他明白该做的是什么。他的脸看不出什么年纪，说年轻不年轻，说老不老；那几乎算不得一张哪个人的脸，毋宁说是一张种族的脸。

===Original===
Slim Girl stood in the doorway, neat, dressed in American clothes.

===Chinese===
瘦姑娘站在门洞里，齐齐整整，穿着一身美国衣裳。

===Original===
“Come here, little sister.” Voice even and impersonal.

===Chinese===
“过来，小妹妹。”嗓音平平稳稳，不带一点私人的口气。

===Original===
She walked slowly. For the first time since he had known her, he saw that her self-possession was only a surface. She looked as though a searing light were shining before her, showing her Hell. She stood beside his saddle.

===Chinese===
她慢慢走来。自打认识她以来，他头一回看出，她的镇定原来只是一层表皮。她那样子，就像眼前烧着一道灼人的光，把她的地狱照了个分明。她在他的马鞍旁站住了。

===Original===
“Did you kill him?”

===Chinese===
“你把他杀了吗？”

===Original===
“No, I hit him in the shoulder.”

===Chinese===
“没有，我射中了他的肩膀。”

===Original===
This was the fourth arrow. It was right that such a thing should happen by fours. The gods were in it.

===Chinese===
这是第四支箭。这样的事就该应着四数而来。诸神就在这事里。

===Original===
“You have killed us both, I think.”

===Chinese===
“我看，你是把我们两个都杀了。”

===Original===
She did not answer. He looked at her eyes, then avoided them; not from shame, but because there was too much in them. He did not want to begin to realize yet. He must keep his head. He thought how beautiful she was, and began to feel the greatness of his loss.

===Chinese===
她没有答话。他望着她的眼睛，随即避开了；不是因为羞耻，而是那里头装的东西太多。他还不想这就去领会。他必须稳住自己的头脑。他想到她有多么美，开始觉出这场损失有多大了。

===Original===
“You understand what I am doing?”

===Chinese===
“你明白我这是在做什么吗？”

===Original===
Again she did not answer.

===Chinese===
她还是没有答话。

===Original===
He notched the fourth arrow meticulously, drew to the head, released. The twang of the string echoed and reechoed over great spaces. At the sound, he became aware of agony pent up behind his mind like high waters behind a too-slight dam, about to break through and carry away. At the same time, with the instant of releasing the string, he saw her open right hand pass across the face of the bow, her left arm rise. Now she stood, smiling stiffly, her eyes her own again. Her right hand was still in front of the bow in a stiff, quaint gesture. There was blood on the tips of the fingers. The arrow stood, through nearly to the feathers, in her left forearm.

===Chinese===
他一丝不苟地把第四支箭扣上弦，开弓拉到镞，撒放。弦声铮然，在辽阔的空旷里回荡复回荡。听着这声响，他才觉出憋在自己脑子后头的苦痛，好比一坝过于单薄的堤拦着的高水，眼看就要破堤而下，把一切冲走。与此同时，就在撒放的一瞬，他看见她张开的那只右手横掠过弓面，左臂迎着抬了起来。这会儿她站定了，僵僵地笑着，那双眼睛又归她自己使唤了。她的右手还停在弓前，姿势僵直而古怪。指尖上沾着血。那支箭立在她的左小臂上，穿透得只剩箭羽还留在外面。

===Original===
He saw her as at a great distance. This was all wrong, something impossible had happened. She held her arm up rigidly, her lips remained set in that stiff smile. In a moment she was going to speak. The feelings and realizations were coming upon him. He lifted the reins and rode slowly around the corner of the house.

===Chinese===
他看她，如同隔着老远的一段路。这一切全不对了，一件不可能的事发生了。她直挺挺地举着那条胳膊，嘴唇还锁在那个僵僵的笑上。再过一会儿她就要开口了。种种滋味和领会正朝他压下来。他提起缰绳，绕过屋角，慢慢骑了过去。

===Original===
The stallion watched him nervously.

===Chinese===
儿马不安地望着他。

===Original===
“Go your way, little brother.” He watched the animal as he rode past, then he contemplated the ears of his mount. “You are saddled and ridden, but you are better off than I. This would be a good world if we were all geldings, I think.”

===Chinese===
“走你的路吧，小兄弟。”他骑马经过时望着那牲口，随后又端详起自己胯下这匹马的耳朵。“你是叫人备了鞍、叫人骑着，可你比我强。要是咱们全都成了阉马，这倒会是个好世界，我想。”

### II / II

===Original===
The pony, wandering unguided, brought him slowly within sight of his house. He turned it aside, making a wide circle to come to the high place by the tree from the other side. The house, the field of corn-stubble, the five struggling peach trees, the corral, all very dear, stood like unanswerable refutations in the long streaks of afternoon shadow. As the sight of the perfect, familiar body of someone just dead, or the little possessions, the objects just set down, ready to be picked up again as always, again and again render that death incredible, so was the sight of these things to Laughing Boy. Her loom stood under the brush sun-shelter before the door, with a half-finished blanket rolled at its foot. Unbelievable, not true, only﻿—it was so. He went through the past day, searched the farther past, as though by travelling it again he could find where the false trail branched off, and reduce this calamity to an error.

===Chinese===
小马没人指引，信步游走，慢慢把他驮到望得见自家房子的地方。他把它拨向一旁，兜一个大圈，好从另一面上到那棵树底下的高地。房子，玉米茬子地，五棵挣扎求活的桃树，牲口圈，样样都亲——在下午斜长的一条条阴影里立着，像一句句无从招架的驳斥。正如人刚死，你看见他那完好而熟惯的身体，或者那些小小的随身物件，那些方才还搁下、照老规矩随时会再拿起的东西，一遍又一遍教你觉得这场死不可能是真的；这些东西落在笑娃眼里，也是一样。她的织机立在门前枝条搭的凉棚底下，脚边卷着一条织了一半的毯子。难以相信，不是真的，只是——事情就是这样。他把过去的一天从头过了一遍，又往更远的过去里搜，仿佛把那条路重走一遍，便能找出岔出假踪迹的地方，把这场大祸折算成一场错。

===Original===
Ten thousand things told him that what he had learned was ridiculous, but it always led again to the window in the adobe house and the clear frightened voice crying, “*Sha hast’ien, sha hast’ien codji!*”

===Chinese===
千万件事情都对他说，他得知的那件事荒唐得很，可想着想着，总又绕回那座土坯房的窗口，绕回那个清亮而惊惶的嗓音在喊：“Sha hast’ien, sha hast’ien codji！（我的丈夫，我的丈夫在那儿！）”

===Original===
Now it was time to think, but an hour or more passed before he could prevent the beginnings of thought from turning to frantic revolt. Prayer helped him. He got himself in hand and rolled a cigarette.

===Chinese===
如今该想一想了，可是过了一个多钟头，他才算把思绪的苗头按住，没叫它转成一场发疯的暴动。祈祷帮了他。他把自己收拾住，卷了一支烟。

===Original===
Now I must choose between her and myself. If I stay with her, I lose myself, really. I am a man. I am a warrior. If I do not give her up, I become something else from what I have always been. The world changes, the good things, the bad things, all change for me. And they change for the bad. I cannot shoot her again. I cannot do that thing. If I leave her, I am still I, but I and the world are dead. Oh, my friend, my friend, your choice was so simple, you were lucky. The arrow only grazed you; it has gone through my bowels. And when it came my turn to send the arrow back, I missed.

===Chinese===
如今我得在她和我自己之间作个拣择。我若守着她，我就真把自己丢了。我是个男人。我是个战士。我若不舍弃她，我就成了另一样东西，不再是我一向的那样。世界变了，好的东西，坏的东西，对我全都变了。而且都往坏里变。我不能再用箭射她。那样的事我做不出。我若离开她，我还是我，可我和这世界都死了。唉，我的朋友，我的朋友，你的拣择多么简单，你是走运的。那支箭只擦了你一下；它却穿透了我的肚肠。轮到我回敬这支箭的时候，我射偏了。

===Original===
Oh, well named, Came With War, Came With War, oh, beautiful! Why do they give women names about war? I know all about that now. My uncle was right. I cannot go now and see their faces. Kill myself. That would settle it. But not now, not in this place. If it keeps on being like this, I shall do that, in my own country. Came With War, Came With War, Slim Girl, you coyote, you devil, you bad woman.

===Chinese===
唉，起得好名，携战而来（Came With War），携战而来，唉，美的呀！他们为什么给女人起打仗的名？这里头的道理我如今全懂了。我舅舅说得对。我如今没法去见他们的脸了。杀了我自己。那样倒一了百了。可不是现在，不是这个地方。要是事情老这样下去，我会那么办的，在我自己的家乡。携战而来，携战而来，瘦姑娘，你这郊狼，你这魔鬼，你这坏女人。

===Original===
I must go away. I cannot stay with her. She is worth everything in the world, but there is something in me that I have no right to trade for her. That is what I must do.

===Chinese===
我必须走。我不能守着她。她抵得上世上的一切，可我身上有一样东西，我没有权利拿它去换她。这就是我必须做的。

===Original===
He struggled for a long time, facing this decision, until it sank into him. The sun was low, the little valley between the buttes was all shadow. He had not seen her return, and hoped she had not. There would be begging, talk, tears﻿—terrible. If she were not there, he would just take his things and go; the missing goods would explain.

===Chinese===
他同这个决定对峙着，挣扎了许久，直到它沉进他心里。太阳低了，方山之间那道小谷整个成了影子。他没有见她回来，也盼着她没回来。那将会是哀求，是诉说，是眼泪——可怕。她若不在，他只消拿了自家的物件就走；少了的那些东西，自会把缘故说明白。

===Original===
It was all too much for him. He felt as if he were shaken by high winds. That little house down there was a place of waiting torment. He stood, clutching his hands together and weaving his head from side to side. This was far worse than war. He turned to the gods, making the prayer of a man going alone to battle:

===Chinese===
这一切他受不住了。他觉得自己像叫大风摇撼着。底下那座小屋，是一处等着人的苦刑。他站着，双手死死攥在一处，脑袋左右摇晃。这比打仗坏到不知哪里去了。他转向诸神，做起一个独自上阵的人的祷告：

===Original===
“*Shinahashé nageï, nageï, alili kat’ bitashah﻿ ﻿…*
						

						I am thinking about the enemy gods, the enemy gods, among their weapons now I wander.
						

						
							*A-yé-yé-yé-ya-hai!*
						
						

						Now Slayer of Enemy Gods, I go down alone among them,
						

						The enemy gods, the enemy gods, I wander among their weapons.
						

						Touched with the tops of the mountains, I go down alone among them,
						

						The enemy gods, the enemy gods, I wander among their weapons.
						

						Now on the old age trail, now on the path of beauty walking,
						

						The enemy gods, the enemy gods, I wander among their weapons.”

===Chinese===
“Shinahashé nageï, nageï, alili kat’ bitashah……
						

						我心里想着那些敌神，那些敌神，此刻我在它们的兵刃之间徘徊。
						

						
							啊-耶-耶-耶-呀-嗨！
						
						

						诛敌神者啊，如今我独自一人下到它们中间，
						

						敌神啊，敌神，我在它们的兵刃之间徘徊。
						

						触着群山的峰顶，我独自一人下到它们中间，
						

						敌神啊，敌神，我在它们的兵刃之间徘徊。
						

						如今走在暮年的古道上，如今走在美之路上，
						

						敌神啊，敌神，我在它们的兵刃之间徘徊。”

===Original===
It was apposite, and it helped enormously. Now it was not merely he battling with these terrific things, now the unseen power of good would uphold him. Leading his horse, he went down slowly to his house.

===Chinese===
这祷词正对景，也帮了大忙。如今不再只是他独自一人同这些骇人的东西搏斗，如今那看不见的善的力量会扶持他。他牵着马，慢慢下坡，朝自己的屋子走去。

## Chapter XIX / 第十九章

### I / I

===Original===
There were her tracks, wind-blurred in the sand. She must have come straight home, arriving before he reached the high place. With dread he entered the door, grateful for the half-darkness inside. She had got back into Navajo clothes, moccasins, skirt, and sash, but her blouse was only pulled over the right shoulder, leaving the left arm and breast bare. Did she think﻿—? He saw her as an enemy.

===Chinese===
沙地上有她的脚印，叫风吹得模糊了。她必定是一径直接回了家，赶在他登上高地之前就到了。他怀着惊惧走进门去，亏得屋里半明半暗。她已经换回了纳瓦霍装束，鹿皮靴、裙子、腰带都在，只是那件上衣单单挎在右肩上，左臂和左胸都袒露着。难道她想的是——？他把她看作一个仇敌。

===Original===
“I am going away.”

===Chinese===
“我要走了。”

===Original===
“All right. But first pull this out; I am not strong enough.” She held out her arm with the arrow through it.

===Chinese===
“行。可先把这个给我拔出来；我力气不够。”她伸出胳膊，那支箭还穿在胳膊上。

===Original===
He stared at it, and it made him feel sick. He was frankly avoiding her face, but he knew that the blood was gone from beneath the bronze surface, leaving it yellow-white with a green tinge under it. He kept on looking at the arrow, his arrow, with his marks on it.

===Chinese===
他盯着它，看得心里发呕。他索性不去看她的脸，可他知道，那层青铜色底下血色已经褪尽，剩下一片黄白，底下透着青。他只顾盯着那支箭，他的箭，上头有他亲手做的记号。

===Original===
“You must come out to the light.”

===Chinese===
“你得到亮处来。”

===Original===
She rose with difficulty, steadying herself against the wall. He supported her to the door.

===Chinese===
她艰难地站起身，扶着墙稳住自己。他搀着她走到门口。

===Original===
The arrow had passed through the flesh of the under side of her arm, just missing the artery and the bone. The shaft stood out on both sides. From the barbed, iron head to the wound there was blood in the zigzag lightning grooves. The roundness of her arm was caked with dried blood and already somewhat swollen. To the one side was the barbed point, to the other were the eagle feathers and the wrappings. He took out his knife.

===Chinese===
箭穿透了她手臂下侧的肉，差一点就伤着动脉和骨头。箭杆两头支棱在外。从带倒钩的铁镞直到伤口，一道道之字形的闪电刻槽里都凝着血。她那条浑圆的胳膊结满了干血痂，已经有些肿了。一头是倒钩的镞尖，另一头是鹰羽和缠绳。他掏出了刀。

===Original===
“I shall try not to make it wiggle,” he said.

===Chinese===
“我尽量不叫它晃动。”他说。

===Original===
“What are you going to do?”

===Chinese===
“你要做什么？”

===Original===
“Cut it off just by the hole; I can’t pull all that through your arm.”

===Chinese===
“就在洞口边上把它削断；那么一大截，我没法整个从你胳膊里拽过去。”

===Original===
“It is a good arrow. Pull it through.”

===Chinese===
“这是支好箭。给我整个拽过去吧。”

===Original===
There was never another woman like this one. “Do you think I would use this again?”

===Chinese===
世上再没有第二个这样的女人了。“你当我会再用它吗？”

===Original===
He held her arm very carefully, he cut with all possible gentleness, but the shaft moved and moved again. He heard her take in her breath and looked quickly to see her teeth clenched on her lower lip. She should have been a man. Every dart of pain in her arm went doubly through his heart. The wood was cut short, just above the wound.

===Chinese===
他小心翼翼地捧住她的胳膊，下刀轻得不能再轻，可箭杆还是动了动，又动了动。他听见她倒吸一口气，急忙抬眼看去，见她的牙关紧紧咬着下唇。她本该生成个男人才对。她胳膊上每闪过一阵痛，都加倍地扎过他的心。木杆削短了，就齐着伤口上头。

===Original===
“Now,” he said, “are you ready?”

===Chinese===
“好了，”他说，“你预备好了吗？”

===Original===
“Pull.”

===Chinese===
“拔。”

===Original===
He jerked it out. She had not moved. She was rigid and her eyes were almost glassy, but she had not made a sign. He still knelt, staring at her, at the fresh blood welling, and at the red stump of the arrow in his hand. She was brave, brave.

===Chinese===
他猛地一下拔了出来。她一动也没有动。她僵直着，眼睛几乎发了呆，可她没有露一点声色。他还跪在那里，怔怔地望着她，望着涌出来的新血，望着手里那截血红的断箭杆。她勇敢，勇敢。

===Original===
She whispered, “Get me some of the whiskey.”

===Chinese===
她低声说：“给我倒点威士忌。”

===Original===
He gave her a stiff dose in a cup. She emptied it at once, and sighed. A little colour came back.

===Chinese===
他拿杯子给她倒了满满一剂。她一口气喝干，叹了口气。脸上回来了一点血色。

===Original===
“It will be dark soon. You had better go now. I can take care of myself. But before you go, know this: whatever you have seen, I love you and you only and altogether. Goodbye.”

===Chinese===
“天快黑了。你这会儿最好走吧。我自己照顾得了自己。可你走以前，要明白这个：不管你看见了什么，我爱你，爱的只是你一个，完完全全是你一个。再见。”

===Original===
She handed him back the cup. As he took it, their fingers touched, and he looked into her eyes. Something snapped inside of him. He fell forward, his head close to his knees, and began sobbing. She laid her hand on his shoulder.

===Chinese===
她把杯子递还给他。他接的时候，两人的手指碰了碰，他望进了她的眼睛。他心里有什么东西啪地一声断了。他身子往前一栽，头几乎抵到膝盖，抽抽噎噎地哭了起来。她把手搁在他肩上。

===Original===
“You have been hasty, I think. One should not turn up a new trail without looking around. And you have not eaten, you are tired. This has been hard for you. In a minute I shall heat some coffee, and we can talk straight about this.”

===Chinese===
“你太急了，我想。人不该连四下里都不瞧一眼，就照直踏上一条新路。再说你还没吃东西，你也乏了。这一场对你不容易。我这就去热一点咖啡，咱们把这桩事好好说个明白。”

### II / II

===Original===
The night was plenty sharp enough for a fire indoors. Under her directions he prepared canned goods and coffee, but neither of them did more than toy with the food. He had a feeling that she was going to find a solution for them; the experience that they had just shared had changed everything again, he didn’t know where he was. Landmarks shifted too quickly, he was in a turmoil once more, with his determinations to be made anew.

===Chinese===
夜里寒气扎人，足够在屋内生火了。他照她的指点备下罐头和咖啡，可两个人都只是把吃食拨弄着玩。他有一种感觉，觉得她会替他们俩找出一条出路来；方才两人一同经过的那一场又把一切都改变了，他不知道自己到了什么地步。地标挪移得太快，他又一次心乱如麻，种种决断都得重新拿主意。

===Original===
She asked him to roll her a cigarette; then,

===Chinese===
她叫他替她卷一支烟；随后，

===Original===
“Make the drink as you have seen me do, only make some for me, too.”

===Chinese===
“照你见我调过的那样把那酒调上，只是也给我调一份。”

===Original===
He hesitated.

===Chinese===
他迟疑了。

===Original===
“Do not be afraid of my medicine.”

===Chinese===
“别怕我的药。”

===Original===
He muttered a denial and fixed the drink. She sipped at hers slowly. She needed strength, for she was nearly exhausted, and there was a battle to be fought.

===Chinese===
他咕哝了一声否认，把酒调好了。她慢慢啜着自己那杯。她需要力气，因为她差不多已经耗干了，何况还有一场仗要打。

===Original===
“You cannot know whether a thing is good or bad unless you know all about it, and the cause of it. I do not try to say that what I have done is good, but I want to tell you my story, that you do not know; then you can judge rightly.”

===Chinese===
“你没法知道一件事是好是坏，除非你把它里里外外都弄清楚，连它的缘由也弄清楚。我不想说我做下的事是好的，可我要把我的经历讲给你听，那是你不知道的；这样你就能断得公正。”

===Original===
He hardly had expected her to come so directly to the point. He prepared to sift lies.

===Chinese===
他没料到她会这样开门见山。他打定主意，要从里头筛出谎来。

===Original===
“Roll me a cigarette.

===Chinese===
“给我卷支烟。

===Original===
“I have to begin way back. Hear me.

===Chinese===
“我得打老远讲起。你听着。

===Original===
“When I was still a little girl, they took me away to the all-year school at Wide Water, as you know. They took me because I did well at the day school at Zhil Tséchiel, so they wanted me to learn more. I told you how they tried to make us not be Indians; they succeeded pretty well. I wanted to be American. I forgot the gods then, I followed the Jesus trail. I did well, then, at that school.

===Chinese===
“我还是个小丫头的时候，他们把我带走，送进宽水（Wide Water）的全年学堂，这你是知道的。他们带我走，是因为我在吉尔·特塞奇尔（Zhil Tséchiel）的走读学堂里念得好，他们要我多学些东西。我跟你讲过，他们是怎样想法子叫我们不做印第安人；他们干得相当成功。我那时想当美国人。我那时把诸神都忘了，走的是耶稣之路（Jesus Road）。在那所学堂里，我那时也念得好。

===Original===
“While I was there both my father and mother went underground. My mother had no brothers or sisters living, and I was her only child. I saw no reason for returning to The People. I was an American, with an American name, thinking in American.

===Chinese===
“我在那儿的时候，我爹和我娘双双入了土。我娘没有兄弟姊妹在世，我又是我娘唯一的孩子。我看不出有什么道理回到迪内（The People）中间去。我是个美国人，顶着一个美国名字，拿美国话想心思。

===Original===
“I grew up. I wanted to work for Washindon on a reservation, like that Papago woman who writes papers for the American Chief at T’o Nanasdési. But I could not get that work right away, so they said I could work for a preacher at Kien Doghaiyoi﻿—you know that big town? The Americans call it Oñate.”

===Chinese===
“我长大了。我想在保留地上替瓦辛顿（Washindon）做事，就像托纳纳斯代西（T’o Nanasdési）那边替美国酋长（American Chief）写文书的那个帕帕戈（Papago）女人。可那样的差事一时轮不上我，于是他们说，我可以到基恩·多盖约伊（Kien Doghaiyoi）去，替一位牧师做事——那座大镇子你知道吧？美国人管它叫奥尼亚特（Oñate）。”

===Original===
“I have heard.” He was studying her intently. Her voice came low and toneless; she spoke slowly, but behind it was something intense.

===Chinese===
“我听说过。”他正凝神端量着她。她的声音低而平板；她说得慢，可那后面有一种紧绷着的东西。

===Original===
“I went there, about three years ago. I loved the Jesus trail; I thought it was very good to work for a preacher. That way it was.”

===Chinese===
“我去那儿，是大约三年前的事。我爱耶稣之路；我想着能给一位牧师做事是顶好的。事情就是那样。”

===Original===
She stared into the fire as she took a sip of liquor.

===Chinese===
她啜了一口酒，眼睛盯进火里。

===Original===
“He was a good man, and his wife was very good. He did not let her have much to say. I worked pretty hard, but it was all right.

===Chinese===
“他是个好人，他太太也很好。他不许她多嘴。我干得挺苦，不过也还过得去。

===Original===
“I learned some strange things. I learned about the bad women﻿—they make their living by lying down with men, just any men who will pay them. Some of them were Americans, some had been schoolgirls like me. The preacher used to preach against them sometimes; I thought, he did not need to do that. Something had happened to their faces, their eyes; their mouths were terrible. They were like something in a bad dream. That way I thought.

===Chinese===
“我学了些怪事。我知道了那些坏女人——她们靠同男人睡挣饭吃，只要是肯给钱的男人，谁都可以。她们中间有的是美国人，有的从前是跟我一样的学堂女生。那牧师有时讲道还骂她们；我心想，他用不着这样。她们的脸上、眼睛里都遭了什么变故；那一张张嘴怕人得很。像噩梦里的什么东西。我那时是那样想的。

===Original===
“Then by and by I fell in love with a man. He was big and good-looking and he talked pious. He was a cowpuncher; he worked on a ranch near there. Lots of American girls liked him. When he paid attention to me, I was flattered. He was wonderful, I thought. We should be married and have a ranch together; it was almost too good to believe, I thought.

===Chinese===
“后来过了一些时候，我爱上了一个男人。他高大，俊俏，说话虔虔诚诚。他是个放牛的；在附近一座牧场上做工。多少美国姑娘都喜欢他。他看上我的时候，我受宠若惊。他真了不起，我想。咱们该成了亲，一起守一座牧场过日子；这好得叫人几乎不敢信，我想。

===Original===
“I was frightened when he wanted me to lie with him, but he made me feel all right. He knew all about how to make women forget themselves, that man.

===Chinese===
“他要我同他睡的时候我害怕，可他有法子叫我安了心。怎样叫女人忘了自己，那个男人一整套全懂。

===Original===
“Then I saw I was going to have a child. The next time he came to town, I asked him to marry me quickly. He made promises. Then he didn’t come to town again, so I went to the ranch where he worked. He was angry when he saw me there. He offered me money, but I said I wanted marriage.

===Chinese===
“后来我看出自己有了身子。他下一回进城，我求他快些同我成亲。他满口应承。后来他不再进城了，我就寻到他做工的牧场去。他见我到了那儿就动了气。他掏钱给我，我说我要的是婚姻。

===Original===
“I became frightened, I begged and I cried. He got very angry, he called me names. He said to get out of his way, he couldn’t be bothered with a ‘squaw.’ That is a word Americans use to mean Indian women; it is contemptuous. I learned a lot then; right then I was not so young as I had been, I think.

===Chinese===
“我害了怕，我求他，我哭。他火透了，张口骂我。他叫我滚开别挡道，说他没工夫缠一个‘婆娘’。这个词是美国人拿来叫印第安女人的；带轻贱的意思。我那时长了许多见识；就在那时候，我已经不像先前那么年轻了，我想。

===Original===
“I went back to the preacher’s. I was not afraid to tell him, but I was ashamed. I could not be calm about it, it was hard to say. I just walked in on them and said:

===Chinese===
“我回到牧师家。对他讲这件事我不怕，可我臊。我沉不住气，这事难出口。我就那么一头闯了进去，对着他们说：

===Original===
“ ‘I am going to have a child. It is that man’s. He will not marry me.

===Chinese===
“‘我有了身子。是那个男人的。他不会娶我。

===Original===
“They were astonished; then the preacher looked angry. He called me bad. He asked what good all my training had done me; he called me ungrateful. He said a lot of things. If I had waited until he got through, his wife would have spoken, and they would have taken care of me, I think. But I was finding out that everyone said one thing and did another. The Jesus trail seemed to be a lie, too. I told him that. I threw his religion at him. Then he said all sorts of things about me, and ordered me out of his house.

===Chinese===
“他们吃了一惊；跟着牧师就挂了怒相。他骂我坏。他问我受的那些教养都落到了什么好处；骂我忘恩负义。他说了一大堆。我若是沉住气等他说完，他太太本来会开口的，他们也会收留照管我，我想。可我那时候正看明白，人人说的是一套，做的是另一套。耶稣之路看来也是一句谎。我这样对他说了。我把他的宗教劈脸掷还给他。于是他又说了我种种的不是，叫我滚出他的家。

===Original===
“My money was soon gone. I went hungry. I thought I had shame written all over my face. But even then I was strong; I thought that the world had beaten me now, but I would keep on fighting and by and by I would beat it. But just then I was desperate.

===Chinese===
“我的钱很快花光了。我挨了饿。我以为自己满脸都写着羞耻。可就连那时候我也还硬气；我想，这世道如今是把我打垮了，可我还要一直斗下去，早晚有一天我要打垮它。只是那一阵子，我已是走投无路。

===Original===
“Then those bad women spoke to me. They took me in and fed me; they were kind, those bad women. All my ideas were turned upside down now. I did not care. My heart was numb. I learned their trade. I did what they did. In a few months so, with the baby in me, that made me very sick. They took care of me, those bad women.

===Chinese===
“后来那些坏女人招呼了我。她们收留下我，给我吃的；她们心肠好，那些坏女人。我从前那些念头如今全翻了个底朝天。我不在乎了。我的心是木的。我学会了她们那一行。她们做什么我也做什么。这样过了几个月，我带着身子，害起很重的病来。她们照看了我，那些坏女人。

===Original===
“I suffered much pain, the child was born much too soon, dead. I was glad.

===Chinese===
“我吃了许多痛，孩子落地太早，是死的。我倒高兴。

===Original===
“When I was well, I went back to work among them. I had thought a lot, I learned a great deal. I saw how this new life was bad. I saw the faces, the empty hearts of those women, kind though they were. I hated all Americans, and I made up my mind that an American should pay for what an American had done. I remembered my true name. I would have gone to my people, but I did not know how, and I wanted to be paid back. I had my plan.

===Chinese===
“等我好了，我回到她们中间重操那营生。我想了许多，也懂得了许多。我看明白了这种新日子是怎么个坏法。我看清了那些女人的脸，她们空了的心，虽说她们心肠好。我恨所有的美国人，我拿定了主意：美国人做下的事，得叫一个美国人来偿还。我想起了我的真名。我本可以回到我族人中去的，可我不知道怎么回去，再说我要讨回这笔债。我心里有了我的打算。

===Original===
“I noticed one thing﻿—that the men, when they went with those women, liked to be helped to fool themselves that they were with another kind of woman, that they were loved. I did not look like those women yet. I looked young, and decent. They liked that, those men. By then it meant nothing to me; it was just as if I cooked them a meal. It had nothing to do with love, nothing to do with what you know.

===Chinese===
“我还看出一件事——那些男人去找那些女人的时候，喜欢有人帮着他们自己哄自己，哄得相信自己搂着的是另一种女人，相信自己受人爱着。我那时还不像那些女人。我看上去年轻，正派。他们中意这个，那些男人。到那时，这于我已经不算什么了；就同我给他们做一顿饭一样。同爱不相干，同你晓得的那个不相干。

===Original===
“I watched for my chance, and by and by I saw it﻿—a man from the East, that one. He had good manners. He was lonely. And he did not have the poor ideas about Indians that most of these people have, that man.

===Chinese===
“我守着等我的机会，不久就叫我等着了——一个从东部来的男人，就是那一个。他举止斯文。他孤零零一个人。他对印第安人也没有这地方大多数人怀的那种下作念头，那个男人。

===Original===
“I was very careful with him. I did not do any of the things those women usually do to get money away from a man and be rid of him quickly. I acted as innocent as I knew how. He said he was sorry to see me leading such a life. I caught him. He was in Kien Doghaiyoi three nights, and all three nights he came to me. I found out all about him.

===Chinese===
“我对他使足了小心。那些女人惯用的手段——好从男人身上把钱掏出来，再赶紧把他打发掉——我一样也没用。我会装多干净就装多干净。他说，看我过这样的日子他心里难过。我把他钓住了。他在基恩·多盖约伊待了三夜，夜夜都来我这里。我把他的底细全摸清了。

===Original===
“Two weeks later he came back, and I saw him again. I had him, I thought.

===Chinese===
“两个星期以后他回来了，我又见了他。他是我掌中的了，我想。

===Original===
“Ten days after that I came here to Chiziai. I had money. I took that house where you saw me. I watched and waited. He lives a day from here. On the fifth day he came in. I managed to meet him when he was alone. He was surprised and glad. I asked him to come to my house in the evening. I had food and much whiskey for him, so that finally he went to sleep.

===Chinese===
“那以后过了十天，我来到这儿奇扎伊（Chiziai）。我手里有钱。我赁下了你见我住的那所房子。我守着，等着。他住的地方离这儿一天的路。第五天他进了镇。我设法趁他独自一个人的时候见着他。他又惊又喜。我请他晚上到我家里来。我备下吃食和许多威士忌，末了他睡着了。

===Original===
“When he woke up in the morning, that was the test. He felt badly then, and ashamed to wake up in the house of a bad woman. I handed him his money, two hundred dollars, and told him to count it, that it was all there. Then I gave him coffee, and a little whiskey, and then food. He asked how much I wanted. I said I was not doing this for money. Then I gave him a little more whiskey, and so I kept him all day. I did not let him get drunk, and I acted like a good woman who called him friend.

===Chinese===
“早上他醒过来，那才是考验。他那时候心里懊丧，在个坏女人家里醒过来，臊得慌。我把他的钱交还他，两百块，叫他点一点，分文都在。随后我给他咖啡，一点威士忌，然后才是吃食。他问我要多少。我说我做这个不为钱。接着我又给了他一点威士忌，就这样把他留了一整天。我不叫他喝醉，我做出一副正经女人的样子，拿他当朋友相待。

===Original===
“The next morning he said he had to get back to work. He said he would see me when he came back to town, and he wished I was not what I was. He was lonely, that man. These were not his people, these Americans here; they did not talk the same. Like a Navajo living among Apaches.”

===Chinese===
“第二天早上，他说他得回去做工了。他说他再进城时会来看我，还说我若不是现在这样的人就好了。他孤零零的，那个男人。这些人都不是他的族人，这一带的这些美国人；他们连话都说不到一块儿。就像一个住在阿帕奇人（Apache）中间的纳瓦霍人。”

===Original===
Her voice was taking on a timbre of triumph.

===Chinese===
她的声音里渐渐带上了一种得胜的音色。

===Original===
“I said, ‘You will not find me here.’

===Chinese===
“我说：‘你在这儿再找不着我了。’

===Original===
“He said, ‘At Kien Doghaiyoi, then.’

===Chinese===
“他说：‘那么，基恩·多盖约伊。’

===Original===
“ ‘No,’ I said, ‘I am through with all that. I only did it because I had to. I hated it.’

===Chinese===
“‘不，’我说，‘那一套我从此不干了。我从前做它，是不得不做。我恨它。’

===Original===
“He asked how that happened. I told him about half the truth and half lies, to make it sound better, saying I had been bad only a few weeks. Now I said an old Navajo whom I had always known was come for me; I did not love him, but he was a good man, and I was going to marry him. But first I wanted to see him﻿—the American﻿—I said, because he had been kind to me, because he was not like the others. So I had come here for just a few days, I said.

===Chinese===
“他问那是怎么回事。我对他说了一半真话、一半谎，好叫这话听着体面些，说我走上这条路才几个星期。这会儿我又说，有个我一向认得的老纳瓦霍人来接我了；我不爱他，可他是个好人，我要嫁给他。只是我想先见见他——那个美国人——我说，因为他一向待我好，因为他跟别的人不一样。所以我才到这儿来住几天，我说。

===Original===
“He thought a little while. He said, ‘Stay.’ He said he would give me money. I pretended not to want to take money from him; I made him persuade me. I was afraid he might ask me to marry him, but he was not that much of a fool. Finally I said, ‘All right.’

===Chinese===
“他想了一会儿。他说：‘留下吧。’他说他愿意给我钱。我装出不愿拿他钱的样子；逼得他反过来劝我。我怕的是他要开口娶我，好在他没有那么傻。末了我说：‘好吧。’

===Original===
“I had conquered.”

===Chinese===
“我把他征服了。”

===Original===
There was a strong triumph in her voice at that last phrase; now it returned to the level, slow, tired speech.

===Chinese===
说到末了那一句，她声音里有一股强烈的得胜；随后又回到那种平平板板、缓慢、疲倦的语调。

===Original===
“I told him I could not just live there, a Navajo woman. It would make talk, men would annoy me. It would be better if I married the old Navajo and lived near by, then I could meet him when he came to town. With whiskey, I said, that man could be kept happy. I said he was old.

===Chinese===
“我对他说，我不能就这么住在那儿，一个纳瓦霍女人。那要招闲话，男人们要来缠扰。不如我嫁给那老纳瓦霍人，住在近处，那么他进城来的时候，我就见得着他。我说，只要有威士忌，那男人就能一直哄得高高兴兴。我说他老了。

===Original===
“He did not want it to be known he was providing for a Navajo woman, so he agreed. He gave me fifty dollars.

===Chinese===
“他不愿意让人知道他养着一个纳瓦霍女人，就应承下来了。他给了我五十块钱。

===Original===
“There was no Navajo.”

===Chinese===
“根本没有什么纳瓦霍人。”

===Original===
She paused. “Roll me a cigarette.” She smoked it through, then resumed:

===Chinese===
她停了停。“给我卷支烟。”她把那支烟从头到尾抽完，接着又讲下去：

===Original===
“I was not happy. I was provided for, I was revenging myself through him, but I was not living. I wanted my own people. I was all alone. That was why I made friends with Red Man. He is not good, that man. He did not care if I were bad, he hoped I might be bad with him. I never was, but I kept him hoping. With him I remembered the ways of The People, I became quick again in their speech. He helped me much. He is not all bad, that man.

===Chinese===
“我并不快活。我吃穿不愁，我借他的手报着自己的仇，可那不是活着。我要我自己的族人。我孤身一个人。所以我才同红汉子（Red Man）交了朋友。他不是个好的，那个男人。他不在乎我坏不坏，他倒巴望我同他一起坏。我从来没有，可我一直吊着他这点盼头。同他在一起，我记起了迪内的规矩做派，说他们的话嘴又溜了。他帮了我许多忙。他也不全坏，那个男人。

===Original===
“The People looked at me askance. I was a young woman living alone, they did not know how, so they made it up. They do that. Your uncle knows that talk. This went on for over a year. Then I saw you, and everything changed. I had thought I was dead to men, and now I knew I loved you. With you I could live, without you I was already dead.

===Chinese===
“迪内人斜着眼看我。我是个独自过活的年轻女人，他们不明白怎么会的，就自己编排了出来。他们就爱那样。你舅舅就晓得那些闲话。这样过了一年多。后来我看见了你，一切就都变了。我原以为我对男人已经死了，如今我知道我爱你。同你在一起我才能活，没有你，我早已经是个死人了。

===Original===
“I was right. Our way of life, to which you have led me, my weaving, our songs, everything, is better than the Americans’. You have made this.

===Chinese===
“我没有看错。咱们的这种过日子的法子，是你领我回去的，我的织布，我们的歌，样样都好过美国人的。这是你一手做成的。

===Original===
“I had enough, but I thought I could have better. I wanted it for you; you were giving back to me what the Americans had robbed from me since they took me from my mother’s hogan. I thought it right that an American should pay tribute to you and me, I thought it was the perfection of my revenge. After what had happened to me, things did not seem bad that seem bad to other people. So I kept on. I did not tell you, I knew you would not like it.

===Chinese===
“我原有了够用的，可我想着还能更好。我要它是为了你；打他们把我从我娘的霍根屋（hogan，纳瓦霍人泥木筑的小屋）里带走那天起，美国人从我这儿抢走的东西，你正一件件还给我。我想着叫一个美国人向你和我进贡是正当的，我想那是我复仇的圆满。经过了我遭的那些事，在旁人眼里不堪的事，我看着已经不算不堪。于是我接着做下去。我没有告诉你，我知道你不会乐意。

===Original===
“I thought it was all right. What I did with him had nothing to do with what I did with you, it was just work. It was for us, for our life.

===Chinese===
“我想着这没有什么不对。我同他做的事，同我同你做的事不相干，那不过是干活。是为了咱们，为了咱们的日子。

===Original===
“And I did not want to herd sheep and grow heavy and ugly early from work, as Navajo women do. I wanted much money, and then to go North and have children with you and stay beautiful until I am old, as American women do. I was foolish.

===Chinese===
“再说，我也不想放羊，不想像纳瓦霍女人们那样，早早叫活计压得又粗笨又丑。我想要许多钱，然后到北方去，同你生儿育女，像美国女人那样，一直到老都保得住好看。我那时真傻。

===Original===
“Then I saw your face in the window, and the world turned to ashes, and I knew that there were things that were worse than death. That is all, that is the truth. I have spoken.”

===Chinese===
“后来我在窗子里看见了你的脸，世界烧成了灰，我才知道世上还有比死更坏的东西。就这些，这是实话。我说完了。”

===Original===
She sank back, exhausted, with closed eyes. Laughing Boy lit a cigarette from the fire. Then he said:

===Chinese===
她精疲力竭地瘫倒下去，闭着眼睛。笑娃就着火点上了一支烟。随后他说：

===Original===
“I hear you. Sleep. It is well.” He squatted in the doorway, smoking.

===Chinese===
“你说的我听下了。睡吧。没事了。”他蹲在门口抽烟。

### III / III

===Original===
He was at peace within himself. Now at last he knew his wife, now at last he understood her, and it was all right. Error, not evil. Something inimical and proud in her had been destroyed. He was tired, emotionally drained, but he could let his smoke curl up to the stars and feel the cold air penetrate his blanket, calmly, while he thought and knew his own mind. He had a feeling, without any specific reason, that he should keep a vigil over Slim Girl, but he became so sleepy. He went in by the fire, pulled sheepskins about himself, and slept.

===Chinese===
他心里平静了。如今他总算认得了自己的妻子，如今他总算懂得了她，这就对了。是过错，不是罪恶。她身上那点与人为敌的、倨傲的东西，已经毁了。他乏了，心力也耗干了，可他还能安安静静地由着烟一缕缕卷上去、升到星星中间，由着寒气渗进毯子里，一面想，一面明白自己的心意。他有一种感觉，说不出什么具体的缘故，只觉得该为瘦姑娘守一夜，可他实在困得很了。他进到火边，拽过几张羊皮裹住身子，睡着了。

===Original===
In the morning he brought her food and tended her wound. After they had eaten and smoked, he spoke.

===Chinese===
早上他给她送来吃食，照料她的伤口。两人吃过、抽过烟，他开口了。

===Original===
“You have lived in a terrible world that I do not know. I cannot judge you by my world. I think I understand. You have deceived me, but you have not been untrue to me, I think. Life without you would be a kind of death. Now I know that I do not have to do what I thought I had to, and I am glad for it. Now I know you, and there is no more of this secret thing that has been a river between us.

===Chinese===
“你活过的是一个可怕的世界，那世界我不认得。我没法拿我的世界来断你。我想我明白。你哄骗过我，可你没有对我不忠，我想。没有你的日子，是一种死。如今我知道，我先前以为非做不可的事，其实不必做了，我为此欢喜。如今我认得你了，那件一直横在咱们中间、像一条河的秘密事，也再也没有了。

===Original===
“As soon as you are able, we shall go North. If there is a place where you have relatives, we can go there. If not, we can go to T’o Tlakai, or some place where your clan is strong, or wherever you wish. We shall get the sheep that my mother is keeping for me, and we shall buy others, and we shall live among The People. That is the only way, I think.

===Chinese===
“等你将养得动了，咱们就上北方去。若有你亲族在的地方，咱们就去那里。若没有，咱们可以去特奥·特莱凯（T’o Tlakai），或者哪个你氏族人多的地方，或者随你愿意去的什么地方。咱们去把我娘替我看着的羊领回来，再添买一些，咱们就在迪内中间过日子。我看只有这一条路。

===Original===
“Understand, if we go on together, it is in my world, The People’s world, and not this world of Americans who have lost their way.”

===Chinese===
“你要明白，咱们若是再一同走下去，那是在我的世界里，迪内的世界里，不是这些迷了路的美国人的这个世界。”

===Original===
They kissed.

===Chinese===
他们接吻了。

===Original===
“I shall be happy with you anywhere that you wish to take me. As you have said, there is nothing between us now. You have made up to me, and revenged me, for everything the Americans have done to me, My Slayer of Enemy Gods.”

===Chinese===
“你要带我到哪里，我在哪里都快活。照你说的，如今咱们中间什么隔也没有了。美国人加在我身上的一切，你都替我补上了，替我报了仇，我的诛敌神者。”

===Original===
“You must not call me that; it is wicked to call a human being by such a name.”

===Chinese===
“你不可这样叫我；拿这样的名号叫一个活人，是罪过的。”

===Original===
She answered him with another kiss. He thought he had never seen her look so happy. For the first time since he had known her, she looked as young as she was, a year or so younger than himself. Her face was full of peace.

===Chinese===
她用又一个吻回答他。他觉得从没见过她这样快活的模样。打他认得她以来，她头一回显得像她实际的年纪那么年轻，比他自己还小一岁上下。她一脸的安宁。

===Original===
They fell to planning. Reckoning their resources, they concluded that they had amassed the astounding sum of three thousand dollars in money, goods, and horses. He did not want to take what came through her lover, but she said:

===Chinese===
两人着手盘算起来。把家当核算下来，他们得出结论：现钱、货物加上马匹，他们已经攒下了三千块这笔惊人的数目。从她情夫手里来的那一份，他不想要，可她说：

===Original===
“No; I took it like spoils in war. It was war I made with him. And you made it yours when your arrow struck him. And we both paid for it, I think.”

===Chinese===
“不；我拿它，拿的是战阵上的掳获。我对他打的是一场仗。你的箭射中他的时候，它也就成了你的。再说这一仗咱们俩都付了代价，我想。”

===Original===
“Perhaps when he gets well he will send policemen after us.”

===Chinese===
“等他养好了伤，说不定会派警察来追咱们。”

===Original===
“No, I know him. He will say nothing; he will be ashamed, I think.”

===Chinese===
“不会，我了解他。他一个字也不会说；他只会臊，我想。”

## Chapter XX / 第二十章

### I / I

===Original===
During the interval, Laughing Boy moved most of his horses a couple of days’ ride farther north, not far from Zhil Clichigi, where he penned them in a box canyon in which there was a spring and still a little feed. He bought provisions at a trading post on the road to T’o Hatchi. Slim Girl had confessed to him that the story about the warrant out for him on account of the Pah-Ute had been a lie, but, all things considered, she felt it best that he stay away from town. He said that it had seemed a little odd that there should be so much trouble over a Pah-Ute.

===Chinese===
在这段空当里，笑娃把自己大半的马匹赶到两天马程更北的地方，离吉尔·克利奇吉（Zhil Clichigi）不远，圈在一处箱形峡谷里，谷中有眼泉水，还剩着一点儿草。他在通往特奥·哈奇（T’o Hatchi）路上的一座交易站采买了口粮。瘦姑娘已向他招认：那道因派尤特人案子而对他发出的通缉令，原是她编出来的谎；可通盘想来，她还是觉得他躲开镇子为好。他说过，为一个派尤特人闹出这么大的动静，他原本就觉得有点儿蹊跷。

===Original===
“No,” she said, “they do not want any shooting.”

===Chinese===
“不，”她说，“是他们不要人开枪。”

===Original===
“That is true. Whenever there is cause for a fight, they want to send men to do the fighting, and only let us come as guides, like that time with Blunt Nose. They must be very fond of fighting, I think, and they have not enough of their own, so they do other people’s fighting for them. It is a good thing and a bad thing.

===Chinese===
“这话不假。但凡有了开打的由头，他们总要派自己的人来打，只许我们做向导，就像追钝鼻子（Blunt Nose）那一回。他们想必是十分爱打仗的，我想，自家的仗又不够打，便替旁人打起仗来。这既是好事，也是坏事。

===Original===
“I do not understand them, those people. They stop us from raiding the Stone House People and the Mexicans, which is a pity; but they stop the Utes and the Comanches from raiding us. They brought in money and silver, and these goods for our clothes. They bring up water out of the ground for us. We are better off than before they came.

===Chinese===
“我弄不懂他们，那些人。他们不许我们去抢石屋人和墨西哥人，这是可惜的；可他们也拦住尤特人和科曼奇人，不叫他们来抢我们。他们带来了钱和银子，带来了我们身上这些布货。他们还替我们从地底下打出水来。比起他们没来的时候，我们的日子是好过了。

===Original===
“But yet it does not matter whether they do good things or bad things or stupid things, I think. When one or two come among us, they are not bad. If they are, sometimes we kill them, as we did Yellow Beard at Kien Dotklish. But a lot of them and we cannot live together, I think. They do good things, and then they do something like taking a child away to school for five years. Around Lukachukai there are many men who went to school; they wear their hair short; they all hate Americans. I understand that now. There is no reason in what they do, they are blind, but in the end they will destroy everything that is different from them, or else what is different must destroy them. If you destroyed everything in me that is different from them, there would only be a quarter of a man left, I think. Look at what they tried to do to you. And yet they were not deliberately trying.

===Chinese===
“可话说回来，他们做好事也罢，做坏事也罢，做蠢事也罢，我看都不要紧。一两个来到我们中间，倒不算坏。真坏了的，有时我们就杀了他们，像我们在基恩·多特克利什（Kien Dotklish）杀掉黄须汉（Yellow Beard）那样。可一来一大群，我们就没法同住了，我想。他们做着好事，跟着又干出把孩子夺去上学五年的事来。卢卡丘凯（Lukachukai）一带，进过学堂的男人多得很；头发剪得短短的；个个恨美国人。这层我如今懂了。他们行事没有道理，他们是瞎的，可到头来，他们要把一切与他们不同的东西都毁掉，不然，就得那不同的东西来毁了他们。若把我身上凡是同他们不一样的都毁掉，剩下的怕只有四分之一个人了，我想。瞧瞧他们要把你弄成什么样子。而他们甚至都不是存心的。

===Original===
“Well, soon we shall be where there are few Americans, very few. And we shall see that our children never go to school.”

===Chinese===
“好了，咱们很快就到没什么美国人的地方去了，少得很。咱们还要看着，别叫咱们的孩子进一天学堂。”

===Original===
“Soon we shall be where there are very few Americans”; that thought was constant in his mind. He was very happy, it was like a second honeymoon. He had kept all the good things of life, and he had saved himself. He saw that his wife depended on him; she was very tender and rather grave. He understood her gravity, in view of her wound and all that had happened. Soon in new surroundings there would be cause for only happiness. A little readjustment, a little helping her into a less comfortable life, but her courage would make nothing of that.

===Chinese===
“咱们很快就到没什么美国人的地方去了”；这个念头在他心里从没断过。他很快乐，像又度一回蜜月。生活里种种的好处他都留住了，他自己也保全了下来。他看得出妻子倚靠着他；她十分温存，又有些沉肃。想想她的伤，想想这一连串出过的事，他懂得她的沉肃。不久换了新的地方，就只剩叫人欢喜的事了。不过稍加调处，稍帮她过一过不那么安逸的日子，可她的勇气不会把这当回事的。

===Original===
She was very tender and very grave, and she was thinking a great deal. That crisis like a blast of white light had shown her life and herself, it had ended her old independence. She had unravelled her blanket back to the beginning, and started again with a design which could not be woven without Laughing Boy, and she knew that there could be no other design.

===Chinese===
她十分温存，也十分沉肃，心里翻来覆去想了许多。那场危变像一道白光炸开，把她的一生和她自己照了个通透，也了结了她从前的自立。她把自己的毯子拆回到起头处，重新起了一个离了笑娃便织不成的花样；她也知道，本不会有第二个花样。

===Original===
It would not be easy at first to be competent and satisfactory up there; to make herself accepted and liked, to do the dull things, to watch sheep and make her own bread. But she would, and she could. They would go to Oljeto, Moonlight Water, a pretty name and a pretty place, if a childhood memory were true. She had relatives there, and it was far from the long arm of the Americans﻿—wild country, with the unexplored fastnesses towards Tsé Nanaazh and the Pah-Utes. That would be better than dealing with his relatives at T’o Tlakai, and it was near enough for visiting.

===Chinese===
起初，要在那北边把日子拾掇得利落、事事做得叫人满意，并不容易；要教众人容下她、喜欢她，要做那些乏味的事，看羊，自己烙饼。可她做得到，也做得来。他们要去奥尔赫托（Oljeto）——月照水，一个好听的名字，若童年的记性不差，也是个好看的地方。她在那边有亲眷，那里又远在美国人的长臂之外——荒僻的地界，往特塞纳纳兹（Tsé Nanaazh）和派尤特人那头，尽是没人探过的深险之地。这总比周旋于特奥·特莱凯（T’o Tlakai）他那些亲眷强，路又近得可以走动。

===Original===
“And we shall see that our children never go to school.” She echoed that, and she longed for them﻿—his children. But the thought gave her pause. Now that she was thinking as true as she knew how, for her salvation, she wondered if she still could have a child. She was young, but she had been through a lot. After that one terrible time, instructed by the prostitutes of Oñate, she had never put herself in danger of it﻿—or had she? She cast back carefully in her mind; she was not sure. It was possible that she could, possible that she could not. The thing stared her in the face like a risen corpse.

===Chinese===
“看着，别叫咱们的孩子进一天学堂。”她应和着这句话，心里渴想着他们——他的孩子。可这念头也叫她顿住了。如今她为着自救，正拿自己所知的往最真里想，便不免自问：她还能不能生养。她还年轻，可经过的太多了。打那可怕的一回之后，得了奥尼亚特（Oñate）那些妓女的指点，她再没让自己沾过这险——可当真没有吗？她在心里细细往回倒；拿不准。可能生得，也可能生不得。这桩事直挺挺地瞪着她的脸，像一具还了阳的尸首。

===Original===
Then what could she do? Have him take another wife, who would bear them to him. Then in the end he would love that other. He would not, of himself, ever want her to go away, but that other would scheme against her, the mother of his children. What would there be in the world for her, a barren Indian, having lost Laughing Boy? An unlocked door in a street by the railroad track, or death. Only death.

===Chinese===
那时她还有什么路？让他再娶一房，替他生养。到头来，他终究会爱上那一个。他自己呢，断不会起意要她走，可那一个会在他跟前算计她——孩子们的娘。一个生不出孩子的印第安婆娘，又没了笑娃，这世上还给她留着什么？铁道边街上一扇不上锁的门，再不就是死。只有死。

===Original===
There must be children. After all, she was only frightening herself with a chance. When she was quite well, and rested, in their new home, she would put it to the test, and it would come out all right. So she was grave and very tender.

===Chinese===
孩子是一定要有的。说到底，她不过是拿个万一吓唬自己。等她将养好了，歇足了，到了新家，她便试它一试，试出来必定无碍。所以她沉肃，而极温存。

### II / II

===Original===
When her arm was almost well, Laughing Boy brought three of his best horses to the corral. They prepared to move in beautiful, clear, cold, sunny weather following a first light snow, the slight thawing of which assured them of water. Their goods made little bulk﻿—well over a thousand dollars in silver, turquoise, and coral, several hundred dollars in coin, his jeweller’s kit, her spindle, batten, cards, and fork, half a dozen choice blankets, some pots and pans and provisions. They carried a good deal on their saddles, and packed the rest, Navajo-fashion, which is to say badly, on the spare pony. They set out with fine blankets over their shoulders, their mounts prancing in the cold, their saddles and bridles heavy with silver and brass, leading the packhorse by a multicoloured horsehair rope, a splendid couple.

===Chinese===
她那条胳膊快好利索的时候，笑娃把自己三匹顶好的马赶进了畜栏。头一场小雪过后，天气晴美、干冷、日头亮，那微微消融的残雪保得他们一路有水，他们便打点动身。家什拢共没多大一堆——银器、绿松石和珊瑚，值足足一千多块；现钱有几百块；他的银匠家什；她的纺锤、打纬刀、梳毛板和打纬叉；半打上选的毯子；还有些锅碗盆罐和口粮。鞍上驮了大半，余下的按纳瓦霍人的法子——也就是捆得很糟——驮在那匹备用的小马上。他们披着好毯子上路了，坐骑在寒气里踏着欢快的碎步，鞍辔笼头压满了银饰铜饰，一匹驮马用五彩的马鬃绳牵着。好体面的一对儿。

===Original===
After a period of worrying, she had reacted, partly by deliberately living each day for itself only, partly by a natural and reasonable swing to optimism. So they were both gay as they rode, and chattered together of the future. Oljeto had been agreed upon for their new home. It was a good winter camp, he said, and he thought that at Segi Hatsosi or Adudjejiai, little over a day’s ride distant, he could find an unclaimed fertile strip for summer. There is good water there, even in dry summers.

===Chinese===
焦心了一阵之后，她缓过来了：一半靠打定主意，一天只当一天活；一半靠天性和事理，顺顺当当地荡回了乐观。于是骑行路上两人都快活，絮絮地谈着将来。新家已说定在奥尔赫托。那是个过冬的好营地，他说；他还想，在塞吉·哈托西（Segi Hatsosi）或阿杜杰贾伊（Adudjejiai）——骑马不过一天挂点儿零的路——能寻到一片没主儿的肥地做夏天的营地。那边水好，哪怕大旱的夏天也不断。

===Original===
“You have seen the stone granaries we build,” he said. “The rock around that part breaks easily into squares, there is lots of good adobe. I can build you a house as good as the one we just left. We shall make a tunnel like that for the smoke from the fire, and we shall have one of those wooden doors that swing. There will be no house like it around there, except the trader’s at T’o Dnesji.”

===Chinese===
“咱们砌的那种石头粮仓，你见过的，”他说。“那一带的岩石一敲就裂成方正的块，好土坯也多得很。我能给你盖一座屋子，跟咱们刚住的那座一样好。烟道也照那样掏一条，把灶火的烟引出去；还要安一扇那种会转的木门。那一带再不会有第二座这样的屋子，除了特奥·德内西（T’o Dnesji）交易商的那座。”

===Original===
She smiled. “And a window?”

===Chinese===
她笑了。“那，再开一扇窗呢？”

===Original===
“Yes, but we cannot have that clear stone in it. We shall put a membrane across it, that will let in light, I think, but you cannot see through it.”

===Chinese===
“开，可咱们弄不来那种透亮的石头。蒙上一层薄膜吧，我想亮光透得进来，只是望不出去。”

===Original===
“That will be good enough.”

===Chinese===
“那就尽够了。”

===Original===
They came into the mouth of Chizbitsé Canyon. Here and there were fragments of petrified trees, all colours, some dull, some reflecting like marble, the many shades made brilliant by the thin blanket of snow around them, and the clear sunlight.

===Chinese===
他们进了奇兹比塞峡谷（Chizbitsé Canyon）谷口。四下里散落着石化树木的碎块，五颜六色，有的暗沉，有的泛出大理石般的光，薄薄一层雪围衬着，晴日的光照着，深深浅浅的色泽都鲜亮起来。

===Original===
“*Ei-yei!* It is a place of jewels!”

===Chinese===
“咿-耶！这简直是个珠宝之地！”

===Original===
They slowed from a jog-trot to a walk, looking about them at the reproductions of trunks, rings, branches, exact even to the way the snow lay upon them, beautiful in colour, and somehow frightfully dead.

===Chinese===
他们从颠颠的小跑放慢成缓步，一路望着四下那些原样化成石头的树身、年轮、枝桠——连雪覆在上头的样子都分毫不差——颜色好看，却不知怎么，死得怕人。

===Original===
“There is a piece I could use.” Laughing Boy dismounted and picked it up, marbled in ruddy blue and yellow. “I can cut it up and polish it, and use it in rings and bow-guards.”

===Chinese===
“有块料我使得上。”笑娃下了马，把它拾起来，赭红、蓝、黄三色缠出大理石般的纹路。“我可以把它切开、磨光，用在戒指和弓护腕上。”

===Original===
“Yes, it will be a new thing, if it is not too hard to work.”

===Chinese===
“好，那算得上一样新东西，只要它雕琢起来不太吃工。”

===Original===
They searched for a few minutes for more good fragments, then he mounted, shouted the packhorse back onto the trail, and they rode on.

===Chinese===
他们又寻了几分钟，想再拣几块好料，随后他上了马，吆喝着把驮马赶回小路，继续赶路了。

### III / III

===Original===
Red Man, on his way to trade at Jadito, rode past the mouth of Chizbitsé. He had not breakfasted, but the clear weather, the liveliness of his new horse, kept him cheerful. He looked up the canyon, saw them, and thought,

===Chinese===
红汉子（Red Man）在去贾迪托（Jadito）做买卖的路上，打奇兹比塞谷口经过。他还没吃早饭，可晴朗的天气，加上新到手那匹马的活泛劲儿，教他心里畅快。他朝峡谷里一望，望见了他们，心想，

===Original===
“Those two!”

===Chinese===
“那两个！”

===Original===
He crossed the canyon-mouth and stopped where a rock hid all but his head and shoulders. He was swept by an emotion of many factors which time and much mulling over had compounded into one.

===Chinese===
他穿过谷口，停在一块岩石后头，石头把他遮得只剩脑袋和肩膀。一种情绪漫过他，里头夹着许多由头，岁月和反复咀嚼早把它们酿成了同一样。

===Original===
I helped that woman, I took care of her. I ran her errands, I made life possible for her. I loved her, in a way. I knew she was bad with Americans, but she would never do it with me. I deserved it from her. She made a fool of me instead. Why not me, too? Always putting me off and getting around me. And then that fool came out of nowhere and she gave him everything. Him! And he threatened me. *He* told *me* what to do.

===Chinese===
我帮过那女人，我照应过她。我替她跑腿，我把她的日子撑持起来。我爱她，也算得爱。我知道她跟美国人不干净，可她断不肯同我来。她本该给我的。她反倒把我当傻子耍。凭什么偏不找我？回回把我往后搪，回回绕着我走。后来那蠢货平地里冒出来，她把什么都给了他。他！他还威胁我。居然是他来告诉——我——该做什么。

===Original===
All this through many months had become a single feeling. They were riding slowly, leaning towards each other, talking. Faintly, he heard her laugh. There was a pack-animal in front of them﻿—they were going on a visit somewhere, very rich, with a packhorse.

===Chinese===
这一切熬过许多个月，早熬成了同一种感觉。他们骑得很慢，彼此倾着身子说话。隐隐地，他听见她的笑声。他们前头有匹驮畜——是出门做什么客去，好阔气，还带着驮马。

===Original===
He thought, There goes the man who may send an arrow into me some day. It made the small of his back squirm.

===Chinese===
他想，瞧这个人，有朝一日没准儿会送一箭进我的身子。这念头教他后腰一阵阵地发麻。

===Original===
He took up his rifle, aimed high for distance, and fired. The gun had not been cleaned for several weeks, his hands were cold, and the pony moved. He fired three times, then ducked low behind the rock, and began riding.

===Chinese===
他端起来复枪，为够着距离抬高了枪口，开了火。那枪好几个星期没擦，他手冷，小马又在动。他连开三枪，随即矮身缩到岩石后面，打马跑了起来。

===Original===
Laughing Boy heard the shots, turned, and ducked as two bullets snapped close to him, before he saw Slim Girl slump forward in the saddle. He threw his arm about her, caught her rein, and drove the horses to a gallop. The pack-animal, startled by the rush behind him, raced ahead. When you have only a bow, and an unseen person or persons begins shooting liberally with a rifle, it is no time for gestures of valour or revenge.

===Chinese===
笑娃听见了枪声，一转身，趁两颗子弹贴着他嗖地掠过便伏低了身子，这才看见瘦姑娘在鞍上向前栽倒。他一条胳膊揽住她，抓住她的缰绳，把几匹马催成狂奔。驮畜被身后这阵猛冲一惊，撒蹄蹿到前头去了。手里只有一张弓，而看不见的、不知是一个还是几个人，正端起来复枪放开了打——这当口，可不是逞英勇、图报仇的时节。

===Original===
They rode thus for about a mile, and then, still seeing no one behind them, drew rein. Here the canyon was wide, and on one side a cleft led into Chizbitsé Mesa, up a slow incline. In there he turned, until at the end of the box canyon sheer cliffs stopped them.

===Chinese===
他们这样跑出约莫一英里，回头仍不见人影，才收住缰。这里峡谷开阔，一侧有道裂隙通进奇兹比塞台地（Chizbitsé Mesa），是条慢坡。他拐了进去，直到箱形峡谷的尽头，笔陡的绝壁拦住了他们。

===Original===
Slim Girl was silent and quite limp as he lowered her from her saddle and placed her on a couch of blankets. Once at ease there, she moaned and asked for water. Her eyes were narrowed and her lips drawn slightly back. He made a fire and melted snow, she drank eagerly.

===Chinese===
笑娃把她从鞍上抱下，安放在毯子铺成的卧榻上，瘦姑娘一声不响，浑身瘫软。在那儿安顿下来，她呻吟着讨水。她眯缝着眼，嘴唇微微往后绷。他生了火，化开雪水，她急切切地喝了。

===Original===
The bullet had gone clean through her; she was soaked with blood. He did what he could to staunch the flow, and arranged her as comfortably as possible. Occasionally she moaned, then said quite clearly:

===Chinese===
子弹打穿了她的身子；她浑身浸透了血。他尽了力止血，把她安放得尽量妥帖。她时而呻吟一声，随后清清楚楚地说：

===Original===
“No. I will be brave. Give me a cigarette, and raise my head a little.”

===Chinese===
“不。我要拿出勇气来。给我一支烟，把我的头垫高一点儿。”

===Original===
She had scarcely strength to smoke, and she began to cough.

===Chinese===
她连抽烟的气力都快没有了，跟着咳嗽起来。

===Original===
“This is the end, my husband, my beloved.” Her voice was faint, and she paused after every few words. “Do not try to avenge me. Promise me that.”

===Chinese===
“这就到头了，我的丈夫，我的爱人。”她的声音微弱，每说几个字便停一停。“不要想法子替我报仇。答应我。”

===Original===
“I promise.” He knelt facing her, unmoving, with lines in his face like carving.

===Chinese===
“我答应你。”他朝她跪着，正对着她，一动不动，脸上的纹路深得像刀刻出来的。

===Original===
“I think this was meant to happen. Perhaps it is right, I think. After all that had happened to me, perhaps I could not have had children. The Americans spoiled me for a Navajo life, but I shall die a Navajo, now.” She spoke very slowly, with long waits while she lay with closed eyes and her hands clenched. “I have saved my soul through you. I have been very happy with you. This last little while, I found myself, I found truth with you.” She broke into coughing, and then was silent for almost five minutes.

===Chinese===
“我想，这事本是注定要来的。也许这样反倒对，我想。经过我遭的那些事，说不定我本来就生不出孩子了。美国人把我糟蹋了，糟蹋得过不成纳瓦霍的日子；可我要死了，就趁现在，死成一个纳瓦霍人。”她说得极慢，中间隔着长长的间歇，闭着眼躺着，双手攥得紧紧的。“经由你，我救回了我的灵魂。跟你在一起，我非常幸福。这最后的一小段日子，我找回了自己，在你这儿找到了真。”她骤然咳嗽起来，随后沉默了差不多五分钟。

===Original===
“I say all this so you shall know that it has not been in vain. You will go on and live and remember me, you have changed because of me; in you I shall live.

===Chinese===
“我说这些，是要你知道，这一切不算白费。你还要往前走，活下去，记着我；因为我，你已经变了；在你身上，我还将活着。

===Original===
“I have come home. I shall die at home, I shall be buried like my People. It is *hozoji*.”

===Chinese===
“我回到家了。我要死在家里，要照我族人那样下葬。这就是霍卓吉。”

===Original===
He had no words at all.

===Chinese===
他一个字也说不出来。

===Original===
“I love you so much. Kiss me.”

===Chinese===
“我这样爱你。吻我吧。”

===Original===
He bent over her, her arm clutched about his neck, he lifted her shoulders against his chest. Her eyes were closed and she kissed him with cool, closed lips of love, not of passion.

===Chinese===
他俯下身去，她的胳膊紧搂着他的脖颈，他把她的肩头抬起来，贴住自己的胸膛。她闭着眼，用凉凉的、合拢的唇吻他——那是爱的唇，不是情欲的唇。

===Original===
She opened her eyes, drew back her head, and smiled at him. Then she said in a clear voice,

===Chinese===
她睁开眼睛，把头向后一仰，朝他一笑。随后她用清亮的声音说，

===Original===
“Nayeinezgani!”﻿—Slayer of Enemy Gods.

===Chinese===
“Nayeinezgani！”——诛敌神者。

===Original===
And so speaking, smiling, died.

===Chinese===
就这样，说着话，带着笑，她死了。

===Original===
Then she is dead. Then it is all over. But just a little while ago we were laughing together and picking up stones. We were so happy together. Now it is all over. But we had everything arranged, we were going North, we had all our goods, our silver, our blankets. I was going to make her a ring with that purple stone. I was going to build her a house. Now it is all over. There is no sense in it. *Ei-ee*, Divine Ones! *Ei*, Slim Girl, Came With War!

===Chinese===
那么，她死了。那么，一切都完了。可就在方才，我们还在一起笑着，还拾着石头。我们在一起多么幸福。如今一切都完了。可我们什么都安排停当了，我们要去北方，全部家当都在，我们的银子，我们的毯子。我还要拿那块紫石头给她打一枚戒指。我还要给她盖一座屋子。如今一切都完了。这里头没有一点道理。咿-欸，神圣之灵！咿，瘦姑娘，携战而来！

===Original===
He threw himself upon her body and pressed his mouth to hers. Her lips were cold, she was cold and inert all over. It was inhuman, it was dead. He drew back and rose to his feet with a revulsion of fear, then grew calm.

===Chinese===
他扑倒在她身上，把嘴紧紧压上她的嘴。她的唇是冷的，她周身冰冷，一动不动。那不是人的，那是死的。他缩回来，怀着恐惧的厌恶站起身，随后平静下来。

===Original===
This is not she, not Slim Girl, Brave Alone, not Came With War, not my wife. This is something she left behind. It is dead, it never had life; it was she inside it who gave it life. I am not afraid of it, and can I ever be afraid of you, oh, beautiful? I shall be calm, I shall bury it, a Navajo burial.

===Chinese===
这不是她，不是瘦姑娘，不是独勇（Brave Alone），不是携战而来，不是我的妻子。这是她撇下的一件东西。它是死的，它从来不曾有过生命；是住在它里头的她给了它生命。我不怕它；而我几时怕过你呢，啊，我美丽的人？我要镇定下来，我要葬了它，行一场纳瓦霍的葬礼。

===Original===
He knelt beside her body and began to sob. After a while he thought, she would not like me to do this. I must bury her before it gets dark. It will snow soon. All alone I went with her, alone I lived with her and knew her beauty, now I alone shall bury her. She was not meant for common knowledge, she was not part of ordinary life, that many people should partake of her.

===Chinese===
他在她遗体旁跪下，呜咽起来。过了一阵他想，她不会乐意我这样。我必须赶天黑前葬了她。快下雪了。我独自一人携她同去，独自与她同住，识得她的美；如今也由我独自一人葬她。她本不是造给众人知晓的，她本不属于寻常日子，容不得许多人来分尝她。

### IV / IV

===Original===
The packhorse had disappeared, but before going it had, like a wise animal, rolled its pack off. He collected all their goods and divided them into two equal parts. Most of the time he was not really thinking, but dully following out with slow movements what seemed to be a foreordained course. It occurred to him that the riches that came through the American ought to be thrown away, but he remembered what she had said about that. In jewelry and blankets it had been transmuted. He picked up one of the heaps of coin. That was a lot of money. They had suffered a lot for it, she had suffered so much. He set it down again.

===Chinese===
驮马不见了，可临走之前，它像一切乖觉的牲口那样，打了个滚把驮子滚脱了。他把家什尽数收拢，均分成两堆。大半时候他并不真在想，只是动作迟缓，昏昏沉沉地顺着一条仿佛早已注定的路子做下去。他闪过一个念头：从那美国人手里来的钱财，本该扔掉；可他记起她对这事说过的话。它们进了首饰和毯子，已经变了质。他拿起其中一堆钱币。那是好大一笔钱。他们为它受了多少苦，她受了那么多苦。他又把它放下了。

===Original===
The farthest corner of the cliffs made a niche about twelve feet square, in which the rocks came to the ground sheer, or slightly overhanging, without talus. Here he carried her, and set her in the farthest recess. He walked carefully, avoiding bushes, observing all the requirements, in so far as was possible for a single individual. Over her he put her blankets, at her head, food, by her hands, her weaving tools, cooking implements at her feet. He covered her form with silver and turquoise and coral and coins. As he arranged her, he prayed. Then he looked about for fair-sized slabs, of which there were plenty roundabout, in the talus. He began to bring them, covering her.
