He had placed the first few, at her feet, when he straightened up and stood still. He walked to his own pile of goods and looked at it. Returning to her, he found her arm under the blankets, and took from it a thin, gold bracelet that she had bought in California. From his own goods he set aside the finest saddle-blanket of her weaving, an old trade blanket, a coffeepot and coffee. Bundling all the rest together, he carried it to the grave and spread it over her. Slowly he took off his heavy silver belt, his turquoise and coral necklace, his two bracelets, his garnet ring and his turquoise ring, his earrings of turquoise matrix, laying each one gently upon the heap. He changed his old bow-guard for one he had made at their house. Remembering something, he went to his pony, took off his silver-mounted bridle, and added it.

===Chinese===
绝壁最深的角落凹成一个龛室，约莫十二英尺见方，四壁直落地面，或微微前倾，没有碎石坡。他把她抱到那里，安放在最里面的深处。他走得小心，避开灌木，把该守的规矩一一守到——就一个人独力所能做到的而言。他把她的毯子盖在她身上；她头边放了吃食；她手边放上她织布的家什；脚头搁了炊具。他用银器、绿松石、珊瑚和钱币盖满她的身形。一边安放，一边祷告。随后他四下里找大小合用的石板，近旁碎石坡上有的是。他开始一趟趟搬来，往她身上盖。头几块在她脚头放好了，他忽然直起身，站定不动。他走到自己那堆家什跟前，看了看。再回到她身边，从毯子底下寻出她的胳膊，褪下一只细细的金镯——她在加利福尼亚买的。他又从自家家什里单摆出几样：她织得最讲究的一条鞍毯，一条旧贸易毯，一只咖啡壶和咖啡。余下的捆作一捆，抱到墓前，摊开盖在她身上。他慢慢解下自己沉重的银腰带，绿松石和珊瑚的项链，两只手镯，石榴石戒指和绿松石戒指，还有绿松石母岩的耳坠，一样一样，轻轻搁到堆上。他摘下旧弓护腕，换上一只在他们的屋里亲手做下的。又想起一样，他走到自己的小马跟前，解下那副镶银的笼头，也添了上去。

===Original===
With difficulty, he forced the thin gold circle up over his right hand, taking some of his skin with it: it was but little wider than his wrist, it would not come off easily. Then he continued covering her. It began to snow, in large, soft, slow flakes out of a grey-white sky.

===Chinese===
他费了好一番劲，才把那细细的金环从右手掌上硬勒过去，带下来几丝皮肉：那环只比他的腕子宽出那么一点儿，轻易褪不下来。随后他接着盖她。下雪了，灰白的天上，落下大片大片又软又慢的雪花。

===Original===
It was nearly dark when he had laid on the last stone, and he began to be aware that he was weary. Blowing cigarette smoke four ways, he stood in prayer for a minute or two. He untethered her pony and led it into the niche. It stood patiently by the pile while he notched his arrow and spoke the requisite words. The string twanged, the shaft struck, the pony leapt and fell partly over the tomb. Those clear-cut things, happening rapidly, were out of tempo with everything else; they put a period to it.

===Chinese===
最后一块石头压上去时，天差不多黑透了，他这才觉出浑身的乏。他把纸烟的烟朝四方各吹了一口，站着祷告了一两分钟。他解开她那匹小马的缰绳，牵进龛室。马在石堆旁耐性地站着，他一面搭箭上弦，一面念了该念的话。弓弦铮的一响，箭杆中的，小马一跃，半边身子跌倒在墓上。这几桩干净利落、来得迅疾的事，同其余的一切全不合拍；它们给这一切画上了句号。

## Chapter XXI / 第二十一章

### I / I

===Original===
Now began the four days of waiting. But just waiting was not enough; there had been no women to wail for her, no outcry of bereaved relatives; he would make it a vigil, all the four days should be one prayer. This was not an ordinary death.

===Chinese===
如今开始的是四天的等待。可光是等待还不够；没有女人们为她哭丧，没有亲眷们丧亲的哭喊；他要把它做成一场守夜，四天统统合成一篇祷告。这不是一场寻常的死亡。

===Original===
It was quite dark, and the snow still drifted down like waterlogged leaves falling through water. He rebuilt the fire till it blazed, arranged the saddle-blanket and his saddle for a reclining place, pulled the trade blanket about him, and began the vigil, staring at the distant blacker place in the blackness of the cliffs behind the snowflakes that marked the niche.

===Chinese===
天已全黑，雪还在飘落，像浸透水的叶子在水中沉降。他把火重新架起，直烧得熊熊，铺开鞍毯、放好马鞍，权作倚卧之处，把那条买卖来的毯子往身上一裹，开始守夜，眼睛盯着雪片后面崖壁的黑影里那处更黑的地方——龛室就在那儿。

===Original===
He tried to pray, but his mind kept wandering, reviewing incidents of their life together, happy and unhappy, but so full of life, so charged with her personality. He would forget that she was dead, he would just be thinking about her. The cold coming through his clothing would wash along his skin, a flake would touch his face, and he would remember.

===Chinese===
他试着祷告，心思却总往外跑，一遍遍过着他俩共同生活的种种情景，有甜有苦，却那样满含生气，那样浸透她的性子。他会忘了她已经死了，只顾想着她。透进衣裳的寒气漫过皮肤，一片雪落在脸上，他才又想起来。

===Original===
Now it is all over. Let it be altogether so. That horse is lucky; well, we shall go with her, too.

===Chinese===
如今一切都完了。就让它整个儿这样吧。那匹马有福气；好，咱们也随她去。

===Original===
He got his pony, took his saddle in his hand, and went back into the niche. The animal was nervous and wild with the darkness and the cold and the smell of death. It would not stand still. Later it was to occur to him as part of the remarkableness that he went unhesitatingly into that place after dark, but now he thought nothing of it. Now he was not a Navajo terrified of the dead, not an Indian, not an individual of any race, but a man who had buried his own heart.

===Chinese===
他牵过小马，一手提着马鞍，回到龛室里去。那牲口又惊又野，是被黑暗、寒冷和死亡的气味闹的，怎么也不肯站定。后来他想起，天黑之后他竟毫不犹豫地走进那个地方，也是这件事的奇处之一，可眼下他全没在意。此刻他不是一个怕死人的纳瓦霍，不是一个印第安人，不是任何族类的一分子，只是一个亲手埋掉了自己心的人。

===Original===
He selected his arrow.

===Chinese===
他挑出一支箭。

===Original===
“… It has not been in vain. You will remember me, I shall live in you.”

===Chinese===
“……这一切不算白费。你会记着我，在你身上，我还将活着。”

===Original===
Wind God had spoken her words in his mind. She would not like this. He put back the arrow in his quiver, and led the horse out to the fire. There he took off its rope, and hobbled it.

===Chinese===
她的这些话，是风神在他心里说出来的。她不会喜欢这样。他把箭放回箭袋，把马牵到火边。到了那里，他解下缰绳，给它绊上了腿。

===Original===
“Go see if you can find food and water, little brother. Go away and be happy.”

===Chinese===
“去找找草料和水吧，小兄弟。走远些，快快活活地过。”

===Original===
He returned to his vigil, collecting a large pile of dead wood for the fire, and making himself as comfortable as possible with his blankets and his saddle. He began to feel some fear, conscious of the nearness of her tomb, being so very much alone in that narrow canyon. He set himself to the task of realizing what had happened, and conceiving a continuing life without Slim Girl. It was not easy; he spent a long time in rebellion, or in a mere thronging of bitter emotions that made him throw his shoulders from side to side.

===Chinese===
他回到守夜的地方，给火堆拢起一大堆枯柴，又拿毯子和马鞍把自己安顿得尽量舒坦。他开始觉出些恐惧来——意识到她的墓近在旁边，又独自一人处在这条窄峡谷里。他给自己立下功课：认清已经发生的事，构想一个没有瘦姑娘、却仍要继续的人生。这不容易；他久久陷在抗拒里，或者说陷在一阵蜂拥而至的苦涩情绪里，搅得他把肩膀来回直摆。

===Original===
Jesting Squaw’s Son had been lucky. But in the end he was better off, because there had been that year and a half. Not for anything would he lose that. He began remembering again﻿—it was a kind of anodyne﻿—until he came back to the inevitable starting point. Then it was worse.

===Chinese===
打趣婆娘之子算是幸运的。可到头来还是他境遇更好，因为有过那一年半。拿什么都换，他也不肯失去它。他又开始回忆——回忆是一种止痛的东西——直到绕回那个绕不开的起点。那时节就更难熬了。

===Original===
After some hours he grew calmer, partly because of fatigue. The disaster was accepted and familiar; he told himself that he could see the life ahead growing, in a way, from what had gone before. Nothing could ever make him forget; what he was and always would be, what he did and thought, would always be conditioned by Slim Girl. The remainder of his life would be a monument to her. All this could not be changed or taken from him, he would never lose its mark. That was a comfort.

===Chinese===
过了几个钟头，他平静了些，一半是因为乏了。这场灾祸已经认下、变得熟悉；他对自己说，往后的日子，也算是从先前的一切里生长出来的。什么也不能叫他忘掉；他如今是什么人、永远是什么人，他做什么、想什么，都将永远由瘦姑娘塑成。他的余生将是献给她的一座碑。这一切改变不了，也夺不走，那印记他永远不会丢。这是一种安慰。

===Original===
He was thinking this way with his intellect, it did not really go inside of him. It was still just platitudes.

===Chinese===
他这些念头都是脑子在转，并没有真进到心里去。仍旧不过是些空话。

===Original===
He became more aware of things about him, the cold, the fire, the snow. Flakes fell into the flames with little hisses, and he remembered his dream.

===Chinese===
他对身周的事物更有了知觉，寒冷、火、雪。雪片落进火焰，嘶地一声轻响，他想起了自己的梦。

===Original===
“Slayer of Enemy Gods” she called me. But Slayer of Enemy Gods spared the Cold Woman and Old Age Woman and Poverty People and Hunger People. She tried to kill the Hunger People; I thought she could. If we had not tried to do that, we should have been living happily within the Navajo country long ago. She was too daring, she wanted perfection.

===Chinese===
她管我叫过“诛敌神者”。可诛敌神者饶过了冷女人（Cold Woman）、老年女人（Old Age Woman）、贫穷之民（Poverty People）和饥饿之民。她却要去杀饥饿之民；我原以为她办得到。倘若我们不曾起意去做那件事，我们早就在纳瓦霍的地界里乐乐和和地过活了。她太胆大，她要的是十全十美。

===Original===
By whatever means she got it, we had perfection. But it could not have gone on. We are not divine. Or I am not; she had made herself above Earth People. What has happened to me, it is like what happened to many people long ago. It happened to Taught Himself and the Magician’s Daughter, to Reared in a Mountain when he went through the homes of the gods, to Eagles’ Friend when he went to the sky. They went away and saw something better than they had ever known. They did not try to bring something too good for earth back to earth. But they did not﻿—lose﻿—Slim Girl.

===Chinese===
不论她用的是何等法子，我们有过十全十美了。可它长不了。我们不是神。至少我不是；她已把自己抬到地上之民之上。我遭逢的这件事，就像很久以前许多人遭逢过的。自学汉（Taught Himself）和巫师之女（Magician’s Daughter）遭逢过，山中养大走遍诸神居所的时候遭逢过，鹰之友上天去的时候也遭逢过。他们走了远路，见着了比自己从来所知的更好的东西。他们不曾想把好得不宜人间的东西带回人间来。可他们不曾——失去——瘦姑娘。

===Original===
His head fell forward on his knees and he stopped thinking. He was exhausted, and shortly fell asleep in that position. He woke when he began to fall over, very cold, and thought he must have drowsed. The fire was low. The snowfall ceased and dawn came limping.

===Chinese===
他的头垂到膝上，他不再想了。他筋疲力尽，不一会儿就那样睡着了。身子开始歪倒时他醒了，冷极了，心想方才准是打了盹。火塌得低了。雪住了，黎明一瘸一拐地来。

===Original===
The first day was sparkling, crisp, and sunny. The first day was one of stunned, dull realization. He wandered about uncertainly and drugged himself with detailed, long-drawn-out memories. And in the end, he would return to the beginning of his circle and stand or sit motionless and groan. It was a long day and a strange one; later he did not remember it clearly.

===Chinese===
第一天清朗、干冷、明亮。第一天却是懵懵的、迟钝的省悟。他没个定准地转来转去，拿细碎的、拖得长长的回忆麻醉自己。到末了，他总会兜回那个圈子的起点，站着或坐着不动，呻吟。那是一段漫长而古怪的日子；后来他记不真切了。

===Original===
The second night he tried hard not to sleep, but it was hard, with cold, hunger, fatigue, and the fire. He dozed a good deal, and his memories became very accurate dreams into which slowly would creep a sense of horror without reason, until he woke, not knowing he had slept, continuing the thought and the mood. He tried to pray, but it was chiefly ejaculations and the names of the gods. It was an endless and terrible night.

===Chinese===
第二夜他极力不睡，可是难，又是冷，又是饿，又是乏，还有那火。他打了好多回盹，回忆变得毫厘不爽，成了梦，一种无缘无故的恐怖慢慢渗进去，直到他惊醒——不觉得自己睡着过——接着先前的念头和心绪往下走。他试着祷告，出口的却多半是呼喊和众神的名字。那是一个无尽而可怕的夜。

===Original===
Daylight, when at last it came, was a release. He shook himself, thinking, I must be calm, I must think clearly. This is no time for wandering without getting anywhere. He quieted himself for a time, achieving a state of apparent resignation which enabled him to pray, but the oft-repeated *hozoji* sounded hollow. He did not really think there was anything beautiful; he was just acting as he thought he ought.

===Chinese===
天光终于来了，是一种解脱。他抖擞一下，想道：我得镇静，我得想清楚。这可不是瞎转悠而一无所获的时候。他让自己安分了一阵，做出一副貌似认命的样子，得以祷告，可那念了又念的霍卓吉听着空洞。他心里并不真觉得有什么美的东西；他只是在照自己以为应该的样子做。

===Original===
Plenty of people had died in his neighbourhood; there had been mourning and grief, when everyone had stayed close to the hogans for four days. But this was different. He had seen the bereaved, he had seen real sorrow upon them, but he could not believe that they had felt as he did.

===Chinese===
他那一带死过不少人；有过哀哭和悲伤，那时人人紧挨着霍根屋守足四天。可这一回不同。他见过死了亲人的人，见过他们脸上真切的哀恸，但他不能相信他们尝过他这样的滋味。

===Original===
He was alone in more than the physical sense. No one, not even Jesting Squaw’s Son, could come near him. All his life, wherever he was, however long he lived, he would remain alone. It would always be like this. The one companionship in the world had gone; when the sun has been destroyed for a man, what comfort is there in a world of moonlight?

===Chinese===
他的孤独不止是身子上的。没有一个人——连打趣婆娘之子也算上——能走近他。他这一辈子，无论在哪里，无论活多久，都是孤身一人。永远都会是这样。世上唯一的那份相伴去了；一个人的太阳既已毁掉，月光的世界里还有什么安慰可言？

===Original===
He had nothing to do in the canyon save tend his fire and think. He would get hold of a thought, work it over and over until he lost all sense of proportion towards it, and finally put it in a phrase or a simile, so that it obtained substance and could not be dismissed.

===Chinese===
在峡谷里他无事可做，只有守火、想事。他会抓住一个念头，翻来覆去地掂弄，直到对它全然失了分寸，末了把它凝成一句话或一个比方，这样它便有了实体，再撵不走了。

===Original===
He hated to watch it grow dark; he felt afraid of the night. He did not want to be shut into that little space of firelight with all the things he was thinking. Alone, alone, all life alone, all life carrying this pain inside himself. He might as well die. But she wanted him to live. It was the third night, and he was approaching the stage of visions. Outlines of things dimly seen in the starlight changed and assumed startling forms. He became the audience listening to unseen people arguing as to whether he ought to kill himself or not. He knew he ought to live, but he could not control which side might win.

===Chinese===
他不忍看天黑下去；他怕夜。他不想被关进那一小圈火光里，同满脑子的念头关在一处。孤独，孤独，一生孤独，一生把这份疼揣在自己心里。不如死了倒好。可她要他活着。这是第三夜，他临近了见异象的地步。星光里影影绰绰的轮廓变了样，显出骇人的形状。他成了听众，听着看不见的人们争论他该不该自尽。他知道该活着，可哪一边占上风，他管不住。

===Original===
He couldn’t always follow very well what was going on. Extraneous things intruded themselves. There were people all around, pitying him. It was being insisted upon that loneliness and pain were not worth enduring for a whole lifetime, without purpose.

===Chinese===
他并不总能跟得很上眼前的光景。不相干的东西自己闯进来。四下里全是人，都在怜悯他。有人一味主张：这孤独和疼痛既然没有个目的，犯不着忍上一辈子。

===Original===
“But I have to live for her,” he said aloud, and thought hard about her.

===Chinese===
“可我得为她活着，”他出声说，一面苦苦地想她。

===Original===
Then he saw her, standing on the other side of the fire. He started to his feet, choking with all a Navajo’s terror of the walking dead. He was dissolved in fear, but she was gone. He was alone, the voices were gone, the people. He sat down, trembling, and quite wide awake. Evidently he did not want to die, but he had no will to live; he did not know himself, it would be wrong to make a decision now. Little by little he grew drowsy, and dozed in snatches. Perhaps her coming was a good thing; one would not expect her ghost to be like other people’s.

===Chinese===
随后他看见她了，站在火堆那一边。他霍地站起，纳瓦霍人对行走死人的那种恐惧把他噎得透不过气。他吓得整个儿化掉了，她却已经不见。他又是一个人了，声音没有了，人也没有了。他坐下来，发着抖，人全然醒透。看来他并不想死，可他也没有求生的意志；他不认得自己了，这时候做决定是不对的。一点一点地他又困了，断断续续打起盹来。也许她的来是件好事；本来也不该指望她的鬼魂跟旁人的一样。

===Original===
This, too, became a discussion outside himself. The spirits of the dead are bad; if they walk, it is for destruction. She is different, she would come to protect him. She would lead him to some frightful end. But no one could imagine blue fire coming out of her eyes and mouth. It went on and on. There was an outline of something he had not seen before, it moved and he felt his scalp crawl. Then he let out a deep sigh and related. It was a bush, some little distance away. Dawn was coming.

===Chinese===
这也成了身外的一场争论。死人的魂是凶的；它们若行走，是为了毁灭。她不同，她会来护着他。她会把他引到什么可怕的结局里去。可谁也想像不出她的眼睛和嘴里会喷出蓝火来。争了又争。有个他从未见过的东西的轮廓，它一动，他觉出头皮发麻。随后他深深叹了口气，定下神来。原来是一丛灌木，隔着一小段路。天快亮了。

===Original===
A little water and the clear sunshine revived him for a time, but soon he was tired and miserable again, listlessly occupying himself with gathering more firewood, bringing a branch at a time, setting aside without interest those that would do for making a sweat-bath. Later, he thought of that day as the day of treason. He protracted his occupation, being meticulous about finding all the wood in one section before moving to the next. He thought of the eventual bath, which should wash him clean of the taint of death; he wished it might wash out his mind. He thought that if he never had met her, he would be happy now, remembering Slender Hair riding by and telling him of the dance that was to be, remembering himself riding, singing, down to Tsé Lani, the firelight and that girl, remembering what chance, what meeting of eyes in a crowd, his uncle’s tactlessness, had put an end to a young man who had no cares.

===Chinese===
一点水，清朗的日头，让他振作了一阵，可不久他又疲惫、凄惶起来，无精打采地忙着多拾些柴火，一次抱一根枝子，顺手把够做汗浴的撂在一边，心不在焉。后来他把那一天想成背叛的日子。他把手里的活计拉得老长，细细致致，一块地段的柴拾尽了才挪到下一块。他想着最后那一场浴，它该把他身上死亡的秽气洗净；但愿也能把他的心思洗空。他想，倘若从未遇见她，他如今是有福的——记着细发骑马路过、告诉他要有一场舞；记着他自己骑着马、唱着歌下特塞拉尼去，那火光，那一个姑娘；记着是怎样的机缘、怎样人堆里目光的一遇，再加上舅舅那不知轻重的一句，结果了一个无忧无虑的年轻人。

===Original===
The thought stayed with him as he wandered about kicking up the snow for bits of dead wood. He knew that it was an entity in itself, and saw it as a tall old man who leered as he walked beside and slightly behind him, a bad, strong old man. The old man kept at him about it, that he had been a fool, that he should have avoided all this. And he thought, in answer, that it was too late now, why couldn’t he be left alone? He tried to explain to himself, to the old man, that he couldn’t have helped doing what he did. He himself, as a third person, repeated that all the suffering was worth while for the happy months, but the old man only sneered. He tried to get himself in hand, and think of a new design for a belt, but that was useless. He would walk around for a long time without looking for wood at all. Picking up his pony’s tracks, he followed them out into the main canyon until he saw the horse in a sheltered place under the east wall, then, realizing how far he had gone from the place of vigil, hurried back. Everything had gone to pieces, he did everything wrong. The old man had waited for him, he was triumphant over this breach of observance.

===Chinese===
他四下里踢开积雪找枯柴，那念头一路跟着他不放。他知道它自身已成了一件实体，便把它看成一个高个子老头，贼忒兮兮地笑着，走在他身旁、稍稍靠后的地方，一个又凶又壮的老头。老头拿这件事数落他个没完，说他是个傻瓜，说他本该躲开这一切。他心里回嘴：如今已经晚了，为什么就不能叫他清静？他试着对自己、也对老头解释：他做下的事，是躲也躲不开的。他自己又站作第三个人，重说一遍：为了那些快活的月份，这些苦都值得；老头只是冷笑。他想把自己管住，琢磨一条腰带的新花样，可是没用。他常常转悠上好一阵，压根儿没找柴。踏着小马的蹄印，他一路跟出去，进了主峡谷，才望见马儿待在东壁下一处背风的地方，这才醒悟自己离守夜的地方已走出多远，急忙赶回去。一切都散了架，他事事都做岔了。老头一直在等他，为这一遭失守得意洋洋。

===Original===
Nightfall was at least a change. Having plenty of wood, he built the fire up high, and went to some trouble to make himself comfortable. This was the fourth night, he was more or less out of his head. The old man had long ceased to be a personification and become a reality; he got in under the same blanket and hammered, hammered at him about the unfortunate past. Laughing Boy saw an empty, drifting future, always with this old man. He saw himself a long time from now, and the dead boy who had ridden down to Tsé Lani crying across a gap full of darkness to the empty husk of a man who had destroyed him. He tried to call on the gods, but there came only Hunger People, Old Age, and Cold Woman. Yellow Singer and his wife were there, looking sorry for him in that unpleasant, understanding way, like the day he was married. They all looked at him that way. He saw the stricken face of Jesting Squaw’s Son, and thought, You too have received the wound, but you were lucky, the knife was pulled out as soon as it was thrust into you.

===Chinese===
入夜总算是个变化。柴有的是，他把火架得高高的，又费了些事把自己安顿舒坦。这是第四夜，他多少已经神志昏乱了。老头早已不再是一个化身，成了实体；他钻进同一条毯子底下，捶啊，捶啊，拿那不幸的过去一个劲儿捶他。笑娃望见一个空落落、飘荡荡的将来，总有这老头在一旁。他望见很久以后的自己，那个骑马下特塞拉尼去的、死了的少年，隔着一道盛满黑暗的鸿沟，向毁掉他的那具男人的空壳呼喊。他想呼唤众神，来的却只有饥饿之民、老年女人和冷女人。黄颂唱师和他的妻子也在，用那种叫人不痛快、却什么都看懂了的神气为他难过，就像他成婚那天一样。他们全都那样望着他。他看见打趣婆娘之子那张挨了重击的脸，心想：你也受了这一伤，可你运气好，刀才扎进去就拔出来了。

===Original===
The old man was pulling at his bow-guard. There was something around his right wrist, and that seemed to be being pulled too. The old man said,

===Chinese===
老头在扯他的弓护腕。他右腕上似乎也有个什么，正一道儿被扯着。老头说，

===Original===
“Where did you get that bow-guard?”

===Chinese===
“你这弓护腕是哪儿来的？”

===Original===
“I made it.”

===Chinese===
“我自己打的。”

===Original===
“I’ll give you six dollars for it.”

===Chinese===
“这只弓护腕我出六块。”

===Original===
“I don’t want to sell it.”

===Chinese===
“我不卖。”

===Original===
He did not really speak, but the words were saying themselves for him in answer to the old man, from a great distance, all the way from that hogan by Tsé Lani.

===Chinese===
他并没有真开口，可那些话在替他回答老头，从很远的地方来，一路从特塞拉尼旁边那座霍根屋那儿来。

===Original===
The old man went on, “That turquoise is no good, and the work is not very good.”

===Chinese===
老头接着说：“那绿松石不怎么样，做工也不大好。”

===Original===
The work was good. He touched the silver with his right hand, to show the four-points-with-three-points design. But it was not that design, it was not the bow-guard he made at T’o Tlakai, it was that one he made at their hogan, the one with stars-following.

===Chinese===
做工是好的。他用右手抚那银件，要指给人看四点带三点的那个花样。可这不是那个花样；这不是他在特奥·特莱凯打的弓护腕，是他在他们的霍根屋打的那个，群星相随的那个。

===Original===
He said out loud, “This is the one I made when she was weaving. I will not sell it.”

===Chinese===
他出声说：“这是她织布那时候我打的那个。我不卖。”

===Original===
He felt the thing on his right wrist; it was the thin, gold circlet. He saw her hand and arm under the blankets, he saw the tomb in the dusk, and her face as he bent over it, so still. His in-turned torment was obliterated by the memory of that exalted agony. He remembered her last kiss, and her voice, and the mound of her blankets and jewels above her. His arms clutched about his knees, his left hand closed around the foreign bracelet, and he began to weep, tears pouring plenteously. As though they were rain on the desert, a coolness spread through him, a sense of majestic beauty.

===Chinese===
他觉出右腕上那个东西；是那只细细的金镯。他看见毯子底下她的手和手臂，他看见暮色里的墓，看见自己俯身下去时她的脸，那么安静。他那向里啃啮的痛楚，被对那一场崇高苦楚的回忆抹掉了。他记起她最后的一吻，她的声音，她身上堆起的毯子和首饰拢成的小丘。他双臂箍住膝盖，左手攥紧那只异邦的手镯，哭了起来，泪水滂沱。仿佛沙漠上落了一场雨，一阵清凉漫过他全身，一种庄严的美。

===Original===
He threw his arms wide, looked up, and began to pray,

===Chinese===
他张开双臂，仰起脸，开始祷告，

===Original===
“House made of dawn light,
						

						House made of evening light,
						

						House made of dark cloud,
						

						House made of he-rain﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
“黎明之光筑成的屋宇，
						

						暮色之光筑成的屋宇，
						

						暗云筑成的屋宇，
						

						雄雨筑成的屋宇……”

===Original===
The old man was gone, and the Hunger People and all the rest. He stood up, stepping back from the fire, stretched out his hands, and his prayer rose in powerful song,

===Chinese===
老头不见了，饥饿之民和其余一切也都不见了。他站起身，从火边退开，伸出双手，他的祷告化作雄壮的歌升起来，

===Original===
“*Kat Yeinaezgani tla disitsaya﻿ ﻿…*
						

						Now, Slayer of Enemy Gods, alone I see him coming,
						

						Down from the skies, alone I see him coming.
						

						His voice sounds all about,
						

						His voice sounds, divine.
						

						
							*Lé-é!*
						
						

						Now, Child of the Waters, alone I see him coming﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
“Kat Yeinaezgani tla disitsaya……
						

						如今，诛敌神者啊，我独自见祂前来，
						

						从诸天而降，我独自见祂前来。
						

						祂的声音四方鸣响，
						

						祂的声音鸣响，神圣。
						

						
							莱-埃！
						
						

						如今，水之子啊，我独自见祂前来……”

===Original===
He finished, and stood with high head and hands still held forth. A log on the fire fell in, the flames leapt up, slightly dazzling his sight. When it had cleared, along the level of his fingertips he saw a Une dividing a deeper from a lesser blackness. The line spread right and left, and now along its upper edge a white glow appeared and widened; the sky above was changing from black to blue, the cliffs of the far side of the main canyon were silhouetted against the corning day.

===Chinese===
他唱完了，仍旧昂着头、双手前伸地站着。火里一根柴塌了进去，火焰蹿起，微微耀了他的眼。等火光定住，顺着指尖的水平线，他看见一道线，分开一深一浅的两片黑。那线向左右延展，此刻沿着它的上缘透出一道白光，渐渐宽阔；头顶的天正由黑转蓝，主峡谷对岸的峭壁，衬着将临的白昼，显出剪影。

===Original===
“*Hozoji, hozoji, hozoji, hozoji!*

===Chinese===
“霍卓吉，霍卓吉，霍卓吉，霍卓吉！

===Original===
“Dawn Boy, Little Chief,
						

						Let all be beautiful before me as I wander,
						

						All beautiful behind me as I wander,
						

						All beautiful above me as I wander,
						

						All beautiful below me as I wander.
						

						Let my eyes see only beauty
						

						This day as I wander.
						

						In beauty,
						

						In beauty,
						

						In beauty,
						

						In beauty!”

===Chinese===
“黎明少年，小首领，
						

						愿我一路浪游，眼前皆在美中，
						

						身后皆在美中，当我一路浪游，
						

						头上皆在美中，当我一路浪游，
						

						脚下皆在美中，当我一路浪游。
						

						愿我的双眼只看见美，
						

						今日，当我一路浪游。
						

						在美之中，
						

						在美之中，
						

						在美之中，
						

						在美之中！”

===Original===
He let his arms fall. “Thanks!”

===Chinese===
他垂下手臂。“多谢！”

===Original===
He rearranged the fire to make a lasting, small flame, enough to melt snow in the coffeepot for drinking and refreshing his hands and face. He looked over towards the niche, a shadowy place; the rocks on each side of it were touched irregularly with sunlight.

===Chinese===
他把火拢成一个经久的小火苗，够在咖啡壶里化雪，好喝水，好抹手脸。他朝那龛室望过去，一处幽暗的所在；它两侧的岩石上，已经斑斑驳驳映上了日光。

===Original===
I nearly lost you, little sister, but now I have you for always.

===Chinese===
我差一点把你丢了，小妹妹，如今我可永远有你了。

===Original===
He began praying again, quietly and earnestly, not in set terms, but according to his need. He had come out of that closet in himself now, and things had fitted back into place. He was grave, and there would be many times when he would go by himself to feel a beloved pain, but regret for the knowledge of happiness that had made that pain possible was ended. He had a clear conscience to pray.

===Chinese===
他又祷告起来，安静而恳切，不用成套的词句，只照着他的需要。此刻他已经从自己心里那间小屋里走了出来，万事各归其位。他神情凝重，往后还会有许多回，他要独自去体味那可爱的痛；但对认识了那份幸福的悔恨——正是那份幸福使这痛成为可能——已经终结了。他有一颗清净的良心可以祷告。

===Original===
He built his sweat-lodge, and, since it was hard to get mud out of the frozen ground, covered it with blankets. In the mid-afternoon he put in the hot rocks, stripped, and entered. He had made it good and hot; he sat in there chanting as long as he could stand it, then he burst out, rolled in the snow, and dressed hastily. He felt infinitely better. He looked at the sun, low in the west; the fourth day was ended.

===Chinese===
他搭起汗浴的小屋，冻土里抠不出泥来，便拿毯子苫顶。午后过半，他把烧烫的石头放进去，脱光了钻进去。他把里面弄得又足又热；他在里头唱着颂歌，熬到熬不住了，才猛地钻出来，扑进雪里打滚，慌忙穿上衣裳。他觉得说不出的轻快。他看看太阳，已经低低挂在西边；第四天完了。

===Original===
He felt clearheaded, peaceful, washed, and very hungry as he tracked his pony. The animal greeted him with a whinny; its legs were stiff from the hobbles, and it had fallen off from lack of feed. He rode back to the camp, and tethered it while he broke his fast with coffee. Then he saddled and mounted. Before he rode on he turned towards the niche and sat still until his mount jerked at the reins.

===Chinese===
他寻着小马的蹄印走去，头脑清明，心里平安，一身像洗过一样，只是饿得很。那牲口一声嘶鸣迎接他；四条腿叫绊索绊得发僵，缺草料也掉了膘。他骑回营地，把它拴住，喝着咖啡开了斋。然后备鞍上马。上路之前，他朝那龛室转过身，静静坐着，直到胯下的马拽动了缰绳。

===Original===
But we shall never be far from each other, he thought, always alone but never lonely. As he rode away he repeated, “In beauty it is finished, in beauty it is finished, in beauty it is finished. Thanks.”

===Chinese===
可我们永远不会离得太远，他想，永远独自一人，却永远不孤单。骑马走远时，他一遍遍念着：“在美之中告成，在美之中告成，在美之中告成。多谢。”

### II / II

===Original===
It was nearly dark when he climbed out of the head of the canyon onto the top of So Selah Mesa. He urged his pony along the level going, anxious to get to the settlement in Jaabani Valley as soon as possible. There was only a day-old moon, and a cold wind blew across the open. It was a talking wind, a voice of sorrow in the growing darkness, and Laughing Boy had been too long alone. He wanted a respite from self-communion; he wanted company and things happening, the old life, support. He was homesick for old, familiar things.

===Chinese===
他爬出峡谷源头、登上索塞拉台地（So Selah Mesa）顶上时，天已擦黑。他催着小马沿平路走，巴望尽早赶到贾巴尼谷地（Jaabani Valley）里的聚落。天上只有一弯才露头一天的月牙，寒风吹过旷野。那是一阵会说话的风，在渐浓的夜色里发出哀愁的声音，而笑娃独自待得也太久了。他想从自我交谈里歇一歇；他要人做伴，要有事情发生，要旧日的生活，要个倚傍。他想那些老旧而熟悉的东西了。

===Original===
This cold plateau was nowhere, a waste land separating the human world from the enchanted. It was always dark here, and a cold wind blew, and there was always a small moon setting.

===Chinese===
这片寒冷的台地哪儿也不属于，是把人的世界同精魅世界隔开的一片荒地。这里永远是黑的，永远刮着冷风，永远有一弯小月西沉。

===Original===
I shall be whimpering in a moment, he thought. I am unworthy of myself and of her. Do I forget everything? It’s because I am cold and hungry. I might sing. He began,

===Chinese===
再过一会儿我就要呜咽了，他想。我配不上自己，也配不上她。我什么都忘了吗？这是因为我又冷又饿。不如唱唱歌。他开口唱，

===Original===
“I rode down from high hills﻿ ﻿…”

===Chinese===
“我骑着马，从高高的山岗上下来……”

===Original===
but the high-pitched love-tune affronted the night. He stopped, with a catch in his throat.

===Chinese===
可那高亢的情歌调子冒犯了夜。他停住了，喉咙里一哽。

===Original===
I will not cry. This is not a thing to cry over; it is a beautiful thing, to be thought of gravely. I devote my life to it, not just cry. He began to chant, in a deep voice:

===Chinese===
我不哭。这不是拿来哭的事；这是一件美的事，要郑重地去想。我把一生献给它，不只是哭一场。他用低沉的声音唱起来，

===Original===
“With a place of hunger in me I wander,
						

						Food will not fill it,
						

						*Aya-ah*, beautiful.
						

						With an empty place in me I wander,
						

						Nothing will fill it,
						

						*Aya-ah*, beautiful.
						

						With a place of sorrow in me I wander,
						

						Time will not end it,
						

						*Aya-ah*, beautiful.
						

						With a place of loneliness in me I wander,
						

						No one will fill it,
						

						*Aya-ah*, beautiful.
						

						Forever alone, forever in sorrow I wander,
						

						Forever empty, forever hungry I wander,
						

						With the sorrow of great beauty I wander,
						

						With the emptiness of great beauty I wander,
						

						Never alone, never weeping, never empty,
						

						Now on the old age trail, now on the path of beauty I wander,
						

						*Ahalani*, beautiful!”

===Chinese===
“我揣着一块饥饿的地方浪游，
						

						饮食填不满它，
						

						阿亚-阿，美啊。
						

						我揣着一块空空的地方浪游，
						

						什么也填不满它，
						

						阿亚-阿，美啊。
						

						我揣着一块悲伤的地方浪游，
						

						岁月了结不了它，
						

						阿亚-阿，美啊。
						

						我揣着一块孤独的地方浪游，
						

						没有人填得满它，
						

						阿亚-阿，美啊。
						

						永远孤独，永远怀着悲伤我浪游，
						

						永远空空，永远饥饿我浪游，
						

						怀着大美的悲伤我浪游，
						

						怀着大美的空空我浪游，
						

						从不孤独，从不哭泣，从不空空，
						

						如今走在暮年的古道上，如今走在美之路上我浪游，
						

						阿哈拉尼，美啊！”

===Original===
It was a prayer. He ended with four solemn *hozoji*s that seemed to travel out from him and fill the darkness. That is a good song, he thought. I shall sing that often, at evening, when I am alone. But I wish we would get to where there are people.

===Chinese===
这是一篇祷文。末了他念了四声庄重的霍卓吉，仿佛从他身上走出去，把黑暗填满了。这是一支好歌，他想。往后傍晚一个人时，我要常常唱它。可我巴望咱们早些到了有人烟的地方。

===Original===
It had been night for more than an hour when he came to the edge of the mesa, looking down into Jaabani. He saw the little pinpricks of fires, very distant. Then, as he watched, near them another began, and grew, until a tall flame rose, throwing light all about it. He heard a drum beat and faint voices singing, and saw around the blaze a wide circle of branches, and people moving. They were beginning the last night of a Mountain Chant, the ritual within the Dark Circle of Branches.

===Chinese===
入夜已经一个多钟头，他到了台地边上，俯望贾巴尼。他望见针尖似的点点火光，非常遥远。随后，正看着，近处又亮起一点，大起来，直到一道高高的火焰立起，四下里照得通明。他听见鼓点和隐隐的歌声，望见火堆四周围着一圈宽宽的枝栏，人影走动。他们在起山地颂仪的最后一夜，暗枝圈之内的仪轨。

===Original===
“Come on, my pet!” His horse began slowly descending the trail in the starlight. As they went down, he sang his song again. This was very good. When Reared in a Mountain returned to his people from the homes of the gods, he taught them these prayers and songs, and they held the Mountain Chant for him, because he was unhappy among them. Even so was he. He was rejoining his people in the presence of the gods. Ah, if she could have been here!

===Chinese===
“走吧，我的小宝贝！”他的马在星光里顺着小径慢慢往下走。一路下去，他又唱起自己那支歌。这再好也没有了。当年山中养大从诸神的居所回到自己人中间，把这些祷词和歌教给了他们，他们为他举行山地颂仪，因为他在他们中间不快活。他如今也正是这样。他正在诸神的面前，归回到自己人中间去。啊，她要是能在这里该多好！

===Original===
The singing grew louder, the triumphant songs. Now he could make out the words. They were completing the magic of the tufted wands. He drew rein a few yards from one of the campfires, tasting again the sense of his isolation. Then he dismounted.

===Chinese===
歌声越来越响，那些得胜的歌。如今他听得出字句了。他们在完成束羽仪杖的法力。离一处营火还有几步远，他勒住缰，又尝了一遍孤独的滋味。然后下了马。

===Original===
Only a few people remained outside the circle, but he found a hospitable pot of broth, some chunks of mutton still in it, bread to dip, and coffee.

===Chinese===
圈子外头只剩不多几个人了，他却寻着一锅待客的肉汤，里头还有几块羊肉，有蘸汤的面包，还有咖啡。

===Original===
“Where do you come from, Grandfather?” the woman asked.

===Chinese===
“你从哪儿来，老爷爷？”那妇人问。

===Original===
“From Chiziai.”

===Chinese===
“从奇扎伊来。”

===Original===
“Where are you going?”

===Chinese===
“往哪儿去？”

===Original===
“To this dance.”

===Chinese===
“去这场舞。”

===Original===
“That is a good saddle-blanket; who made it?”

===Chinese===
“这条鞍毯不错；谁织的？”

===Original===
“My wife, she weaves well.”

===Chinese===
“我妻子，她织得好。”

===Original===
“Is she here?”

===Chinese===
“她来了吗？”

===Original===
“No, she stayed behind.”

===Chinese===
“没有，她留在后头了。”

### III / III

===Original===
The brush fence enclosed an oval some forty feet across, in the centre of which blazed the bonfire, higher than a tall man. All around the edge sat people, several hundreds of them; they were happy, their faces were grave but joyful. At one end were the singers.

===Chinese===
树枝围栏圈出一个椭圆，横里约莫四十英尺，当中一堆营火熊熊，比高个子人还高。四周边沿坐满了人，有好几百；他们快活，一张张脸庄重而喜悦。一头坐着颂唱师们。

===Original===
Now Red God came into the open space, leading a file of dancers, the Grandfather of the Gods, who guided Reared in a Mountain through the homes of the Divine Ones, who saved him from the Utes. With his plumed sticks and his sacred insignia, Red God led the dance before them.

===Chinese===
此刻红神走进场中，领着一列舞者——诸神之祖，是祂领着山中养大走遍神圣之灵的居所，是祂从尤特人手里救了他。红神执着插羽的仪杖和神圣的徽记，在他们前头领舞。

===Original===
Talking God and South God and Young Goddess came before them with dancers, and all the place was full of sacred songs. They were leading good dances, with good music.

===Chinese===
说话神和南神（South God）、年轻女神带着舞者来到他们跟前，整个场子满是神圣的歌。他们领的是好舞，配的是好乐。

===Original===
The magicians came in and planted the yucca root. They sang and danced about it; the yucca grew, it became tall, it flowered. In midwinter the enchanted yucca bloomed before them. These were the magics that the people of distant tribes brought to the first Mountain Chant. Now the magicians placed the board and the disk of the sun on the ground, the people all shouted, “Stand! Stand!” The board stood up on end, the sun rose to the top and set slowly; four times the sun rose and set by magic; then the board lay flat again.

===Chinese===
巫师们进来，栽下丝兰根。他们围着它又唱又跳；丝兰长起来，长高了，开花了。隆冬里，着了法的丝兰在他们眼前绽放。这些都是远方部族的人们带去头一场山地颂仪的法术。此刻巫师们把木板和太阳的圆盘放在地上，众人齐声喊：“立起来！立起来！”木板直立起来，太阳升到顶上，缓缓落下；太阳凭法术升落了四回；然后木板又平平躺了下去。

===Original===
A man, stripped to his breechclout, danced before a basket. Out of the basket an eagle feather rose; it danced up into the air, to the height of the man, and there it moved backward and forward in time with him.

===Chinese===
一个男人，身上只系一条遮裆布，在一只篮子前跳舞。篮子里升起一根鹰羽；它舞到空中，升到那人齐头的高处，就在那里应着他的节拍前前后后地动。

===Original===
Jesters came in, dressed as Americans and Mexicans, and made the people laugh. The spirits of the ancestral animals, hovering over the brush circle, were made happy. Laughing Boy, sitting among friendly strangers, smiled at them and said,

===Chinese===
丑角们进来了，装扮成美国人和墨西哥人，逗得众人直笑。盘旋在枝圈上空的先祖兽灵，也被逗乐了。笑娃坐在友善的生人中间，朝他们微笑着说，

===Original===
“It is good!”

===Chinese===
“真好！”

===Original===
The great central fire and the small fires that people made for themselves, kept the place warm. He had eaten, he was comfortable. He did not realize how sleepy he was. At times the details of what he was watching became blurred and he drowsed deliciously; but he was permeated with the general feeling of the prayer, and he looked upon it as he had when an uninitiated child.

===Chinese===
当中那堆大火，加上众人各自生的小火，把这地方烘得暖暖的。他吃过了，浑身舒坦。他不觉得自己有多困。有时眼前的细节模糊起来，他甜甜地打着盹；可祷告的总体感受浸透了他，他像未入仪礼的孩提那时一样，看着眼前这一切。

===Original===
Young men painted all white with black forearms, foxskins hanging from their waists, came in with the magic arrows adorned with breath-feathers. This was the holiest part; this was the charm that the Tall Gods taught to Reared in a Mountain in their divine home. The young men danced, they swallowed the arrows and shouted in triumph; these were the very acts of the gods.

===Chinese===
小伙子们周身涂白、小臂涂黑，腰间垂着狐皮，捧着缀有气息之羽的法箭进来。这是至圣的部分；这是高神们（Tall Gods）在他们的神圣居所里传授给山中养大的那道法咒。小伙子们跳舞，把箭吞下去，得胜地呼喊；这些正是诸神本身的作为。

===Original===
Laughing Boy felt a deep sense of peace, and rejoicing over ugliness defeated. The gods danced before him, he felt the influence of their divinity. The naked youths danced with torches, they bathed in flame, they leapt through and through the fire. He had been bathed in flame, he had been through a fire.

===Chinese===
笑娃觉出一种深深的平安，一种丑恶既败的欢欣。众神在他面前起舞，他感到他们神性的力量。赤裸的少年们举着火把跳舞，他们在火焰里沐浴，一遍又一遍地窜过火中。他也在火焰里沐浴过，他也熬穿过一场火。

===Original===
The past and the present came together, he was one with himself. The good and true things he had thought entered into his being and were part of the whole continuity of his life.

===Chinese===
过去与现在合到了一处，他与他自身合成了一个人。他想到过的那些好的、真的东西，进入了他的生命，成为他一生整个绵延的一部分。

===Original===
It was beginning to dawn, the last prayer came to a close. Quietly, the people left the enclosure. He went to where his horse was tethered and rolled up in his blanket. Sleepily there, he kissed the gold bracelet, saying,

===Chinese===
天开始亮了，最后一篇祷告收了尾。人们静静散出围场。他走到拴马的地方，裹进毯子里。半睡半醒间，他吻了吻那只金镯，说道，

===Original===
“Never alone, never lamenting, never empty. *Ahalani*, beautiful!”

===Chinese===
“从不孤独，从不悲叹，从不空空。阿哈拉尼，美啊！”

## Dedication / 献词

===Original===
**Dedicated

			to**

			The only beautiful squaw

			I have ever seen in all my life,

			whose name I have

			forgotten

===Chinese===
**献给**

我一生中所见过的
唯一美丽的婆娘，
她的名字
我已经忘了

## Introductory Note / 序言

===Original===
This book is a work of fiction. I have tried to be as true as I knew how to the general spirit of Navajo things, to customs and character, but all personages and incidents in the story are fictitious, as well as places. I have used some real place-names applied to imaginary places, or else have shifted them a hundred miles or so. There are about thirty thousand Navajos, most of whom have at least two descriptive names; it would be impossible, then, to invent names for all my characters and not hit upon some real ones. So I have frankly borrowed from names I have heard, or those listed by scientists. But neither whites nor Indians are real.

===Chinese===
本书是一部虚构之作。就我所知所能，我力求忠于纳瓦霍风物的总体精神，忠于他们的习俗与民性；但故事中的一切人物与事件均属虚构，地点亦然。我用了些真实的地名，安在虚构的地方，或是把它们挪移了一百英里上下。纳瓦霍人约有三万，多半每人至少有两个描述性的名字；既然如此，要给书中所有人物编造姓名而竟不撞上几个真名，那是不可能的事。于是我索性坦然借用自己听来的名字，或是学者们名录里载着的名字。但无论白人还是印第安人，都并非实有其人。

===Original===
I have been as accurate as possible about ceremonies, rites, and customs. If occasionally I have taken liberties, I plead a writer’s privilege. Any innovations I may have made are nonetheless true to the general pattern of Navajo ideas.

===Chinese===
关于各种仪式、祭典与习俗，我已力求准确。倘若偶有随意损益之处，我援引作家的特权为自己开脱。不过，我纵然有所自创，也仍不失纳瓦霍人观念的总体格局。

===Original===
This story is meant neither to instruct nor to prove a point, but to amuse. It is not propaganda, nor an indictment of anything. The hostility with which certain of the characters in it view Americans and the American system is theirs, arising from the plot, and not the author’s. The picture is frankly one-sided. It is also entirely possible.

===Chinese===
这个故事既无意教训人，也无意证明什么道理，只为博人一乐。它不是宣传品，也不是对任何事物的控诉书。书中某些人物之敌视美国人和美国制度，那是他们自己的敌意，由情节而生，并非作者之见。这幅图景分明是片面的。而它也完全可能是真的。

===Original===
O. La F.

New Orleans, 1920

===Chinese===
O. La F.

新奥尔良，1920年
